[义] A continuous low sound made by bees, flies, or machines; to make this sound. 蜜蜂、蝇或机器发出的持续低沉声（bees buzz 蜜蜂嗡嗡，buzz around 忙碌穿梭〔喻〕）。可数/动词。
[例] ① Bees buzzed around the lavender bushes. 蜜蜂在薰衣草丛间嗡嗡飞。 ② Flies buzzed against the window screen. 蝇撞纱窗嗡嗡作响。
[搭] bees buzz 蜜蜂嗡嗡, buzz around 忙碌穿梭, buzz off 走开（口语）, create a buzz 引起轰动（喻）
[联] bee 蜜蜂, fly 苍蝇, insect 昆虫, sound 声音, hive 蜂巢, mosquito 蚊子
[记] 可数/动词。buzz 亦可指兴奋感或电话「嗡嗡振」，比喻义

**crawl**
英/美 /krɔːl/
v. 爬行；缓慢移动
n. 爬行；匍匐泳
[义] To move forward on hands and knees or with the body close to the ground. 手足着地或贴地向前移动（babies crawl 婴儿爬行，snake crawl 蛇行，ants crawl 蚁爬）。动词/可数名词。
[例] ① The baby crawled across the rug toward the toy. 婴儿爬过地毯够玩具。 ② Insects crawled over the fallen fruit. 昆虫爬满落地的水果。
[搭] babies crawl 婴儿爬行, crawl on hands and knees 手脚并用爬, crawl space 狭小空隙, snake crawl 蛇行
[联] snake 蛇, insect 昆虫, slow 缓慢的, ground 地面, baby 婴儿, creep 爬行
[记] 动词/名词。crawl 亦可指交通「龟速」或自由泳 crawl stroke

**enclosure**
英/美 /ɪnˈkləʊʒə(r)/
n. 圈地；圈舍
[义] An area surrounded by a fence or wall for keeping animals in. 用栅栏或墙围起、关养动物的区域（animal enclosure 动物圈舍，safari enclosure 游猎围场）。可数。
[例] ① Visitors watched lions from outside the enclosure. 游客在圈舍外观看狮子。 ② The zoo enlarged the elephant enclosure last year. 动物园去年扩建了象圈。
[搭] animal enclosure 动物圈舍, safari enclosure 游猎围场, fenced enclosure 围栏圈地, zoo enclosure 动物园圈舍
[联] zoo 动物园, cage 笼子, fence 栅栏, habitat 栖息地, captive 圈养的, wildlife 野生动物
[记] 可数：an enclosure。enclosure 亦可指信件「附件」，不同义

**gill**
英/美 /ɡɪl/
n. 鳃
[义] The organ that fish and some other water animals use to breathe. 鱼及其他水生动物用以呼吸的器官（fish gills 鱼鳃，gill slits 鳃裂）。可数，常用复数 gills。
[例] ① Fish extract oxygen from water through their gills. 鱼经鳃从水中吸取氧气。 ② The shark's gills moved rhythmically as it swam. 鲨游动时鳃节律张合。
[搭] fish gills 鱼鳃, gill slits 鳃裂, behind the gills 鳃后
[联] fish 鱼, breathe 呼吸, water 水, shark 鲨鱼, oxygen 氧气, fin 鳍
[记] 可数，常用复数 gills。to the gills 指「满到嗓子眼」（吃撑），习语

**flipper**
英/美 /ˈflɪpə(r)/
n. 鳍肢；脚蹼
[义] A flat limb used for swimming by seals, turtles, and whales. 海豹、龟、鲸等用于游泳的扁平肢（front flipper 前鳍肢，dolphin flipper 海豚鳍肢）。可数。
[例] ① The seal waved its flippers on the rock. 海豹在礁上摆动鳍肢。 ② Sea turtles use their flippers to steer through the water. 海龟用鳍肢在水中转向。
[搭] front flipper 前鳍肢, rear flipper 后鳍肢, dolphin flipper 海豚鳍肢, swim with flippers 用脚蹼游
[联] seal 海豹, dolphin 海豚, turtle 龟, swim 游泳, fin 鳍, ocean 海洋
[记] 可数：a flipper。flipper 亦指潜水用脚蹼或弹球机拨杆，不同义

**sparrow**
英/美 /ˈspærəʊ/
n. 麻雀
[义] A small brown and grey bird common in towns and countryside. 城镇与乡间常见的小型褐灰色鸟（house sparrow 家麻雀，sparrow hawk 雀鹰）。可数。
[例] ① Sparrows chirped on the fence in the morning. 早晨麻雀在栅栏上啁啾。 ② House sparrows nested under the roof tiles. 家麻雀在瓦下筑巢。
[搭] house sparrow 家麻雀, sparrow hawk 雀鹰, sparrow nest 麻雀巢, flock of sparrows 一群麻雀
[联] bird 鸟, chirp 啁啾, small 小的, nest 巢, urban 城市的, flock 鸟群
[记] 可数：a sparrow。a sparrow in the hand 类似「一鸟在手」（较少用）

**moth**
英/美 /mɒθ/
n. 蛾
[义] An insect like a butterfly that flies at night and is often attracted to light. 似蝶、夜飞、常趋光的昆虫（clothes moth 衣蛾，moth to a flame 扑火〔喻〕）。可数。
[例] ① A moth fluttered around the porch light. 一只蛾在门廊灯旁扑闪。 ② Clothes moths can damage wool stored in closets. 衣蛾可蛀坏衣橱里的羊毛织物。
[搭] clothes moth 衣蛾, moth to a flame 扑火, night moth 夜蛾, moth wings 蛾翅
[联] butterfly 蝴蝶, insect 昆虫, night 夜晚, light 灯光, wing 翅膀, caterpillar 毛虫
[记] 可数：a moth。moth 与 butterfly：蛾多夜飞、触角羽状；蝶多昼飞

**raven**
英/美 /ˈreɪvn/
n. 渡鸦
[义] A large black bird related to the crow, often associated with mystery in literature. 与乌鸦同科的大型黑鸟，文学中常与神秘联想（raven black 乌黑的，Edgar Allan Poe 文化联想）。可数。
[例] ① A raven croaked from the dead tree. 渡鸦在枯树上沙哑啼叫。 ② Ravens are among the most intelligent birds. 渡鸦是最聪慧的鸟类之一。
[搭] raven black 乌黑的, raven croak 渡鸦沙哑叫声, flock of ravens 一群渡鸦
[联] crow 乌鸦, bird 鸟, black 黑色的, clever 聪明的, wing 翅膀, mystery 神秘
[记] 可数：a raven。raven 比 crow 更大，文化意象更浓（《乌鸦》诗）

**peacock**
英/美 /ˈpiːkɒk/
n. 孔雀（雄）
[义] A male bird with long colourful tail feathers that it can spread out. 尾羽修长艳丽、可展开的雄性鸟（peacock feather 孔雀羽，proud as a peacock 骄傲如孔雀〔喻〕）。可数。雌性 peahen。
[例] ① The peacock fanned its tail in the garden. 孔雀在花园里开屏。 ② Peacocks roamed freely on the palace grounds. 孔雀在宫殿院落里自由漫步。
[搭] peacock feather 孔雀羽, proud as a peacock 骄傲如孔雀, peacock display 孔雀开屏, peahen 雌孔雀
[联] bird 鸟, feather 羽毛, tail 尾巴, colorful 艳丽的, display 展示, proud 骄傲的
[记] 可数：a peacock。雌孔雀 peahen；统称 peafowl

**alligator**
英/美 /ˈælɪɡeɪtə(r)/
n. 短吻鳄
[义] A large reptile with a long body, short legs, and a broad snout, found mainly in the Americas. 体长、腿短、吻部宽的大型爬行动物，主要见于美洲（American alligator 美洲短吻鳄）。可数。
[例] ① Alligators basked on the muddy bank of the swamp. 短吻鳄在沼泽泥岸晒太阳。 ② An alligator slid silently into the water. 一条短吻鳄悄然滑入水中。
[搭] American alligator 美洲短吻鳄, alligator skin 鳄鱼皮, alligator swamp 鳄鱼沼泽, baby alligator 幼鳄
[联] crocodile 鳄鱼, reptile 爬行动物, swamp 沼泽, predator 捕食者, teeth 牙齿, dangerous 危险的
[记] 可数：an alligator。alligator 吻宽且圆；crocodile 吻较尖，下齿常外露

**guinea pig**
英/美 /ˈɡɪni pɪɡ/
n. 豚鼠；天竺鼠
[义] A small rodent with no tail, often kept as a pet. 无尾、常作宠物的小型啮齿动物（pet guinea pig 宠物豚鼠，guinea pig for experiments 实验对象〔喻〕）。可数。
[例] ① The guinea pig squeaked when it heard the fridge open. 豚鼠听见冰箱开门就吱吱叫。 ② Guinea pigs are gentle pets for children. 豚鼠是适合儿童的温顺宠物。
[搭] pet guinea pig 宠物豚鼠, guinea pig cage 豚鼠笼, guinea pig for experiments 实验对象
[联] rodent 啮齿动物, hamster 仓鼠, pet 宠物, cage 笼子, squeak 吱吱叫, small 小的
[记] 可数：a guinea pig。并非猪（pig），亦非来自几内亚。喻「试验品」极常见

**pony**
英/美 /ˈpəʊni/
n. 矮种马；小马
[义] A small horse, often used for children to ride. 体型较小的马，常供儿童骑乘（Shetland pony 设得兰矮马，pony ride 骑小马）。可数。
[例] ① Children lined up for pony rides at the fair. 孩子们在集市排队骑小马。 ② The pony grazed quietly in the paddock. 矮种马在围场里安静吃草。
[搭] Shetland pony 设得兰矮马, pony ride 骑小马, pony tail 马尾辫（喻）, small horse 小马
[联] horse 马, ride 骑乘, mane 鬃毛, hoof 蹄, farm 农场, child 儿童
[记] 可数：a pony。ponytail 指「马尾辫」，与马相关

**koala**
英/美 /kəʊˈɑːlə/
n. 考拉
[义] An Australian tree-dwelling marsupial that eats eucalyptus leaves. 澳大利亚树栖有袋动物，以桉树叶为食（koala bear 考拉〔误称〕，koala habitat 考拉栖息地）。可数。
[例] ① A koala slept curled in the fork of a gum tree. 考拉蜷在桉树杈上睡觉。 ② Koalas spend most of the day resting. 考拉一天大部分时间都在休息。
[搭] koala bear 考拉（俗称）, koala habitat 考拉栖息地, eucalyptus leaves 桉树叶, marsupial 有袋动物
[联] kangaroo 袋鼠, Australia 澳大利亚, tree 树, mammal 哺乳动物, pouch 育儿袋, endangered 濒危的
[记] 可数：a koala。虽常被叫 koala bear，但不是熊

**tuna**
英/美 /ˈtjuːnə/
n. 金枪鱼
[义] A large fast-swimming sea fish eaten as food. 游速快的大型海鱼，可食用（bluefin tuna 蓝鳍金枪鱼，tuna can 金枪鱼罐头）。可数/不可数。
[例] ① Tuna migrate across entire oceans. 金枪鱼穿越整片大洋迁徙。 ② Fishermen tracked tuna schools on radar. 渔民用雷达追踪金枪鱼群。
[搭] bluefin tuna 蓝鳍金枪鱼, tuna can 金枪鱼罐头, tuna fishing 金枪鱼捕捞, tuna school 金枪鱼群
[联] fish 鱼, ocean 海洋, migrate 迁徙, shark 鲨鱼, seafood 海鲜（食材→4-01）, fast 快速的
[记] 可数/不可数。作鱼→本章；罐头/菜肴→4-01。单复数同形或 tunas

**ape**
英/美 /eɪp/
n. 类人猿
v. 模仿
[义] A primate without a tail, such as a gorilla, chimpanzee, or orangutan. 无尾的灵长类，如大猩猩、黑猩猩、猩猩（great ape 类人猿，ape behavior 类人猿行为）。可数/动词。动词 ape 指「模仿」。
[例] ① Apes use tools and solve problems in the wild. 类人猿在野外使用工具、解决问题。 ② Gorillas and chimpanzees are both apes, not monkeys. 大猩猩与黑猩猩都是类人猿，不是猴。
[搭] great ape 类人猿, ape behavior 类人猿行为, ape intelligence 类人猿智力
[联] monkey 猴子, gorilla 大猩猩, chimpanzee 黑猩猩, primate 灵长类, intelligent 聪慧的, tail 尾巴
[记] 可数/动词。ape 无尾；monkey 通常有尾。go ape 指「发疯」（俚语）

**primate**
英/美 /ˈpraɪmeɪt/
n. 灵长类动物
[义] A mammal belonging to the group that includes monkeys, apes, and humans. 包括猴、类人猿和人类的哺乳动物类群（primate species 灵长物种，primate research 灵长类研究）。可数。
[例] ① Humans are primates, though we often forget it. 人类是灵长类，尽管我们常忘记这点。 ② Primate habitats are shrinking in many regions. 许多地区灵长类栖息地正在缩小。
[搭] primate species 灵长物种, primate research 灵长类研究, primate family 灵长目
[联] monkey 猴子, ape 类人猿, gorilla 大猩猩, human 人类, mammal 哺乳动物, intelligent 聪慧的
[记] 可数：a primate。primary（主要的）同形异义，勿混

**swarm**
英/美 /swɔːm/
n. 蜂群；一大群
v. 蜂拥；群集
[义] A large group of insects flying together; to move in a large group. 一起飞行的大群昆虫；大群移动（a swarm of bees 一群蜜蜂，swarm with tourists 游人如织〔喻〕）。可数/动词。
[例] ① A swarm of bees passed over the garden. 一群蜜蜂飞过花园上空。 ② Ants swarmed out of the cracked pavement. 蚂蚁从开裂的路面蜂拥而出。
[搭] a swarm of bees 一群蜜蜂, swarm with 挤满, swarm of insects 虫群, bee swarm 蜂群
[联] bee 蜜蜂, flock 群, herd 兽群, insect 昆虫, fly 飞, crowd 人群
[记] 可数/动词。a swarm of + 昆虫/人。swarm 强调密集涌动

**burrow**
英/美 /ˈbʌrəʊ/
n. 洞穴；地道
v. 挖洞；钻入
[义] A hole in the ground dug by an animal for shelter; to dig such a hole. 动物挖作庇护的地下洞；挖洞（rabbit burrow 兔洞，burrow into 钻入〔喻〕）。可数/动词。
[例] ① The rabbit disappeared into its burrow. 兔子钻进洞里不见了。 ② Owls do not burrow, but rabbits and foxes do. 猫头鹰不挖洞，兔和狐会挖。
[搭] rabbit burrow 兔洞, fox burrow 狐狸洞, burrow into 钻入, underground burrow 地下洞穴
[联] rabbit 兔, fox 狐狸, den 兽穴, nest 巢, dig 挖, underground 地下的
[记] 可数/动词。burrow 与 den：burrow 多指挖出的地道；den 泛指兽穴

**hunt**
英/美 /hʊnt/
v. 狩猎；搜寻
n. 狩猎；搜寻
[义] To chase and kill wild animals for food or sport; to search for something. 为食物或运动追猎野生动物；搜寻（hunt for food 觅食，hunt deer 猎鹿，job hunt 求职〔喻〕）。动词/可数名词。
[形] hunted / hunted / hunting
[例] ① Lions hunt in groups at dawn and dusk. 狮在晨昏群猎。 ② Owls hunt mice in the fields at night. 猫头鹰夜间在田野捕鼠。
[搭] hunt for food 觅食, hunt deer 猎鹿, hunt in packs 结群狩猎, hunting season 狩猎季
[联] predator 捕食者, prey 猎物, pack 狼群, lion 狮子, wolf 狼, chase 追逐
[记] 动词/名词。hunt 亦可指「找工作、找东西」，比喻义。本章取动物狩猎义

**sing**
英/美 /sɪŋ/
v. 鸣叫；唱歌
[义] To make musical sounds with the voice; (of a bird) to make clear musical calls. 用嗓音发出乐声；（鸟）清脆鸣叫（birds sing 鸟鸣，sing in the morning 晨鸣）。动词。
[形] sang / sung / singing
[例] ① Birds sing loudest at dawn in spring. 春天黎明时鸟鸣最响。 ② A nightingale sang from the thicket. 夜莺在灌木丛中鸣唱。
[搭] birds sing 鸟鸣, sing in the morning 晨鸣, sing beautifully 鸣声悦耳
[联] bird 鸟, chirp 啁啾, song 歌声, morning 早晨, nest 巢, melody 旋律
[记] 动词。sing 用于鸟时中文常说「鸣」；人唱歌同词

**scavenger**
英/美 /ˈskævɪndʒə(r)/
n. 食腐动物；清道夫
[义] An animal that feeds on dead animals or decaying matter. 以死动物或腐质为食的动物（scavenger hunt 寻宝游戏〔不同义〕，nature's scavengers 大自然的清道夫）。可数。
[例] ① Vultures are scavengers that clean up carcasses. 秃鹫是清理尸体的食腐鸟。 ② Hyenas act as scavengers when prey is scarce. 猎物稀少时鬣狗充当食腐者。
[搭] nature's scavengers 大自然的清道夫, scavenger species 食腐物种, urban scavengers 城市清道夫（喻）
[联] hyena 鬣狗, vulture 秃鹫, prey 猎物, dead 死的, clean 清理, predator 捕食者
[记] 可数：a scavenger。scavenger hunt 指「寻宝游戏」，与食腐无关

**domesticate**
英/美 /dəˈmestɪkeɪt/
v. 驯化；驯养
[义] To tame an animal and breed it to live with humans. 驯服动物并培育其与人共处（domesticate dogs 驯化犬类，domesticated animals 被驯化动物）。动词。
[形] domesticated / domesticated / domesticating
[例] ① Humans domesticated wolves into dogs thousands of years ago. 数千年前人类将狼驯化为狗。 ② Cattle were domesticated for milk and meat. 牛被驯化以产奶食肉。
[源] 源自拉丁语 *domesticus*（家的），与 domestic 同族。
[族] domestic 家养的, domesticated 被驯化的, domestication 驯化
[搭] domesticate animals 驯化动物, domesticated species 驯化物种, domestication process 驯化过程
[联] tame 驯服的, wild 野生的, dog 狗, cattle 牛, breed 繁殖, pet 宠物
[记] 动词。domesticated 形容词：domesticated animals 家养动物

**nocturnal**
英/美 /nɒkˈtɜːnl/
adj. 夜行的；夜间的
[义] Active at night rather than during the day. 夜间而非白天活动（nocturnal animals 夜行动物，nocturnal hunter 夜间猎手）。只作形容词。
[例] ① Owls are nocturnal and hunt after dark. 猫头鹰夜行，天黑后捕猎。 ② Many desert animals are nocturnal to avoid heat. 许多沙漠动物夜行以避酷暑。
[搭] nocturnal animals 夜行动物, nocturnal hunter 夜间猎手, nocturnal activity 夜间活动
[联] owl 猫头鹰, bat 蝙蝠, night 夜晚, diurnal 昼行的, hunt 狩猎, active 活动的
[记] 只作形容词。与 diurnal（昼行）相对。nocturnal 亦可指人「夜猫子」习性

**dinosaur**
英/美 /ˈdaɪnəsɔː(r)/
n. 恐龙
[义] A reptile that lived millions of years ago, often very large. 数百万年前生活、常体巨大的爬行动物（dinosaur fossil 恐龙化石，dinosaur extinction 恐龙灭绝）。可数。
[例] ① Children learn about dinosaurs in natural history museums. 孩子在自然史博物馆了解恐龙。 ② Dinosaur fossils were unearthed in the desert. 沙漠中出土了恐龙化石。
[搭] dinosaur fossil 恐龙化石, dinosaur extinction 恐龙灭绝, dinosaur skeleton 恐龙骨架, living dinosaur 活恐龙（喻守旧者）
[联] reptile 爬行动物, fossil 化石, extinct 灭绝的, museum 博物馆, prehistoric 史前的, giant 巨大的
[记] 可数：a dinosaur。口语 living dinosaur 指「老古董、守旧的人」

**ladybug**
英/美 /ˈleɪdibʌɡ/
n. 瓢虫
[义] A small round beetle, usually red with black spots. 通常红底黑斑的小型圆甲虫（英式 ladybird）。可数。
[例] ① A ladybug crawled across the child's palm. 瓢虫爬过孩子手心。 ② Ladybugs eat aphids that damage garden plants. 瓢虫吃危害园艺植物的蚜虫。
[搭] ladybug spots 瓢虫斑点, beneficial ladybug 益虫瓢虫, ladybird 瓢虫（英式）
[联] insect 昆虫, beetle 甲虫, garden 花园, beneficial 益虫, spot 斑点, pest 害虫
[记] 可数：a ladybug。美式 ladybug；英式 ladybird。常被视为益虫

**cricket**
英/美 /ˈkrɪkɪt/
n. 蟋蟀；板球
[义] A jumping insect that chirps, especially at night; also a ball game. 会跳、会唧唧叫的昆虫，尤夜鸣；亦指板球运动。可数/不可数。板球归体育章。
[例] ① Crickets chirped in the grass after sunset. 日落后草丛里蟋蟀唧唧叫。 ② A cricket leaped away when we approached. 我们走近时蟋蟀跳走了。
[搭] cricket chirp 蟋蟀唧唧, field cricket 田间蟋蟀, cricket song 虫鸣
[联] insect 昆虫, chirp 唧唧叫, night 夜晚, grass 草, jump 跳, sound 声音
[记] 可数。cricket 板球运动归 2-07 体育章；本章仅昆虫义

**firefly**
英/美 /ˈfaɪəflaɪ/
n. 萤火虫
[义] A beetle that produces a flashing light at night. 夜间能发出闪光的甲虫（lightning bug 萤火虫〔美式〕，firefly glow 萤光）。可数。
[例] ① Fireflies blinked over the meadow in summer. 夏天萤火虫在草地上闪烁。 ② Children chased fireflies in jars at dusk. 孩子们黄昏用罐子追萤火虫。
[搭] firefly glow 萤光, lightning bug 萤火虫（美式）, firefly season 萤火虫季节, catch fireflies 捉萤火虫
[联] insect 昆虫, beetle 甲虫, night 夜晚, glow 发光, summer 夏天, light 光
[记] 可数：a firefly。美式亦说 lightning bug

**snail**
英/美 /sneɪl/
n. 蜗牛
[义] A small soft animal with a spiral shell that moves very slowly. 体软、螺壳、移动极慢的小型动物（garden snail 园蜗牛，at a snail's pace 蜗牛般缓慢〔习语〕）。可数。
[例] ① A snail left a shiny trail across the path. 蜗牛在小径上留下闪亮黏液痕。 ② Snails come out after rain in the garden. 雨后花园里蜗牛爬出来。
[搭] garden snail 园蜗牛, at a snail's pace 蜗牛般缓慢, snail shell 螺壳, snail trail 蜗牛黏液痕
[联] shell 壳, slow 缓慢的, garden 花园, slug 蛞蝓, crawl 爬行, wet 潮湿的
[记] 可数：a snail。snail mail 指「纸质慢邮」，比喻义。食用蜗牛归 4-01

**starfish**
英/美 /ˈstɑːfɪʃ/
n. 海星
[义] A flat sea animal with five arms arranged in a star shape. 扁平、五臂呈星形的海洋动物（tide pool starfish 潮池海星）。可数。
[例] ① A starfish clung to the rock in the tide pool. 海星附在潮池礁石上。 ② Starfish can regenerate lost arms. 海星可再生失去的腕。
[搭] tide pool starfish 潮池海星, starfish arms 海星腕, five-armed starfish 五腕海星
[联] ocean 海洋, fish 鱼, arm 腕, reef 礁石, sea 海, marine 海洋的
[记] 可数：a starfish。虽名 fish，不是鱼类。sea star 为更精确说法

**trunk**
英/美 /trʌŋk/
n. 象鼻；树干；行李箱
[义] The long nose of an elephant; also the main stem of a tree or a large suitcase. 象的长鼻；亦指树干或大行李箱。可数。多义。
[例] ① The elephant raised its trunk to smell the wind. 象举起长鼻嗅风。 ② Elephants use their trunks to drink and greet each other. 象用长鼻饮水和互致问候。
[搭] elephant trunk 象鼻, trunk raised 举鼻, swing one's trunk 摆动象鼻
[联] elephant 象, nose 鼻, tusk 长牙, drink 饮水, mammal 哺乳动物, long 长的
[记] 可数。动物义专指象鼻；tree trunk、car trunk 为其他义

**snout**
英/美 /snaʊt/
n. 口鼻部；猪鼻
[义] The long nose and mouth of an animal such as a pig or dog. 猪、狗等动物突出的口鼻部（pig snout 猪鼻，snout first 鼻子朝前）。可数。
[例] ① The pig rooted in the soil with its snout. 猪用鼻拱土。 ② A dog nudged her hand with its wet snout. 狗用湿鼻拱她的手。
[搭] pig snout 猪鼻, dog snout 狗鼻, snout first 鼻子朝前, long snout 长吻
[联] pig 猪, dog 狗, nose 鼻, mouth 嘴, smell 嗅觉, root 拱
[记] 可数：a snout。snout 比 nose 更指动物突出口鼻

**whisker**
英/美 /ˈwɪskə(r)/
n. 胡须；触须
[义] A long stiff hair growing near the mouth of a cat, mouse, or other mammal. 猫、鼠等哺乳动物口边生长的长硬毛（cat's whiskers 猫须，by a whisker 差一点〔习语〕）。可数，常用复数 whiskers。
[例] ① The cat's whiskers twitched as it stalked the toy. 猫潜行扑玩具时胡须颤动。 ② Mice use whiskers to sense objects in the dark. 鼠用触须在黑暗中感知物体。
[搭] cat's whiskers 猫须, mouse whiskers 鼠须, by a whisker 差一点, whisker twitch 胡须颤动
[联] cat 猫, mouse 老鼠, fur 毛, sense 感知, mammal 哺乳动物, beard 胡须（人）
[记] 可数，常用复数 whiskers。the cat's whiskers 俚语指「极好的人/物」

**warm-blooded**
英/美 /ˌwɔːm ˈblʌdɪd/
adj. 温血的
[义] Having a body temperature that stays fairly constant and is not affected much by the surroundings. 体温相对稳定、不受环境大幅影响的（warm-blooded animals 温血动物，warm-blooded mammal 温血哺乳动物）。只作形容词。
[例] ① Birds and mammals are warm-blooded. 鸟和哺乳动物是温血动物。 ② Warm-blooded animals can stay active in cold weather. 温血动物在寒冷天气仍可活动。
[搭] warm-blooded animals 温血动物, warm-blooded mammal 温血哺乳动物
[联] cold-blooded 冷血的, mammal 哺乳动物, bird 鸟, blood 血, temperature 体温, active 活跃的
[记] 只作形容词。与 cold-blooded 成对。fish、reptile 多为冷血

**cold-blooded**
英/美 /ˌkəʊld ˈblʌdɪd/
adj. 冷血的；残忍的
[义] Having a body temperature that varies with the surroundings. 体温随环境变化的（cold-blooded animals 冷血动物，cold-blooded reptile 冷血爬行动物）。只作形容词。亦可喻「残忍」。
[例] ① Lizards and snakes are cold-blooded reptiles. 蜥蜴和蛇是冷血爬行动物。 ② Cold-blooded animals bask in the sun to warm up. 冷血动物晒太阳取暖。
[搭] cold-blooded animals 冷血动物, cold-blooded reptile 冷血爬行动物, cold-blooded killer 冷血杀手（喻）
[联] warm-blooded 温血的, reptile 爬行动物, fish 鱼, lizard 蜥蜴, snake 蛇, sun 阳光
[记] 只作形容词。动物义与「冷酷无情」比喻义同形

**vulture**
英/美 /ˈvʌltʃə(r)/
n. 秃鹫
[义] A large bird that feeds on dead animals. 以死动物为食的大型鸟（vulture circling 秃鹫盘旋，culture vulture 文化秃鹫〔喻〕）。可数。
[例] ① Vultures circled high above the dry plain. 秃鹫在干旱平原上空盘旋。 ② Vultures play a vital role as nature's cleaners. 秃鹫充当大自然清道夫，作用关键。
[搭] vulture circling 秃鹫盘旋, culture vulture 文化秃鹫（喻）, vulture fund 秃鹫基金（金融喻）
[联] scavenger 食腐动物, bird 鸟, prey 猎物, hyena 鬣狗, carcass 尸体, wing 翅膀
[记] 可数：a vulture。culture vulture 指「文化追星族」，比喻义

**orangutan**
英/美 /əˈræŋʊtæn/
n. 猩猩
[义] A large red-haired ape living in the rainforests of Borneo and Sumatra. 生活在婆罗洲与苏门答腊雨林的大型红毛类人猿（orangutan habitat 猩猩栖息地）。可数。
[例] ① Orangutans build nests in trees each night. 猩猩每晚在树上筑巢。 ② Palm oil farming threatens orangutan habitats. 棕榈油种植威胁猩猩栖息地。
[搭] orangutan habitat 猩猩栖息地, wild orangutan 野生猩猩, orangutan nest 猩猩巢
[联] ape 类人猿, gorilla 大猩猩, primate 灵长类, rainforest 雨林, endangered 濒危的, intelligent 聪慧的
[记] 可数：an orangutan。亦拼 orang-utang。与 chimpanzee、gorilla 并为常见类人猿

**platypus**
英/美 /ˈplætɪpəs/
n. 鸭嘴兽
[义] An Australian mammal that lays eggs and has a bill like a duck. 澳大利亚的卵生哺乳动物，喙似鸭（duck-billed platypus 鸭嘴兽）。可数。
[例] ① The platypus hunts underwater with its sensitive bill. 鸭嘴兽用敏感的喙在水下觅食。 ② Platypuses are among the few egg-laying mammals. 鸭嘴兽是少数卵生哺乳动物之一。
[搭] duck-billed platypus 鸭嘴兽, platypus bill 鸭嘴兽喙, egg-laying mammal 卵生哺乳动物
[联] mammal 哺乳动物, Australia 澳大利亚, duck 鸭, egg 蛋, unique 独特的, river 河
[记] 可数：a platypus。复数 platypuses。单孔目代表种，常作「奇特动物」例子

**migratory**
英/美 /maɪˈɡreɪtəri/
adj. 迁徙的
[义] Moving from one place to another according to the season. 按季节从一地迁往另一地的（migratory birds 候鸟，migratory route 迁徙路线）。只作形容词。
[例] ① Migratory birds rest in wetlands during their long journeys. 候鸟在长途迁徙中于湿地休憩。 ② Whales follow migratory routes along coastlines. 鲸沿海岸迁徙路线游动。
[搭] migratory birds 候鸟, migratory route 迁徙路线, migratory species 迁徙物种, migratory pattern 迁徙模式
[联] migrate 迁徙, migration 迁徙, flock 鸟群, seasonal 季节性的, wildlife 野生动物, route 路线
[记] 只作形容词。与 migrate、migration 成组记忆

**invertebrate**
英/美 /ɪnˈvɜːtɪbrət/
n. 无脊椎动物
adj. 无脊椎的
[义] An animal without a backbone, such as an insect, worm, or jellyfish. 无脊柱的动物，如昆虫、蠕虫、水母（marine invertebrates 海洋无脊椎动物）。可数/形容词。
[例] ① Coral reefs support countless invertebrates. 珊瑚礁支撑无数无脊椎动物。 ② Octopuses are highly intelligent invertebrates. 章鱼是高度聪慧的无脊椎动物。
[搭] marine invertebrates 海洋无脊椎动物, invertebrate species 无脊椎物种, spineless invertebrate 无脊椎的
[联] insect 昆虫, worm 蠕虫, jellyfish 水母, octopus 章鱼, spine 脊柱, animal 动物
[记] 可数/形容词。in-（无）+ vertebra（椎骨）。与 vertebrate（脊椎动物）相对

**plumage**
英/美 /ˈpluːmɪdʒ/
n. 羽衣；全身羽毛
[义] A bird's feathers collectively, especially when considered for colour or pattern. 鸟羽的总体，尤指就颜色或斑纹而言（bright plumage 艳丽羽色，winter plumage 冬羽）。不可数。
[例] ① The peacock's plumage shimmered in the sunlight. 孔雀羽衣在阳光下闪烁。 ② Male birds often have brighter plumage than females. 雄鸟羽色常较雌鸟鲜艳。
[搭] bright plumage 艳丽羽色, winter plumage 冬羽, colorful plumage 彩色羽毛, molt plumage 换羽
[联] feather 羽毛, bird 鸟, wing 翅膀, colorful 艳丽的, peacock 孔雀, nest 巢
[记] 不可数。略书面；日常可说 feathers。plume（羽饰）同族

**slug**
英/美 /slʌɡ/
n. 蛞蝓；子弹
[义] A slow-moving soft animal like a snail without a shell. 似蜗牛无壳、行动缓慢的软体动物（garden slug 园蛞蝓）。可数/动词。亦指子弹、猛击——多义。
[例] ① Slugs chewed holes in the lettuce leaves overnight. 蛞蝓一夜啃出莴苣叶上的洞。 ② After rain, slugs slid across the garden path. 雨后蛞蝓滑过花园小径。
[搭] garden slug 园蛞蝓, slug trail 蛞蝓黏液痕, slug damage 蛞蝓危害
[联] snail 蜗牛, garden 花园, slow 缓慢的, pest 害虫, wet 潮湿的, crawl 爬行
[记] 可数。slug 与 snail：slug 无壳。子弹义与动物无关

**tick**
英/美 /tɪk/
n. 蜱虫；滴答声
v. 滴答；打勾
[义] A small blood-sucking parasite that attaches to animals and humans. 吸附在动物和人身上吸血的小型寄生虫（tick bite 蜱叮咬，deer tick 鹿蜱）。可数/动词。亦指滴答声——多义。
[例] ① Check your dog for ticks after walks in the woods. 林间散步后检查狗身上有无蜱。 ② Tick bites can spread serious diseases. 蜱叮咬可传播严重疾病。
[搭] tick bite 蜱叮咬, deer tick 鹿蜱, remove a tick 拔除蜱虫, tick-borne disease 蜱媒疾病
[联] parasite 寄生虫, dog 狗, bite 叮咬, blood 血, pest 害虫, disease 疾病
[记] 可数/动词。动物义与 clock tick、打勾义不同

**flea**
英/美 /fliː/
n. 跳蚤
[义] A very small jumping insect that bites animals and humans and sucks blood. 叮咬人畜吸血、会跳极小昆虫（flea bite 跳蚤叮咬，flea market 跳蚤市场〔喻〕）。可数。
[例] ① The vet treated the puppy for fleas. 兽医为小狗除跳蚤。 ② Fleas can jump surprisingly far for their size. 跳蚤相对体型能跳得惊人远。
[搭] flea bite 跳蚤叮咬, flea market 跳蚤市场, flea treatment 除蚤处理, dog fleas 狗蚤
[联] insect 昆虫, dog 狗, cat 猫, bite 叮咬, parasite 寄生虫, pest 害虫
[记] 可数：a flea。a flea in one's ear 指「刺耳的拒绝」（习语）

**beetle**
英/美 /ˈbiːtl/
n. 甲虫
v. 匆忙走；突出
[义] An insect with hard wing cases that meet in a straight line on its back. 背板中线相遇的硬翅鞘昆虫（ladybug 瓢虫，dung beetle 蜣螂）。可数/动词。
[例] ① A beetle crawled across the picnic table. 甲虫爬过野餐桌。 ② Dung beetles roll balls of dung across the sand. 蜣螂在沙地上滚粪球。
[搭] ladybug 瓢虫, dung beetle 蜣螂, beetle shell 甲虫壳, ground beetle 步甲
[联] insect 昆虫, ladybug 瓢虫, shell 壳, crawl 爬行, garden 花园, hard 坚硬的
[记] 可数/动词。动词 beetle 指「匆匆走」，罕用。甲壳虫种类极多，日常泛指即可

**foal**
英/美 /fəʊl/
n. 马驹；幼马
[义] A young horse, especially one still with its mother. 年幼的马，尤指仍随母的幼马（newborn foal 新生马驹，mare and foal 母马与马驹）。可数。
[例] ① The foal stayed close to its mother in the field. 马驹在田野里紧挨母马。 ② Farmers watched the foal take its first steps. 农民看着马驹迈出第一步。
[搭] newborn foal 新生马驹, mare and foal 母马与马驹, foal at foot 随母幼驹
[联] horse 马, pony 矮种马, mare 母马, colt 幼雄马, hoof 蹄, pasture 牧场
[记] 可数：a foal。colt 常指未阉雄马驹；filly 指雌驹

**chick**
英/美 /tʃɪk/
n. 小鸡；雏鸟
[义] A baby bird, especially a young chicken. 幼鸟，尤指小鸡（newly hatched chick 刚孵出雏鸡，mother hen and chicks 母鸡与小鸡）。可数。
[例] ① Chicks peeped under the hen's wings. 小鸡在母鸡翅下唧唧叫。 ② The eagle fed fish to its hungry chick. 鹰把鱼喂给饥饿的幼雏。
[搭] newly hatched chick 刚孵出雏鸡, mother hen and chicks 母鸡与小鸡, baby chick 小鸡
[联] chicken 鸡, hen 母鸡, hatch 孵化, bird 鸟, egg 蛋, nest 巢
[记] 可数：a chick。chick 口语亦可指年轻女子，语境需谨慎

**piglet**
英/美 /ˈpɪɡlət/
n. 小猪
[义] A young pig. 年幼的猪（litter of piglets 一窝小猪，piglet squeal 小猪尖叫）。可数。
[例] ① Piglets tumbled over each other in the straw. 小猪在稻草里互相打滚。 ② The sow nursed eight healthy piglets. 母猪哺乳八只健康小猪。
[搭] litter of piglets 一窝小猪, newborn piglet 新生小猪, piglet squeal 小猪叫声
[联] pig 猪, sow 母猪, livestock 家畜, farm 农场, cute 可爱的, squeak 吱吱叫
[记] 可数：a piglet。-let 表「小」：piglet, booklet 等

**mane**
英/美 /meɪn/
n. 鬃毛
[义] The long hair growing on the back of a horse's or lion's neck. 马或狮颈背生长的长毛（lion's mane 狮鬃，flowing mane 飘逸鬃毛）。可数。
[例] ① The lion shook its mane and roared. 狮子甩鬃怒吼。 ② A horse's mane blew in the wind as it galloped. 马奔驰时鬃毛在风中飞扬。
[搭] lion's mane 狮鬃, flowing mane 飘逸鬃毛, horse mane 马鬃, thick mane 浓密的鬃毛
[联] horse 马, lion 狮子, hair 毛发, neck 脖子, fur 毛皮, gallop 奔驰
[记] 可数：a mane。main（主要的）同音异义

**tusk**
英/美 /tʌsk/
n. 长牙；象牙
[义] A long curved tooth that sticks out of the mouth of elephants, walruses, and some other animals. 象、海象等动物口外突出的长弯牙（elephant tusk 象牙，ivory tusk 象牙）。可数。
[例] ① Elephants use tusks to strip bark from trees. 象用长牙剥树皮。 ② Poachers killed elephants for their tusks. 偷猎者为取象牙杀害大象。
[搭] elephant tusk 象牙, ivory tusk 象牙, tusk poaching 盗猎象牙, walrus tusk 海象牙
[联] elephant 象, ivory 象牙, horn 角, poach 偷猎, walrus 海象, teeth 牙齿
[记] 可数：a tusk。tusk 与 horn：tusk 为牙；horn 为角质的角

**fauna**
英/美 /ˈfɔːnə/
n. 动物群；动物区系
[义] All the animals living in a particular area or period. 特定地区或时期的所有动物（local fauna 当地动物群，marine fauna 海洋动物区系）。不可数/集合名词。
[例] ① The island has unique fauna found nowhere else. 该岛有别处没有的独特动物群。 ② Guides explained the local fauna on the nature walk. 向导在自然漫步中讲解当地动物。
[搭] local fauna 当地动物群, marine fauna 海洋动物区系, island fauna 岛屿动物群, rich fauna 丰富的动物群
[联] flora 植物群, wildlife 野生动物, species 物种, habitat 栖息地, ecosystem 生态系统, animal 动物
[记] 不可数/集合名词。fauna 与 flora 成对：动物区系 vs 植物区系

**stingray**
英/美 /ˈstɪŋreɪ/
n. 魟鱼；刺魟
[义] A flat sea fish with a long tail that can sting. 扁平海鱼，长尾可蜇刺（stingray sting 魟鱼刺伤，giant stingray 巨型魟鱼）。可数。
[例] ① A stingray glided over the sandy seabed. 魟鱼滑过沙质海床。 ② Swimmers shuffle their feet to avoid stepping on stingrays. 游泳者拖脚走以防踩到魟鱼。
[搭] stingray sting 魟鱼刺伤, giant stingray 巨型魟鱼, stingray tail 魟鱼尾刺
[联] fish 鱼, ocean 海洋, sting 蜇刺, shark 鲨鱼, tail 尾巴, seabed 海床
[记] 可数：a stingray。尾刺有毒，浅滩须小心

**walrus**
英/美 /ˈwɔːlrəs/
n. 海象
[义] A large sea mammal with long tusks and whiskers, living in Arctic regions. 长牙长须、栖于北极地区的大型海洋哺乳动物（walrus tusk 海象牙，walrus herd 海象群）。可数。
[例] ① Walruses hauled themselves onto the ice floe. 海象爬上浮冰。 ② A walrus used its tusks to pull its body from the water. 海象用长牙把身体拉出水面。
[搭] walrus tusk 海象牙, walrus herd 海象群, walrus whiskers 海象须, Arctic walrus 北极海象
[联] tusk 长牙, seal 海豹, Arctic 北极的, mammal 哺乳动物, ocean 海洋, whisker 须
[记] 可数：a walrus。tusk 为海象与象共有特征

**squeak**
英/美 /skwiːk/
n. 吱吱声
v. 吱吱叫
[义] A short high sound made by a mouse, door, or small animal; to make this sound. 鼠、门或小动物发出的短促尖声（mouse squeak 鼠吱吱声，narrow squeak 九死一生〔习语〕）。可数/动词。
[例] ① We heard a squeak from behind the cupboard. 我们听见橱柜后传来吱吱声。 ② Guinea pigs squeak when they are excited. 豚鼠兴奋时会吱吱叫。
[搭] mouse squeak 鼠吱吱声, narrow squeak 九死一生, squeak by 勉强通过（喻）
[联] mouse 老鼠, guinea pig 豚鼠, rat 大鼠, sound 声音, high 尖的, small 小的
[记] 可数/动词。squeak 亦可指门轴、鞋等发出的吱呀声

**caw**
英/美 /kɔː/
n. 呱呱声
v. 呱呱叫
[义] The harsh cry of a crow or similar bird; to make this sound. 乌鸦等刺耳叫声；发出此声（crow caw 乌鸦呱呱叫）。可数/动词。
[例] ① A crow cawed from the telephone wire. 乌鸦在电话线上呱呱叫。 ② Caws echoed across the empty field. 呱呱声在空旷田野回荡。
[搭] crow caw 乌鸦呱呱叫, harsh caw 刺耳的呱呱声, caw loudly 大声呱呱叫
[联] crow 乌鸦, raven 渡鸦, bird 鸟, call 叫声, harsh 刺耳的, wing 翅膀
[记] 可数/动词。专描乌鸦科叫声；与 chirp（细鸣）对照

**hoot**
英/美 /huːt/
n. 咕咕声；鸣笛
v. 咕咕叫；鸣笛
[义] The deep call of an owl; a loud laugh or vehicle horn. 猫头鹰低沉叫声；亦指大笑或喇叭（owl hoot 猫头鹰咕咕叫，not give a hoot 毫不在乎〔习语〕）。可数/动词。
[例] ① An owl hooted in the woods after midnight. 午夜后林中猫头鹰咕咕叫。 ② We heard distant hoots from the owl nest. 我们听见远处猫头鹰巢传来的咕咕声。
[搭] owl hoot 猫头鹰咕咕叫, hoot of laughter 哄笑, not give a hoot 毫不在乎
[联] owl 猫头鹰, bird 鸟, night 夜晚, call 叫声, howl 嚎叫, forest 森林
[记] 可数/动词。hoot 亦可指汽车喇叭或哄笑，与 owl 义最绑

**flutter**
英/美 /ˈflʌtə(r)/
v. 振翅；飘动
n. 振翅；慌乱
[义] To flap wings quickly without much forward flight; (of wings) to move in this way. 快速扇翅而少前进；翅膀如此挥动（butterflies flutter 蝴蝶翩飞，heart flutter 心跳加速〔喻〕）。动词/可数名词。
[例] ① Butterflies fluttered among the wildflowers. 蝴蝶在野花间翩飞。 ② A wounded bird fluttered on the ground. 受伤鸟在地上扑腾翅膀。
[搭] butterflies flutter 蝴蝶翩飞, wings flutter 振翅, flutter away 翩然飞走
[联] butterfly 蝴蝶, bird 鸟, wing 翅膀, fly 飞, moth 蛾, delicate 轻盈的
[记] 动词/名词。flutter 亦可指纸张飘动或心里「扑通」，比喻义

**pounce**
英/美 /paʊns/
v. 猛扑；抓住
n. 猛扑
[义] To jump suddenly to catch prey; to seize quickly. 突然跃起捕猎；迅速抓住（cat pounce 猫扑，pounce on 扑向/抓住〔喻〕）。动词/可数名词。
[例] ① The cat pounced on the toy mouse. 猫扑向玩具鼠。 ② An eagle pounced from the cliff ledge. 鹰从悬崖檐上猛扑而下。
[搭] cat pounce 猫扑, pounce on prey 扑向猎物, pounce on an opportunity 抓住机会（喻）
[联] cat 猫, eagle 鹰, predator 捕食者, prey 猎物, hunt 狩猎, leap 跳跃
[记] 动词/名词。pounce on 常喻「抓住（机会、错误）」

**camouflage**
英/美 /ˈkæməflɑːʒ/
n. 伪装；迷彩
v. 伪装
[义] The colouring or pattern that helps an animal blend into its surroundings. 帮助动物融入环境的颜色或斑纹（natural camouflage 天然伪装，camouflage pattern 迷彩纹）。不可数/动词。
[例] ① The chameleon uses camouflage to hide from predators. 变色龙用伪装躲避捕食者。 ② Soldiers wore camouflage in the forest exercise. 士兵在森林演习中穿迷彩。
[源] 源自法语 *camoufler*（掩饰），军事用语入英后亦指动物保护色。
[搭] natural camouflage 天然伪装, camouflage pattern 迷彩纹, blend into 融入, protective coloration 保护色
[联] hide 躲藏, predator 捕食者, prey 猎物, pattern 斑纹, blend 融合, lizard 蜥蜴
[记] 不可数/动词。动物义与军装迷彩同词。chameleon 为经典例子

**mimicry**
英/美 /ˈmɪmɪkri/
n. 拟态；模仿
[义] When an animal copies the appearance or behaviour of another species for protection or advantage. 动物为自保或获利而模仿另一物种外形或行为的现象（Batesian mimicry 贝茨拟态， mimicry in insects 昆虫拟态， warning mimicry 警告拟态）。不可数/可数。
[例] ① Harmless snakes use mimicry to look like venomous species. 无毒蛇借拟态看起来像有毒种。 ② Insect mimicry can fool predators into staying away. 昆虫拟态可骗过捕食者使其退避。
[搭] Batesian mimicry 贝茨拟态, mimicry in insects 昆虫拟态, warning mimicry 警告拟态, defensive mimicry 防御拟态
[联] camouflage 伪装, predator 捕食者, prey 猎物, species 物种, insect 昆虫, snake 蛇, copy 模仿
[记] 不可数/可数。与 camouflage（融入背景）不同：mimicry 模仿他种外形

**shelter**
英/美 /ˈʃeltə(r)/
n. 收容所；庇护所
v. 庇护；躲避
[义] A place where homeless or stray animals are cared for; protection from danger. 照料无家可归或流浪动物的场所；躲避危险（animal shelter 动物收容所，wildlife shelter 野生动物庇护站）。可数/动词。
[例] ① They adopted a dog from the local animal shelter. 他们从当地动物收容所领养了狗。 ② The shelter cares for dozens of stray cats each winter. 收容所每年冬天照料数十只流浪猫。
[搭] animal shelter 动物收容所, wildlife shelter 野生动物庇护站, pet shelter 宠物收容所, take shelter 躲避
[联] stray 流浪的, pet 宠物, adopt 领养, homeless 无家可归的, rescue 救助, cage 笼子
[记] 可数/动词。本章取动物收容义；storm shelter 等为其他义

**poach**
英/美 /pəʊtʃ/
v. 偷猎；水煮
[义] To illegally hunt or catch animals on someone else's land or in protected areas. 在他人领地或保护区非法猎捕动物（poach elephants 偷猎大象，poach fish 偷捕鱼）。动词。亦指水煮（蛋）——多义。
[形] poached / poached / poaching
[例] ① Rangers arrested men who poached rhinos for their horns. 护林员逮捕了为取角偷猎犀牛的人。 ② Poaching threatens several endangered species. 偷猎威胁若干濒危物种。
[族] poacher 偷猎者, poaching 偷猎（名词）
[搭] poach elephants 偷猎大象, poach rhinos 偷猎犀牛, anti-poaching patrol 反偷猎巡逻, poach fish 偷捕鱼
[联] hunt 狩猎, endangered 濒危的, rhino 犀牛, elephant 象, illegal 非法的, wildlife 野生动物
[记] 动词。poached eggs 指水煮蛋，与偷猎无关。poacher 指偷猎者

**poaching**
英/美 /ˈpəʊtʃɪŋ/
n. 偷猎；盗猎
[义] The illegal hunting or catching of wild animals, especially in protected areas. 非法猎捕野生动物，尤指在保护区内（rhino poaching 盗猎犀牛， anti-poaching patrol 反偷猎巡逻， poaching crisis 偷猎危机）。不可数。
[例] ① Elephant poaching fell after stricter patrols in the park. 加强巡护后盗猎大象减少。 ② Poaching for bushmeat threatens local primate populations. 为获取野味而偷猎威胁当地灵长类种群。
[搭] rhino poaching 盗猎犀牛, anti-poaching patrol 反偷猎巡逻, poaching crisis 偷猎危机, ivory poaching 盗猎象牙
[联] poach 偷猎, poacher 偷猎者, illegal 非法的, wildlife 野生动物, endangered 濒危的, hunt 狩猎, conservation 保护
[记] 不可数。与 poach（动词）、poacher（偷猎者）同族；环保新闻高频

**poacher**
英/美 /ˈpəʊtʃə(r)/
n. 偷猎者；盗猎者
[义] A person who illegally hunts or catches wild animals, especially in protected areas. 在保护区等非法猎捕野生动物的人（wildlife poacher 盗猎野生动物者， rhino poacher 盗猎犀牛者， anti-poaching unit 反偷猎队）。可数。
[例] ① Rangers arrested poachers carrying ivory tusks. 护林员逮捕了携带象牙的偷猎者。 ② Poachers set traps along animal migration routes. 偷猎者在动物迁徙路线上设陷阱。
[搭] wildlife poacher 盗猎野生动物者, rhino poacher 盗猎犀牛者, anti-poaching unit 反偷猎队, catch a poacher 抓获偷猎者
[联] poach 偷猎, poaching 偷猎, illegal 非法的, hunt 狩猎, wildlife 野生动物, ranger 护林员, endangered 濒危的
[记] 可数：a poacher。与 poach（水煮）同形异义，依语境区分

**vertebrate**
英/美 /ˈvɜːtɪbrət/
n. 脊椎动物
adj. 有脊椎的
[义] An animal that has a backbone or spinal column. 有脊柱或脊椎的动物（vertebrate species 脊椎动物物种，marine vertebrates 海洋脊椎动物）。可数/形容词。
[例] ① Fish, birds, and mammals are all vertebrates. 鱼、鸟、哺乳动物都是脊椎动物。 ② Vertebrates make up only a small fraction of animal species. 脊椎动物仅占动物物种的一小部分。
[搭] vertebrate species 脊椎动物物种, marine vertebrates 海洋脊椎动物, vertebrate evolution 脊椎动物进化
[联] invertebrate 无脊椎动物, mammal 哺乳动物, fish 鱼, bird 鸟, spine 脊柱, backbone 脊椎
[记] 可数/形容词。与 invertebrate 成对。vertebra 指单节椎骨

**chameleon**
英/美 /kəˈmiːliən/
n. 变色龙
[义] A lizard that can change the colour of its skin to match its surroundings. 能改变肤色以配合环境的蜥蜴（chameleon colour change 变色龙变色）。可数。
[例] ① The chameleon crept slowly along the branch. 变色龙沿树枝缓慢爬行。 ② A chameleon shifted from green to brown on the bark. 变色龙在树皮上由绿转褐。
[搭] chameleon colour change 变色龙变色, chameleon tongue 变色龙舌, blend into 融入
[联] lizard 蜥蜴, reptile 爬行动物, camouflage 伪装, colour 颜色, tongue 舌头, slow 缓慢的
[记] 可数：a chameleon。喻「善变的人」：social chameleon

**flamingo**
英/美 /fləˈmɪŋɡəʊ/
n. 火烈鸟
（复数 flamingos / flamingoes）
[义] A tall pink wading bird with long legs and neck, often standing on one leg. 粉红、长腿长颈、常单腿站立的涉水鸟（flamingo flock 火烈鸟群，pink flamingo 粉红火烈鸟）。可数。
[例] ① A flock of flamingos fed in the shallow lagoon. 一群火烈鸟在浅潟湖中觅食。 ② Flamingos get their pink colour from their diet. 火烈鸟的粉红色来自食物。
[搭] flamingo flock 火烈鸟群, pink flamingo 粉红火烈鸟, stand on one leg 单腿站立
[联] bird 鸟, pink 粉红色的, flock 鸟群, wetland 湿地, wade 涉水, long-legged 长腿的
[记] 可数，复数 flamingos 或 flamingoes。塑料火烈鸟为草坪装饰文化符号

**nightingale**
英/美 /ˈnaɪtɪŋɡeɪl/
n. 夜莺
[义] A small brown bird famous for its beautiful song, especially at night. 以美妙歌声著称、尤夜鸣的褐色小鸟（nightingale song 夜莺啼）。可数。
[例] ① A nightingale sang from the thicket after dark. 夜莺天黑后从灌木丛中鸣唱。 ② Poets have praised the nightingale for centuries. 诗人数百年来赞美夜莺。
[搭] nightingale song 夜莺啼, sing like a nightingale 歌如夜莺, nightingale night 夜莺之夜
[联] bird 鸟, sing 鸣叫, night 夜晚, song 歌声, chirp 啁啾, spring 春天
[记] 可数：a nightingale。文学意象浓厚（济慈《夜莺颂》）

**mare**
英/美 /meə(r)/
n. 母马
[义] An adult female horse. 成年雌性马（mare and foal 母马与马驹，brood mare 种母马）。可数。
[例] ① The mare guarded her foal from the rest of the herd. 母马护着马驹不让马群靠近。 ② Farmers bred the mare to a strong stallion. 农民让母马与壮硕种公马配种。
[搭] mare and foal 母马与马驹, brood mare 种母马, pregnant mare 孕马
[联] horse 马, foal 马驹, stallion 种公马, colt 幼雄马, herd 兽群, pasture 牧场
[记] 可数：a mare。nightmare 原义「梦魇女妖」，与母马同形异源

**colt**
英/美 /kəʊlt/
n. 幼雄马；驹
[义] A young male horse, usually under four years old. 通常四岁以下的幼年雄马（young colt 小驹，coltish 蹦蹦跳跳的〔喻〕）。可数。
[例] ① The colt galloped beside its mother across the field. 幼马随母马奔驰过田野。 ② Trainers worked gently with the nervous young colt. 驯马师温和训练这匹紧张的幼马。
[搭] young colt 小驹, colt training 幼马训练, spirited colt 烈性幼马
[联] horse 马, foal 马驹, mare 母马, filly 幼雌马, gallop 奔驰, stallion 种公马
[记] 可数：a colt。美式橄榄球 Indianapolis Colts 为队名，不同义

**filly**
英/美 /ˈfɪli/
n. 幼雌马
[义] A young female horse, usually under four years old. 通常四岁以下的幼年雌马（racing filly 赛马雌驹）。可数。
[例] ① The filly won her first race at two years old. 这匹雌驹两岁赢得首场比赛。 ② A filly followed closely behind the mare. 幼雌马紧跟母马。
[搭] racing filly 赛马雌驹, young filly 幼雌马, filly race 雌驹赛
[联] horse 马, colt 幼雄马, mare 母马, foal 马驹, race 赛马, gallop 奔驰
[记] 可数：a filly。与 colt 性别相对

**sow**
英/美 /saʊ/
n. 母猪
v. 播种
[义] An adult female pig. 成年雌性猪（sow and piglets 母猪与小猪，old sow 老母猪）。可数/动词。动词 sow 指「播种」——同形异义。
[例] ① The sow nursed a litter of ten piglets. 母猪哺乳一窝十只小猪。 ② Sows build nests from straw before giving birth. 母猪产前用稻草筑巢。
[搭] sow and piglets 母猪与小猪, breeding sow 繁殖母猪, old sow 老母猪
[联] pig 猪, piglet 小猪, boar 公猪, livestock 家畜, farm 农场, litter 一窝
[记] 可数/动词。动物义 /saʊ/；播种义同形。boar 指公猪

**raccoon**
英/美 /rəˈkuːn/
n. 浣熊
[义] A small North American mammal with a masked face and a ringed tail, often seen in towns at night. 北美小型哺乳动物，面有黑斑、尾有环纹，夜间常见于城镇（raccoon raid 浣熊翻找垃圾）。可数。
[例] ① A raccoon knocked over the trash bin on the driveway. 浣熊弄翻了车道上的垃圾桶。 ② Raccoons wash food with their front paws. 浣熊用前爪「洗」食物。
[搭] raccoon raid 浣熊翻垃圾, raccoon mask 浣熊面斑, urban raccoon 城市浣熊
[联] mammal 哺乳动物, nocturnal 夜行的, tail 尾巴, urban 城市的, pest 害虫, clever 聪明的
[记] 可数：a raccoon。北美城郊极常见，常翻垃圾桶

**skunk**
英/美 /skʌŋk/
n. 臭鼬
[义] A black-and-white North American animal that sprays a strong unpleasant smell when threatened. 北美黑白动物，受威胁时喷出强烈恶臭液体（skunk spray 臭鼬喷雾）。可数。
[例] ① The dog was sprayed by a skunk in the backyard. 狗在后院被臭鼬喷了一身。 ② Skunks are easy to recognize by their bold stripes. 臭鼬醒目的条纹极易辨认。
[搭] skunk spray 臭鼬喷雾, striped skunk 条纹臭鼬, smell like a skunk 臭如臭鼬
[联] mammal 哺乳动物, smell 气味, spray 喷射, stripe 条纹, nocturnal 夜行的, pest 害虫
[记] 可数：a skunk。drunk as a skunk 烂醉（俚语）

**hedgehog**
英/美 /ˈhedʒhɒɡ/
n. 刺猬
[义] A small mammal covered with sharp spines that rolls into a ball when threatened. 身披尖刺、受威胁时蜷成球的小型哺乳动物（hedgehog spines 刺猬刺，hedgehog garden 花园刺猬）。可数。
[例] ① A hedgehog snuffled along the garden path at dusk. 黄昏时刺猬在花园小径上嗅闻前行。 ② Hedgehogs curl into spiny balls when predators approach. 捕食者靠近时刺猬蜷成刺球。
[搭] hedgehog spines 刺猬刺, garden hedgehog 花园刺猬, curl into a ball 蜷成球
[联] mammal 哺乳动物, spines 刺, garden 花园, nocturnal 夜行的, small 小的, curl 蜷缩
[记] 可数：a hedgehog。英式花园常见野生动物。hog 古义「猪」，与刺猬外形联想

**bison**
英/美 /ˈbaɪsn/
n. 野牛；美洲野牛
（复数 bison）
[义] A large wild ox with a shaggy mane and curved horns, native to North America and Europe. 鬃毛蓬、弯角、原产北美欧洲的大型野牛（American bison 美洲野牛，bison herd 野牛群）。单复数同形。
[例] ① Bison once roamed the plains in vast herds. 野牛曾以大群漫游平原。 ② A bison raised its massive head above the tall grass. 一头野牛从高草中抬起巨首。
[搭] American bison 美洲野牛, bison herd 野牛群, plains bison 平原野牛
[联] herd 兽群, horn 角, mammal 哺乳动物, prairie 草原, wild 野生的, cattle 牛
[记] 单复数同形：one bison, many bison。与 buffalo 常混用，北美多称 bison

**alpaca**
英/美 /ælˈpækə/
n. 羊驼
[义] A South American animal related to the llama, kept for its soft wool. 南美动物，与 llama 近缘，饲养取柔软羊毛（alpaca wool 羊驼毛，herd of alpacas 羊驼群）。可数。
[例] ① Alpacas grazed on the hillside farm. 羊驼在山坡农场吃草。 ② Soft alpaca wool is prized for sweaters. 柔软的羊驼毛适于织毛衣。
[搭] alpaca wool 羊驼毛, herd of alpacas 羊驼群, alpaca farm 羊驼农场
[联] llama 美洲驼, wool 羊毛, livestock 家畜, South America 南美, herd 兽群, soft 柔软的
[记] 可数：an alpaca。alpaca 比 llama 体型小，毛更细软

**llama**
英/美 /ˈlɑːmə/
n. 美洲驼
[义] A South American animal related to the camel, used for carrying loads in the mountains. 南美动物，与骆驼同科，用于山地驮载（pack llama 驮运美洲驼，llama trek 美洲驼徒步）。可数。
[例] ① Llamas carried gear up the mountain trail. 美洲驼把装备驮上山道。 ② A llama spat when a tourist got too close. 游客靠太近时美洲驼吐了口水。
[搭] pack llama 驮运美洲驼, llama trek 美洲驼徒步, llama herd 美洲驼群
[联] alpaca 羊驼, camel 骆驼, herd 兽群, mountain 山地, carry 驮运, South America 南美
[记] 可数：a llama。llama 比 alpaca 大，更常用作役畜

**pod**
英/美 /pɒd/
n. 鲸群；豆荚
[义] A small group of sea animals, especially whales or dolphins, swimming together. 一起活动的小型海洋动物群，尤指鲸或海豚（dolphin pod 海豚群，pod of whales 鲸群）。可数。亦指豆荚——多义。
[例] ① A pod of dolphins rode the bow wave beside the ship. 一群海豚在船舷旁乘浪而游。 ② The documentary followed a whale pod across the Pacific. 纪录片跟拍一群鲸穿越太平洋。
[搭] dolphin pod 海豚群, pod of whales 鲸群, whale pod 鲸群, pod member 群成员
[联] dolphin 海豚, whale 鲸, herd 兽群, flock 群, ocean 海洋, swim 游泳
[记] 可数。动物义专指海洋哺乳类小群；pea pod 为豆荚

**colony**
英/美 /ˈkɒləni/
n. 群落；殖民地
[义] A group of the same type of animal living or nesting together. 同种动物共同生活或筑巢的群体（penguin colony 企鹅群落，bat colony 蝙蝠群，ant colony 蚁群）。可数。亦指殖民地——多义。
[例] ① The penguin colony numbered thousands of breeding pairs. 企鹅群落有数千对繁殖个体。 ② A bat colony roosted in the old cave. 蝙蝠群在旧洞中栖息。
[搭] penguin colony 企鹅群落, bat colony 蝙蝠群, ant colony 蚁群, seabird colony 海鸟群落
[联] flock 群, herd 兽群, nest 巢, penguin 企鹅, ant 蚂蚁, group 群体
[记] 可数。动物义与政治殖民地义同形。ant colony 亦指蚁群社会结构

**diurnal**
英/美 /daɪˈɜːnl/
adj. 昼行的；每日的
[义] Active during the day rather than at night. 白天而非夜间活动（diurnal animals 昼行动物，diurnal rhythm 昼夜节律）。只作形容词。
[例] ① Squirrels are diurnal and forage in daylight. 松鼠昼行，白天觅食。 ② Most birds of prey are diurnal hunters. 多数猛禽昼间捕猎。
[搭] diurnal animals 昼行动物, diurnal rhythm 昼夜节律, diurnal activity 昼间活动
[联] nocturnal 夜行的, day 白天, active 活动的, bird 鸟, squirrel 松鼠, hunt 狩猎
[记] 只作形容词。与 nocturnal 成对。diurnal 亦指「每日的」（diurnal temperature）

**gnaw**
英/美 /nɔː/
v. 啃咬；折磨
[义] To bite or chew something repeatedly, as rodents do. 反复咬嚼，如啮齿动物所为（gnaw at bone 啃骨头，gnaw through 啃穿）。动词。
[形] gnawed / gnawed / gnawing
[例] ① Rats gnawed through the wooden crate. 老鼠啃穿了木箱。 ② Beavers gnaw down trees to build dams. 河狸啃倒树木筑坝。
[搭] gnaw at bone 啃骨头, gnaw through 啃穿, gnaw on 啃咬
[联] rodent 啮齿动物, rat 大鼠, mouse 老鼠, teeth 牙齿, chew 咀嚼, bite 咬
[记] 动词。gnaw 亦可喻「焦虑折磨」（gnawing worry），比喻义

**woof**
英/美 /wʊf/
n. 汪汪声
v. 汪汪叫
[义] The sound a dog makes; to bark in a deep voice. 狗发出的叫声；低沉吠叫（woof woof 汪汪，拟声）。可数/动词。
[例] ① The dog gave a friendly woof at the door. 狗在门口友好地汪了一声。 ② Big dogs woof more than small yappy ones. 大狗比爱叫的小狗吠声更低沉。
[搭] woof woof 汪汪, deep woof 低沉吠声, friendly woof 友好的叫声
[联] bark 狗吠, dog 狗, puppy 小狗, sound 声音, pet 宠物, loud 响亮的
[记] 可数/动词。woof 亦指织物「纬线」或「汪」打招呼（俚语），不同义

**horseshoe crab**
英/美 /ˈhɔːsʃuː kræb/
n. 鲎；马蹄蟹
[义] A large sea animal with a hard shell and a long tail, more closely related to spiders than to crabs. 硬壳长尾的大型海生动物，与蜘蛛的亲缘近于蟹（horseshoe crab blood 鲎血）。可数。
[例] ① Horseshoe crabs crawl ashore to lay eggs in spring. 鲎春天爬上海滩产卵。 ② Their blue blood is used in medical testing. 其蓝色血液用于医学检测。
[搭] horseshoe crab blood 鲎血, horseshoe crab spawning 鲎产卵, living fossil 活化石
[联] crab 蟹, ocean 海洋, shell 壳, spider 蜘蛛, ancient 古老的, beach 海滩
[记] 可数：a horseshoe crab。虽名 crab，不是真蟹，常被称「活化石」

**wildebeest**
英/美 /ˈwɪldəbiːst/
n. 角马；牛羚
[义] A large African antelope with curved horns, famous for mass migration. 弯角非洲大羚羊，以大规模迁徙著称（wildebeest migration 角马迁徙，gnu 同义）。可数。
[例] ① Wildebeest crossed the river in huge numbers during migration. 迁徙时角马大批渡河。 ② Crocodiles waited as wildebeest entered the water. 角马入水时鳄鱼伺机而动。
[搭] wildebeest migration 角马迁徙, gnu 角马（同义）, herd of wildebeest 角马群
[联] zebra 斑马, herd 兽群, migrate 迁徙, Africa 非洲, antelope 羚羊, savanna 稀树草原
[记] 可数：a wildebeest。亦称 gnu /ɡnuː/。非洲迁徙纪录片标志物种

**sloth**
英/美 /sləʊθ/
n. 树懒
[义] A slow-moving South American mammal that hangs upside down from trees. 行动缓慢、倒挂树上的南美哺乳动物（three-toed sloth 三趾树懒）。可数。亦指「懒惰」——多义。
[例] ① A sloth moved slowly along the branch. 树懒沿树枝缓慢移动。 ② Sloths sleep up to twenty hours a day. 树懒每天可睡二十小时。
[搭] three-toed sloth 三趾树懒, two-toed sloth 二趾树懒, sloth habitat 树懒栖息地
[联] mammal 哺乳动物, slow 缓慢的, tree 树, lazy 懒惰的, rainforest 雨林, hang 悬挂
[记] 可数：a sloth。sloth 七宗罪之一「懒惰」与动物同形

**boar**
英/美 /bɔː(r)/
n. 公猪；野猪
[义] A wild pig or an adult male domestic pig. 野猪或家猪成年雄性（wild boar 野猪，boar hunt 猎野猪）。可数。
[例] ① Wild boars rooted up the forest floor at night. 野猪夜间拱翻林地地表。 ② The farmer kept one boar for breeding. 农民留一头公猪配种。
[搭] wild boar 野猪, boar hunt 猎野猪, breeding boar 种公猪
[联] pig 猪, sow 母猪, piglet 小猪, tusk 长牙, wild 野生的, forest 森林
[记] 可数：a boar。sow 为母猪，boar 为公猪

**school**
英/美 /skuːl/
n. 鱼群；学校
v. 集群游动
[义] A large group of fish swimming together. 一起游动的大群鱼（school of fish 鱼群，schooling fish 集群鱼类）。可数/动词。学校义极高频——多义。
[例] ① A school of fish shimmered beneath the boat. 一群鱼在船下闪光游动。 ② Tuna school in open water for safety. 金枪鱼在开阔水域集群以保安全。
[搭] school of fish 鱼群, schooling fish 集群鱼类, break formation 散开队形
[联] fish 鱼, shoal 鱼群, ocean 海洋, swim 游泳, tuna 金枪鱼, group 群体
[记] 多义：school 学校义更常见。鱼群亦可用 shoal。动词 school 指鱼集群

**anteater**
英/美 /ˈæntɪːtə(r)/
n. 食蚁兽
[义] A mammal with a long snout and tongue that feeds mainly on ants. 长吻长舌、主食蚂蚁的哺乳动物（giant anteater 大食蚁兽）。可数。
[例] ① The anteater lapped up ants with its sticky tongue. 食蚁兽用黏舌舔食蚂蚁。 ② Anteaters have poor eyesight but a keen sense of smell. 食蚁兽视力差但嗅觉灵敏。
[搭] giant anteater 大食蚁兽, anteater tongue 食蚁兽长舌, feed on ants 以蚁为食
[联] ant 蚂蚁, mammal 哺乳动物, tongue 舌头, snout 长吻, insect 昆虫, South America 南美
[记] 可数：an anteater。aardvark 亦食蚁，但非洲原产、不同物种

**armadillo**
英/美 /ˌɑːməˈdɪləʊ/
n. 犰狳
[义] A small mammal with a hard shell on its back that can roll into a ball. 背有硬壳、可蜷成球的小型哺乳动物（nine-banded armadillo 九带犰狳）。可数。
[例] ① An armadillo crossed the road at twilight. 黄昏时一只犰狳穿过公路。 ② Armadillos dig for insects and grubs in the soil. 犰狳在土里挖食昆虫和幼虫。
[搭] nine-banded armadillo 九带犰狳, armadillo shell 犰狳壳, roll into a ball 蜷成球
[联] mammal 哺乳动物, shell 壳, insect 昆虫, dig 挖, nocturnal 夜行的, curl 蜷缩
[记] 可数：an armadillo。西班牙语借词，意为「小甲兵」

**buffalo**
英/美 /ˈbʌfələʊ/
n. 水牛；野牛
（复数 buffalo / buffaloes）
[义] A large ox native to Africa and Asia, or the North American bison. 非洲亚洲原产大型牛；亦指北美野牛（water buffalo 水牛，Cape buffalo 非洲水牛）。单复数同形或 buffaloes。
[例] ① Water buffalo pulled carts through the muddy fields. 水牛拉车穿过泥泞田地。 ② Cape buffalo are among Africa's most dangerous animals. 非洲水牛是非洲最危险的动物之一。
[搭] water buffalo 水牛, Cape buffalo 非洲水牛, buffalo herd 水牛群, American buffalo 美洲野牛
[联] bison 野牛, cattle 牛, horn 角, herd 兽群, Africa 非洲, farm 农场
[记] 可数。与 bison 常混用；严格说美洲野牛多称 bison，亚洲非洲多称 buffalo

**orca**
英/美 /ˈɒrkə/
n. 虎鲸；逆戟鲸
[义] A large black-and-white predatory sea mammal, also called killer whale. 大型黑白捕食性海洋哺乳动物，亦称 killer whale（orca pod 虎鲸群）。可数。
[例] ① An orca breached beside the research boat. 虎鲸在研究船旁跃出水面。 ② Orcas hunt in coordinated pods. 虎鲸以协调的群体捕猎。
[搭] orca pod 虎鲸群, killer whale 虎鲸, orca breach 虎鲸跃出
[联] whale 鲸, dolphin 海豚, predator 捕食者, pod 群, ocean 海洋, black and white 黑白的
[记] 可数：an orca。orca 与 dolphin 同科；killer whale 为常用别名

**porpoise**
英/美 /ˈpɔːpəs/
n. 鼠海豚
[义] A small sea mammal related to dolphins, with a rounded snout. 与海豚同科、吻部圆钝的小型海洋哺乳动物（harbour porpoise 港湾鼠海豚）。可数。
[例] ① Porpoises surfaced briefly near the harbour wall. 鼠海豚在港墙附近短暂浮出水面。 ② Porpoises are smaller and shy compared with dolphins. 鼠海豚比海豚更小、更羞怯。
[搭] harbour porpoise 港湾鼠海豚, porpoise sighting 鼠海豚目击, small cetacean 小型鲸豚
[联] dolphin 海豚, whale 鲸, ocean 海洋, mammal 哺乳动物, snout 吻部, swim 游泳
[记] 可数：a porpoise。porpoise 吻短圆；dolphin 吻通常更长

**fawn**
英/美 /fɑːn/
n. 幼鹿
v. 奉承
[义] A young deer in its first year. 一岁以内的幼鹿（fawn coat 鹿斑纹，spotted fawn 有斑幼鹿）。可数/动词。动词 fawn 指「奉承」——多义。
[例] ① A fawn lay hidden in the tall grass. 幼鹿藏在高草中。 ② The doe left her fawn while she foraged nearby. 母鹿在附近觅食时离开幼鹿。
[搭] spotted fawn 有斑幼鹿, fawn coat 鹿斑纹, newborn fawn 新生幼鹿
[联] deer 鹿, doe 母鹿, forest 森林, spotted 有斑点的, young 年幼的, hide 躲藏
[记] 可数/动词。动物义与奉承义同形。fawn colour 指浅黄褐色

**shoal**
英/美 /ʃəʊl/
n. 鱼群；浅滩
v. 集群游动
[义] A large group of fish swimming together; a shallow area of water. 一起游动的大群鱼；浅水区（shoal of fish 鱼群，shoaling fish 集群鱼）。可数/动词。
[例] ① A shoal of sardines moved like a silver cloud. 一群沙丁鱼如银云移动。 ② Fish shoal near the reef for protection. 鱼在礁旁集群以获保护。
[搭] shoal of fish 鱼群, shoaling fish 集群鱼, break the shoal 打散鱼群
[联] school 鱼群, fish 鱼, ocean 海洋, swim 游泳, group 群体, reef 礁石
[记] 可数/动词。shoal 与 school 均可指鱼群；shoal 亦指浅滩

**rook**
英/美 /rʊk/
n. 白嘴乌鸦
[义] A large black bird similar to a crow, nesting in colonies in treetops. 似乌鸦的大型黑鸟，在树顶群巢（rookery 鸦巢群）。可数。
[例] ① Rooks nested in tall elms above the village. 白嘴乌鸦在村上方高大榆树上筑巢。 ② A rook cawed from the churchyard trees. 白嘴乌鸦在教堂墓地树上呱呱叫。
[搭] rookery 鸦巢群, rook caw 白嘴乌鸦叫声, colony of rooks 白嘴乌鸦群
[联] crow 乌鸦, raven 渡鸦, bird 鸟, nest 巢, black 黑色的, flock 鸟群
[记] 可数：a rook。rookery 指鸦群繁殖地。国际象棋 rook 指「车」，不同义

**magpie**
英/美 /ˈmæɡpaɪ/
n. 喜鹊
[义] A black-and-white bird known for collecting shiny objects and chattering calls. 黑白相间、以收集闪亮物子和嘈杂叫声著称的鸟（magpie nest 喜鹊巢）。可数。
[例] ① A magpie swooped down and stole a shiny bottle cap. 喜鹊俯冲偷走闪亮瓶盖。 ② Magpies chatter loudly from the fence posts. 喜鹊在栅栏柱上嘈杂鸣叫。
[搭] magpie nest 喜鹊巢, magpie chatter 喜鹊嘈杂叫声, black and white 黑白的
[联] bird 鸟, crow 乌鸦, nest 巢, shiny 闪亮的, chatter 嘈杂, clever 聪明的
[记] 可数：a magpie。one for sorrow, two for joy 为英国民间数鹊迷信

**stallion**
英/美 /ˈstæliən/
n. 种公马；牡马
[义] An adult male horse, especially one kept for breeding. 成年雄马，尤指用于配种的（stud stallion 种公马，wild stallion 野马）。可数。
[例] ① The stallion guarded his herd on the open range. 种公马在开阔牧场护着马群。 ② Trainers handled the powerful black stallion with care. 驯马师小心驾驭这匹强壮的黑牡马。
[搭] stud stallion 种公马, wild stallion 野马, stallion herd 以种公马为首的马群
[联] mare 母马, horse 马, colt 幼雄马, gallop 奔驰, herd 兽群, breeding 繁殖
[记] 可数：a stallion。与 mare（母马）性别相对

**antelope**
英/美 /ˈæntɪləʊp/
n. 羚羊
[义] A swift hoofed mammal found in Africa and Asia, often with horns. 非洲亚洲常见、常具角、奔跑迅捷的有蹄哺乳动物（impala 黑斑羚，antelope herd 羚羊群）。可数。
[例] ① Antelope sprinted across the savanna in a blur. 羚羊飞掠过稀树草原。 ② Cheetahs hunt antelope on the open plain. 猎豹在开阔平原猎羚羊。
[搭] antelope herd 羚羊群, impala 黑斑羚, swift antelope 敏捷的羚羊
[联] deer 鹿, horn 角, herd 兽群, Africa 非洲, graze 啃食, swift 敏捷的
[记] 可数：an antelope。多种羚羊，impala、gnu 等可单独学

**beaver**
英/美 /ˈbiːvə(r)/
n. 河狸；海狸
v. 勤奋工作
[义] A brown furry mammal that builds dams across rivers with branches. 棕色毛皮、用树枝在河上筑坝的哺乳动物（beaver dam 河狸坝，busy as a beaver 忙如河狸〔习语〕）。可数/动词。
[例] ① Beavers gnawed through trees to block the stream. 河狸啃倒树木拦溪筑坝。 ② A beaver lodge rose from the middle of the pond. 河狸巢露出池塘中央。
[搭] beaver dam 河狸坝, beaver lodge 河狸巢, busy as a beaver 忙如河狸, gnaw trees 啃树
[联] gnaw 啃咬, dam 水坝, pond 池塘, fur 毛皮, mammal 哺乳动物, river 河
[记] 可数/动词。动词 beaver away 指「埋头苦干」，比喻义

**moose**
英/美 /muːs/
n. 驼鹿；麋
（复数 moose）
[义] A very large deer with broad flat antlers, found in northern forests. 角宽平、分布于北方森林的特大鹿（moose antlers 驼鹿角，moose crossing 驼鹿穿越路标）。单复数同形。
[例] ① A moose waded through the shallow lake at dusk. 黄昏时一头驼鹿涉过浅湖。 ② Moose browse on willow branches in summer. 驼鹿夏天啃食柳枝。
[搭] moose antlers 驼鹿角, moose crossing 驼鹿穿越路标, Alaskan moose 阿拉斯加驼鹿
[联] deer 鹿, antler 鹿角, forest 森林, large 巨大的, northern 北方的, elk 麋鹿
[记] 单复数同形：one moose, many moose。美式 moose；英式 elk 有时指同种

**elk**
英/美 /elk/
n. 麋鹿；驼鹿
（复数 elk）
[义] A large deer with broad antlers; in American English, the same animal as moose. 角宽的大型鹿；美式英语中与 moose 常指同种（elk herd 麋鹿群）。单复数同形。英式 elk 可指马鹿。
[例] ① Elk grazed in meadows below the mountains. 麋鹿在山下草甸吃草。 ② Drivers slowed for elk near the national park road. 司机在国家公园公路附近为麋鹿减速。
[搭] elk herd 麋鹿群, Rocky Mountain elk 落基山麋鹿, elk antlers 麋鹿角
[联] deer 鹿, moose 驼鹿, antler 鹿角, herd 兽群, forest 森林, graze 啃食
[记] 单复数同形。美式 elk 与 moose 地区用法有别，宜对照当地说法

**gecko**
英/美 /ˈɡekəʊ/
n. 壁虎
[义] A small lizard with soft skin, often found in houses in warm countries. 软皮小型蜥蜴，暖区屋内常见（house gecko 家壁虎，gecko feet 壁虎足）。可数。
[例] ① A gecko clicked on the wall near the lamp. 壁虎在灯边墙上发出咔嗒声。 ② Geckos can cling to smooth vertical surfaces. 壁虎能附在光滑垂直表面。
[搭] house gecko 家壁虎, gecko feet 壁虎足, tropical gecko 热带壁虎
[联] lizard 蜥蜴, reptile 爬行动物, wall 墙, tropical 热带的, cling 附着, insect 昆虫
[记] 可数：a gecko。gecko 足垫为仿生粘附研究对象

**porcupine**
英/美 /ˈpɔːrkjupaɪn/
n. 豪猪；箭猪
[义] A rodent with long sharp quills on its back and sides. 背侧有长尖刺的啮齿动物（porcupine quills 豪猪刺）。可数。
[例] ① A porcupine raised its quills when the dog approached. 狗靠近时豪猪竖起刺。 ② Porcupines climb trees to reach tender bark. 豪猪爬树啃嫩树皮。
[搭] porcupine quills 豪猪刺, raise quills 竖起刺, tree porcupine 树栖豪猪
[联] rodent 啮齿动物, quill 刺, spike 尖刺, forest 森林, defensive 防御的, mammal 哺乳动物
[记] 可数：a porcupine。porcupine 与 hedgehog 不同：豪猪刺可脱落射出

**opossum**
英/美 /əˈpɒsəm/
n. 负鼠
[义] A small American marsupial that often hangs by its tail and plays dead when threatened. 常倒挂尾巴、受威胁时装死的小型美洲有袋动物（play possum 装死〔习语〕）。可数。
[例] ① An opossum crossed the fence at night. 负鼠夜里翻过栅栏。 ② When cornered, the opossum played dead on the lawn. 被逼到角落时负鼠在草坪上装死。
[搭] play possum 装死, Virginia opossum 北美负鼠, opossum tail 负鼠尾
[联] marsupial 有袋动物, mammal 哺乳动物, nocturnal 夜行的, tail 尾巴, play dead 装死, North America 北美
[记] 可数：an opossum。口语常简称 possum。play possum 为固定习语

**meow**
英/美 /miˈaʊ/
n. 喵叫
v. 喵喵叫
[义] The cry of a cat; to make this sound. 猫的叫声；发出此声（cat meow 猫喵叫）。可数/动词。亦拼 miaow。
[例] ① The kitten meowed for food at the door. 小猫在门口喵喵要食。 ② A stray cat meowed outside the window. 流浪猫在窗外喵喵叫。
[搭] cat meow 猫喵叫, kitten meow 小猫喵叫, loud meow 大声喵叫
[联] cat 猫, kitten 小猫, bark 狗吠, pet 宠物, sound 声音, cry 叫声
[记] 可数/动词。亦拼 miaow /mjaʊ/。拟声词，猫专用

**quack**
英/美 /kwæk/
n. 嘎嘎声；江湖郎中
v. 嘎嘎叫
[义] The sound a duck makes; to make this sound. 鸭的叫声；发出此声（duck quack 鸭嘎嘎叫）。可数/动词。亦指「庸医」——多义。
[例] ① Ducks quacked loudly on the village pond. 鸭在村池塘里大声嘎嘎叫。 ② A lone duck quacked as it paddled across the lake. 一只鸭游过湖面时嘎嘎叫。
[搭] duck quack 鸭嘎嘎叫, quack loudly 大声嘎嘎叫, quack like a duck 像鸭一样叫
[联] duck 鸭, goose 鹅, bird 鸟, pond 池塘, sound 声音, water 水
[记] 可数/动词。quack doctor 指「江湖郎中」，与鸭叫无关

**iguana**
英/美 /ɪˈɡwɑːnə/
n. 鬣蜥
[义] A large tropical lizard, often green, with a row of spines along its back. 热带大型蜥蜴，常绿色、背有棘刺（green iguana 绿鬣蜥）。可数。
[例] ① An iguana sunned itself on a branch over the river. 鬣蜥在河上树枝晒太阳。 ② Iguanas bask on rocks in the tropical heat. 鬣蜥在热带高温下卧岩晒背。
[搭] green iguana 绿鬣蜥, iguana spine 鬣蜥背棘, tropical iguana 热带鬣蜥
[联] lizard 蜥蜴, reptile 爬行动物, tropical 热带的, sun 阳光, spines 刺, green 绿色的
[记] 可数：an iguana。常见宠物爬宠，需恒温环境

**manatee**
英/美 /ˈmænəti/
n. 海牛
[义] A large gentle sea mammal with a paddle-shaped tail, found in warm coastal waters. 尾似桨、性情温和、见于暖海岸水域的大型海洋哺乳动物（Florida manatee 佛罗里达海牛）。可数。
[例] ① Manatees surfaced slowly in the warm spring. 海牛在暖泉中缓缓上浮。 ② Boaters must slow down to protect manatees in the channel. 船主须减速以保护水道中的海牛。
[搭] Florida manatee 佛罗里达海牛, manatee sanctuary 海牛保护区, gentle giant 温和的巨兽
[联] mammal 哺乳动物, ocean 海洋, gentle 温和的, flipper 鳍肢, endangered 濒危的, slow 缓慢的
[记] 可数：a manatee。又称 sea cow。佛罗里达保护新闻常见

**puffin**
英/美 /ˈpʌfɪn/
n. 海鹦；角嘴海雀
[义] A seabird with a black-and-white body and a large colourful beak. 黑白体羽、喙大且色彩鲜艳的海鸟（Atlantic puffin 大西洋海鹦，puffin colony 海鹦群落）。可数。
[例] ① Puffins nested on the cliff ledges above the sea. 海鹦在海上方悬崖檐上筑巢。 ② A puffin carried several fish in its beak. 海鹦喙中夹着几条鱼。
[搭] Atlantic puffin 大西洋海鹦, puffin colony 海鹦群落, colourful beak 彩色喙
[联] bird 鸟, beak 喙, ocean 海洋, cliff 悬崖, nest 巢, colony 群落
[记] 可数：a puffin。喙色彩鲜明，为北欧旅游标志鸟之一

**seahorse**
英/美 /ˈsiːhɔːs/
n. 海马
[义] A small sea fish with a head like a horse and a curled tail. 头似马、尾卷曲的小型海鱼（seahorse tail 海马尾，male seahorse 雄性海马育幼）。可数。
[例] ① A seahorse drifted among the seagrass. 海马在海草间漂动。 ② Male seahorses carry eggs in a brood pouch. 雄性海马在育幼袋中携带卵。
[搭] seahorse tail 海马尾, male seahorse 雄性海马, seahorse habitat 海马栖息地
[联] fish 鱼, ocean 海洋, tail 尾巴, curl 卷曲, marine 海洋的, unique 独特的
[记] 可数：a seahorse。虽名 horse，是鱼不是马。雄性负责孵育

**coyote**
英/美 /kaɪˈəʊti/
n. 郊狼；草原狼
[义] A wild dog-like animal of North America, smaller than a wolf and often living near towns. 北美类犬野生动物，比狼小，常栖于城镇附近（coyote howl 郊狼嚎叫，urban coyote 城市郊狼）。可数。
[例] ① Coyotes howled in the hills behind the suburb. 郊狼在郊区后面的山丘上嚎叫。 ② Urban coyotes hunt rodents in alleys at night. 城市郊狼夜间在小巷捕鼠。
[搭] coyote howl 郊狼嚎叫, urban coyote 城市郊狼, coyote pack 郊狼群
[联] wolf 狼, fox 狐狸, howl 嚎叫, wild 野生的, North America 北美, predator 捕食者
[记] 可数：a coyote。Wile E. Coyote 为流行文化经典形象

**badger**
英/美 /ˈbædʒə(r)/
n. 獾
v. 纠缠；烦扰
[义] A grey-and-white mammal that lives underground and comes out at night. 灰白相间、地下穴居、夜出活动的哺乳动物（European badger 欧洲獾，badger sett 獾穴）。可数/动词。动词 badger 指「纠缠」——多义。
[例] ① A badger shuffled across the lane after dark. 天黑后一只獾慢步穿过小路。 ② Badgers dig extensive setts in woodland banks. 獾在林岸挖出庞大的穴系。
[搭] European badger 欧洲獾, badger sett 獾穴, honey badger 蜜獾
[联] burrow 洞穴, nocturnal 夜行的, mammal 哺乳动物, sett 穴系, forest 森林, stripe 条纹
[记] 可数/动词。badger someone 指「烦扰某人」，与动物无关。honey badger 为不同物种

**narwhal**
英/美 /ˈnɑːwəl/
n. 一角鲸
[义] An Arctic whale with a long spiral tusk growing from its upper jaw. 上颌长出长螺旋长牙的北极鲸（narwhal tusk 一角鲸长牙，Arctic narwhal 北极一角鲸）。可数。
[例] ① Narwhals surfaced among the ice floes. 一角鲸在浮冰间浮出水面。 ② The male narwhal's tusk can grow over two metres long. 雄一角鲸的长牙可超过两米。
[搭] narwhal tusk 一角鲸长牙, Arctic narwhal 北极一角鲸, unicorn of the sea 海中独角兽（喻）
[联] whale 鲸, tusk 长牙, Arctic 北极的, ocean 海洋, mammal 哺乳动物, ice 冰
[记] 可数：a narwhal。常称「海中独角兽」。长牙实为特化犬齿

**doe**
英/美 /dəʊ/
n. 雌鹿；雌兔
[义] A female deer or rabbit. 雌性鹿或兔（doe and fawn 母鹿与幼鹿）。可数。
[例] ① The doe led her fawn into the tree line. 母鹿带幼鹿进入林缘。 ② A doe grazed quietly while keeping watch for predators. 母鹿安静吃草同时警戒捕食者。
[搭] doe and fawn 母鹿与幼鹿, female deer 雌鹿, watchful doe 警觉的母鹿
[联] deer 鹿, fawn 幼鹿, buck 雄鹿, rabbit 兔, forest 森林, graze 啃食
[记] 可数：a doe。buck 指雄鹿；doe 指雌鹿。John Doe 为「某先生」，不同义

**dragonfly**
英/美 /ˈdræɡənflaɪ/
n. 蜻蜓
[义] A long-bodied insect with two pairs of large transparent wings. 体细长、两对大透明翅的昆虫（dragonfly wings 蜻蜓翅，damselfly 豆娘〔近缘〕）。可数。
[例] ① A dragonfly hovered over the pond before darting away. 蜻蜓在池塘上悬停后倏然飞走。 ② Dragonflies hunt mosquitoes in flight. 蜻蜓飞行中捕蚊。
[搭] dragonfly wings 蜻蜓翅, hover over water 在水面上悬停, dart away 倏然飞走
[联] insect 昆虫, wing 翅膀, fly 飞, pond 池塘, mosquito 蚊子, transparent 透明的
[记] 可数：a dragonfly。damselfly（豆娘）体型更细，翅合拢

**cicada**
英/美 /sɪˈkɑːdə/
n. 蝉
[义] A large insect that makes a loud buzzing sound in summer, especially in warm regions. 夏季、尤暖区发出响亮嗡鸣的大型昆虫（cicada song 蝉鸣，periodical cicada 周期蝉）。可数。
[例] ① Cicadas filled the afternoon with their steady drone. 蝉声填满午后的空气。 ② After seventeen years underground, cicadas emerged in millions. 地下十七年后，数以百万计蝉羽化而出。
[搭] cicada song 蝉鸣, periodical cicada 周期蝉, summer cicada 夏蝉, cicada shell 蝉蜕
[联] insect 昆虫, buzz 嗡嗡叫, summer 夏天, shell 壳, loud 响亮的, emerge 羽化
[记] 可数：a cicada。周期蝉为北美自然现象新闻常见

**shrew**
英/美 /ʃruː/
n. 鼩鼱；泼妇
[义] A tiny mouse-like mammal with a long pointed snout; (archaic) a bad-tempered woman. 吻尖似鼠的极小哺乳动物；古语指脾气坏的女人（pygmy shrew 侏儒鼩鼱）。可数。动物义与「泼妇」比喻义同形。
[例] ① A shrew darted through the leaf litter in seconds. 一只鼩鼱几秒内窜过落叶层。 ② Shrews must eat constantly to survive their fast metabolism. 鼩鼱代谢极快，须不停进食才能存活。
[搭] pygmy shrew 侏儒鼩鼱, shrew-like 似鼩鼱的
[联] mouse 老鼠, rodent 啮齿动物, tiny 极小的, mammal 哺乳动物, snout 尖吻, fast 快速的
[记] 可数：a shrew。Taming of the Shrew 为莎士比亚剧名。动物 shrew 与「泼妇」同形

**vole**
英/美 /vəʊl/
n. 田鼠
[义] A small rodent similar to a mouse, living in fields or near water. 似鼠、栖于田野或水边的小型啮齿动物（water vole 水田鼠，field vole 田野田鼠）。可数。
[例] ① Voles tunneled under the snow all winter. 田鼠整个冬天在雪下挖道。 ② Owls depend on voles as a main food source. 猫头鹰以田鼠为主要食物来源。
[搭] water vole 水田鼠, field vole 田野田鼠, vole population 田鼠数量
[联] mouse 老鼠, rodent 啮齿动物, owl 猫头鹰, field 田野, burrow 挖洞, prey 猎物
[记] 可数：a vole。vole 与 mole（鼹鼠）不同：vole 似鼠，mole 几乎失明、善挖土

**panther**
英/美 /ˈpænθə(r)/
n. 豹；黑豹
[义] A large wild cat, especially a leopard with black fur, or the cougar of the Americas. 大型野猫，尤指黑毛豹，或美洲的美洲狮（black panther 黑豹）。可数。
[例] ① Legends spoke of a panther moving silently through the jungle. 传说黑豹无声穿行丛林。 ② Black panthers are leopards or jaguars with dark colouration. 黑豹是毛色黑化的豹或美洲豹。
[搭] black panther 黑豹, panther stalk 豹潜行, panther eyes 豹眼（喻）
[联] leopard 豹, jaguar 美洲豹, tiger 虎, predator 捕食者, cat 猫, stealthy 潜行的
[记] 可数：a panther。Black Panther 亦为文化/政治符号，语境须辨

**jaguar**
英/美 /ˈdʒæɡjuə(r)/
n. 美洲豹
[义] A large spotted wild cat of Central and South America. 中美洲与南美洲的大型斑纹野猫（jaguar habitat 美洲豹栖息地）。可数。
[例] ① Jaguars hunt along riverbanks in the rainforest. 美洲豹在雨林河岸捕猎。 ② A jaguar dragged its prey into dense cover. 美洲豹把猎物拖进密林。
[搭] jaguar habitat 美洲豹栖息地, spotted jaguar 斑纹美洲豹, black jaguar 黑化美洲豹
[联] leopard 豹, panther 黑豹, tiger 虎, predator 捕食者, rainforest 雨林, cat 猫
[记] 可数：a jaguar。与 leopard 分布不同：jaguar 主美洲

**mantis**
英/美 /ˈmæntɪs/
n. 螳螂
[义] A predatory insect that waits with folded front legs before grabbing prey. 前足折叠待机、再擒获猎物的捕食性昆虫（praying mantis 螳螂）。可数。
[例] ① A mantis waited motionless on the garden stake. 螳螂在花园木桩上一动不动守候。 ② Praying mantises eat other insects in the flower bed. 螳螂在花坛里吃其他昆虫。
[搭] praying mantis 螳螂, mantis strike 螳螂出击, predatory insect 捕食性昆虫
[联] insect 昆虫, predator 捕食者, prey 猎物, garden 花园, still 静止的, grab 擒获
[记] 可数：a mantis。praying mantis 因前足似祈祷姿势得名

**cockroach**
英/美 /ˈkɒkrəʊtʃ/
n. 蟑螂
[义] A flat brown insect that runs fast and is often found in houses. 扁平褐色、跑得快、常见于室内的昆虫（cockroach infestation 蟑螂滋生）。可数。
[例] ① A cockroach scuttled under the fridge when the light came on. 开灯时蟑螂窜到冰箱底下。 ② Warm climates see more cockroach problems in kitchens. 暖区厨房蟑螂问题更常见。
[搭] cockroach infestation 蟑螂滋生, cockroach trap 蟑螂诱捕器, German cockroach 德国小蠊
[联] insect 昆虫, pest 害虫, kitchen 厨房, fast 快速的, disgusting 令人厌恶的, nocturnal 夜行的
[记] 可数：a cockroach。口语常简称 roach

**termite**
英/美 /ˈtɜːmaɪt/
n. 白蚁
[义] A pale soft-bodied insect that lives in large colonies and eats wood. 体色浅、体软、群居、食木的白蚁（termite mound 白蚁丘，termite damage 白蚁危害）。可数。
[例] ① Termites hollowed out the wooden beam over years. 白蚁多年蛀空木梁。 ② A termite mound rose above the savanna grass. 白蚁丘高出草原草丛。
[搭] termite mound 白蚁丘, termite damage 白蚁危害, termite colony 白蚁群
[联] insect 昆虫, ant 蚂蚁, colony 群落, wood 木材, pest 害虫, mound 丘
[记] 可数：a termite。与 ant 社会性类似，但食木为害

**falcon**
英/美 /ˈfɔːlkən/
n. 隼；猎鹰
[义] A bird of prey with long pointed wings, used in the sport of falconry. 翼尖长、用于猎鹰运动的猛禽（peregrine falcon 游隼，falcon swoop 隼俯冲）。可数。
[例] ① A falcon stooped on a pigeon in mid-air. 隼在半空俯冲扑鸽。 ② Peregrine falcons nest on city skyscrapers. 游隼在城市摩天楼筑巢。
[搭] peregrine falcon 游隼, falcon swoop 隼俯冲, falconry 猎鹰运动, bird of prey 猛禽
[联] eagle 鹰, hawk 鹰, bird 鸟, prey 猎物, hunt 狩猎, swift 迅捷的
[记] 可数：a falcon。falconry 为传统猎鹰运动，中东与欧洲历史深厚

**pelican**
英/美 /ˈpelɪkən/
n. 鹈鹕
[义] A large water bird with a long bill and a pouch for catching fish. 长喙、有捕鱼皮囊的大型水禽（pelican pouch 鹈鹕喉囊）。可数。
[例] ① A pelican scooped fish into its bill pouch. 鹈鹕把鱼舀进喉囊。 ② Pelicans glided low over the harbour at dawn. 黎明时鹈鹕低掠港口。
[搭] pelican pouch 鹈鹕喉囊, pelican flock 鹈鹕群, scoop fish 舀鱼
[联] bird 鸟, beak 喙, fish 鱼, ocean 海洋, water 水, flock 鸟群
[记] 可数：a pelican。喉囊可大幅扩张储鱼

**stork**
英/美 /stɔːk/
n. 鹳
[义] A large wading bird with long legs and a long bill, often nesting on rooftops in Europe. 长腿长喙的大型涉水鸟，欧洲常筑巢于屋顶（white stork 白鹳，stork delivery 鹳送婴〔民间说法〕）。可数。
[例] ① A stork stood on one leg in the marsh. 鹳在沼泽里单腿站立。 ② White storks returned to the village nests in spring. 白鹳春天回到村中巢位。
[搭] white stork 白鹳, stork nest 鹳巢, stork delivery 鹳送婴（民间说法）
[联] bird 鸟, long-legged 长腿的, nest 巢, wetland 湿地, wade 涉水, spring 春天
[记] 可数：a stork。西方民间说 stork brings babies 鹳送子

**heron**
英/美 /ˈherən/
n. 鹭
[义] A long-legged wading bird that hunts fish in shallow water. 长腿、在浅水中捕鱼的水禽（grey heron 灰鹭，heron stand 鹭静立）。可数。
[例] ① A heron stood motionless at the river's edge. 鹭在河边一动不动伫立。 ② Herons spear fish with their sharp bills. 鹭用尖喙刺鱼。
[搭] grey heron 灰鹭, heron stand 鹭静立, wading bird 涉水鸟, shallow water 浅水
[联] bird 鸟, fish 鱼, wetland 湿地, long-legged 长腿的, hunt 狩猎, pond 池塘
[记] 可数：a heron。静立待猎为典型行为

**sea lion**
英/美 /ˈsiː laɪən/
n. 海狮
[义] A large seal that can walk on land using its front flippers. 能用前鳍肢在陆地行走的大型海豹（California sea lion 加州海狮，sea lion colony 海狮群）。可数。
[例] ① Sea lions barked on the rocky pier. 海狮在岩石码头吠叫。 ② A sea lion balanced a ball during the show. 海狮在表演中顶球平衡。
[搭] California sea lion 加州海狮, sea lion colony 海狮群, sea lion bark 海狮吠叫
[联] seal 海豹, flipper 鳍肢, ocean 海洋, colony 群落, mammal 哺乳动物, bark 吠叫
[记] 可数：a sea lion。比 seal 更善在陆地移动，常作表演动物

**mole**
英/美 /məʊl/
n. 鼹鼠；痣
[义] A small mammal that lives underground and digs tunnels. 地下生活、挖隧道的小型哺乳动物（mole hill 鼹鼠丘，blind as a mole 瞎如鼹鼠〔喻〕）。可数。亦指皮肤痣——多义。
[例] ① Moles left ridges across the lawn overnight. 鼹鼠一夜在草坪上留下隆脊。 ② A mole's tunnel system can damage garden roots. 鼹鼠隧道网可损害花园根系。
[搭] mole hill 鼹鼠丘, mole tunnel 鼹鼠隧道, blind as a mole 瞎如鼹鼠
[联] burrow 挖洞, underground 地下的, mammal 哺乳动物, dig 挖, lawn 草坪, vole 田鼠
[记] 可数。动物义与 skin mole（痣）同形。mole 几乎失明，靠触觉觅食

**buck**
英/美 /bʌk/
n. 雄鹿；公羊
v. 尥蹶子；反对
[义] The male of deer, rabbit, or goat; to jump with the back arched. 鹿、兔、山羊的雄性；马等尥蹶子（mule buck 骡尥蹶，pass the buck 推卸责任〔习语〕）。可数/动词。亦指美元俚语——多义。
[例] ① A buck stepped out of the trees at dawn. 黎明时一头雄鹿走出树林。 ② The horse bucked and threw the rider. 马尥蹶子把骑手摔下。
[搭] mule buck 骡尥蹶, buck deer 雄鹿, pass the buck 推卸责任, big buck 大雄鹿
[联] doe 雌鹿, deer 鹿, fawn 幼鹿, antler 鹿角, male 雄性的, forest 森林
[记] 可数/动词。quick buck 指「快钱」，与动物无关

**moult**
英 /məʊlt/ 美 /moʊlt/
v. 脱毛；换羽
n. 换羽期
[义] To shed old feathers, hair, or skin so new ones can grow. 脱落旧羽毛或皮肤以便生新（birds moult 鸟换羽，snake moult 蛇蜕皮）。动词/可数名词。美式拼 molt。
[例] ① The snake moulted and left a complete skin behind. 蛇蜕皮，留下完整旧皮。 ② Ducks look shabby while they moult in late summer. 鸭晚夏换羽时显得邋遢。
[搭] birds moult 鸟换羽, snake moult 蛇蜕皮, moulting season 换羽季, shed feathers 脱羽
[联] feather 羽毛, snake 蛇, shed 蜕/脱, bird 鸟, skin 皮肤, season 季节
[记] 动词/名词。英式 moult；美式 molt。与 malt（麦芽）勿混

**woodpecker**
英/美 /ˈwʊdpekə(r)/
n. 啄木鸟
[义] A bird that pecks holes in tree bark to find insects. 啄树皮层找虫的鸟（great spotted woodpecker 大斑啄木鸟，woodpecker drumming 啄木鸟啄木声）。可数。
[例] ① A woodpecker drummed on the dead pine trunk. 啄木鸟在枯松树干上啄击。 ② Woodpeckers can hear insects moving inside the bark. 啄木鸟能听见树皮内昆虫活动。
[搭] great spotted woodpecker 大斑啄木鸟, woodpecker drumming 啄木声, peck holes 啄洞
[联] bird 鸟, peck 啄, tree 树, insect 昆虫, beak 喙, forest 森林
[记] 可数：a woodpecker。啄击声用于标记领地

**salamander**
英/美 /ˈsæləmændə(r)/
n. 蝾螈
[义] An amphibian like a lizard that lives near water and has a long tail. 似蜥蜴、近水生活、长尾的两栖动物（fire salamander 火蝾螈）。可数。
[例] ① A salamander hid under the damp log. 蝾螈躲在潮湿原木下。 ② Salamanders are sensitive to pollution in streams. 蝾螈对溪流污染很敏感。
[搭] fire salamander 火蝾螈, stream salamander 溪蝾螈, amphibian habitat 两栖动物栖息地
[联] amphibian 两栖动物, newt 蝾螈（较小）, frog 蛙, lizard 蜥蜴, tail 尾巴, wet 潮湿的
[记] 可数：a salamander。与 newt 近缘，newt 通常更小

**newt**
英/美 /njuːt/
n. 蝾螈（小种）
[义] A small amphibian with a long tail, related to the salamander. 长尾小型两栖动物，与蝾螈近缘（smooth newt 光滑蝾螈）。可数。
[例] ① A newt swam slowly in the garden pond. 一只小蝾螈在花园池塘里慢游。 ② Newts return to ponds to breed each spring. 小蝾螈每年春天回塘繁殖。
[搭] smooth newt 光滑蝾螈, pond newt 池栖蝾螈, newt larva 蝾螈幼体
[联] salamander 蝾螈, amphibian 两栖动物, frog 蛙, pond 池塘, tail 尾巴, small 小的
[记] 可数：a newt。英式 pond 常见物种；与 salamander 常并用

**cobra**
英/美 /ˈkəʊbrə/
n. 眼镜蛇
[义] A poisonous snake that can spread the skin of its neck into a hood. 可将颈皮展成兜帽的毒蛇（king cobra 眼镜王蛇，cobra hood 眼镜蛇耸颈兜）。可数。
[例] ① The cobra raised its hood and hissed. 眼镜蛇竖起兜帽嘶嘶发声。 ② Snake charmers once used cobras in street performances. 耍蛇人曾在街头表演中使用眼镜蛇。
[搭] king cobra 眼镜王蛇, cobra hood 眼镜蛇耸颈兜, spitting cobra 喷毒眼镜蛇
[联] snake 蛇, venom 毒液, reptile 爬行动物, poisonous 有毒的, hiss 嘶嘶声, hood 兜帽
[记] 可数：a cobra。印度次大陆文化意象强，但勿浪漫化街头耍蛇

**python**
英/美 /ˈpaɪθən/
n. 蟒蛇
[义] A large non-venomous snake that kills prey by squeezing. 大型无毒蛇，以缠绕勒杀猎物（Burmese python 缅甸蟒，python coil 蟒蛇盘绕）。可数。
[例] ① A python lay coiled in the sun after feeding. 蟒蛇进食后在阳光下盘卧。 ② Pythons swallow prey whole. 蟒蛇整吞猎物。
[搭] Burmese python 缅甸蟒, python coil 蟒蛇盘绕, constrictor 蟒（同义）, swallow whole 整吞
[联] snake 蛇, reptile 爬行动物, constrict 勒紧, prey 猎物, large 巨大的, non-venomous 无毒的
[记] 可数：a python。Python 亦为编程语言名，语境消歧

**scorpion**
英/美 /ˈskɔːpiən/
n. 蝎子
[义] A small creature with pincers and a curved tail that can sting. 有钳、弯尾可蜇刺的小型生物（scorpion sting 蝎蜇，desert scorpion 沙漠蝎）。可数。
[例] ① A scorpion glowed under the UV lamp on the desert trail. 沙漠小径上蝎子在紫外灯下发光。 ② Scorpions hunt insects at night. 蝎子夜间捕虫。
[搭] scorpion sting 蝎蜇, desert scorpion 沙漠蝎, scorpion tail 蝎尾, UV glow 紫外荧光
[联] sting 蜇, desert 沙漠, insect 昆虫, venom 毒液, arachnid 蛛形纲, nocturnal 夜行的
[记] 可数：a scorpion。属蛛形纲，不是 insect

**centipede**
英/美 /ˈsentɪpiːd/
n. 蜈蚣
[义] A long thin creature with many legs, one pair per body segment. 体细长、每体节一对足的多足动物（house centipede 蚰蜒）。可数。
[例] ① A centipede darted across the bathroom floor. 蜈蚣窜过浴室地面。 ② Centipedes are predators that bite small insects. 蜈蚣是捕食小虫的掠食者。
[搭] house centipede 蚰蜒, centipede bite 蜈蚣咬伤, many-legged 多足的
[联] insect 昆虫, millipede 马陆, legs 足, bite 咬, pest 害虫, fast 快速的
[记] 可数：a centipede。centi-（百）+ ped（足）为词源，足数未必正好一百

**hummingbird**
英/美 /ˈhʌmɪŋbɜːd/
n. 蜂鸟
[义] A tiny bird that hovers by beating its wings very fast and drinks nectar. 极小鸟，极速振翅悬停、吸食花蜜（ruby-throated hummingbird 红喉北蜂鸟）。可数。
[例] ① A hummingbird hovered at the red feeder. 蜂鸟在红色喂食器前悬停。 ② Hummingbirds must eat frequently to fuel their flight. 蜂鸟须频繁进食以支撑飞行。
[搭] ruby-throated hummingbird 红喉北蜂鸟, hover at flowers 在花前悬停, beat wings 振翅
[联] bird 鸟, nectar 花蜜, hover 悬停, tiny 极小的, wing 翅膀, flower 花
[记] 可数：a hummingbird。振翅频率极高，唯一可倒飞鸟类之一

**reindeer**
英/美 /ˈreɪndɪə(r)/
n. 驯鹿
（复数 reindeer）
[义] A deer with large antlers, found in cold northern regions and used to pull sleds. 具大角、见于寒冷北方、可拉雪橇的鹿（reindeer herd 驯鹿群，Santa's reindeer 圣诞驯鹿〔文化〕）。单复数同形。
[例] ① Reindeer migrated across the tundra in autumn. 驯鹿秋天穿越苔原迁徙。 ② Sami herders follow reindeer herds. 萨米牧人跟随驯鹿群。
[搭] reindeer herd 驯鹿群, Santa's reindeer 圣诞驯鹿, reindeer sled 驯鹿雪橇, Arctic reindeer 北极驯鹿
[联] deer 鹿, antler 鹿角, Arctic 北极的, herd 兽群, migrate 迁徙, cold 寒冷的
[记] 单复数同形。与 caribou 北美野种常并用；圣诞文化符号

**yak**
英/美 /jæk/
n. 牦牛
[义] A long-haired ox found in the Himalayas and Tibet, used for milk and transport. 喜马拉雅与西藏长毛牛，产奶役用（wild yak 野牦牛，yak milk 牦牛奶）。可数。
[例] ① Yaks grazed on the high plateau grassland. 牦牛在高原草场吃草。 ② Nomads rely on yaks for milk and wool. 游牧民依赖牦牛获取奶与毛。
[搭] wild yak 野牦牛, yak milk 牦牛奶, domestic yak 家养牦牛, Himalayan yak 喜马拉雅牦牛
[联] ox 牛, cattle 牛, horn 角, fur 毛, mountain 山地, herd 兽群
[记] 可数：a yak。yak 亦口语指「闲聊」（动词），不同义

**eel**
英/美 /iːl/
n. 鳗；黄鳝
[义] A long thin fish that looks like a snake and lives in rivers or the sea. 似蛇的长细鱼，栖于河海（freshwater eel 淡水鳗，electric eel 电鳗）。可数。
[例] ① Eels hide in mud during the day. 鳗白天躲在泥里。 ② Fishermen caught eels in the estuary nets. 渔民在河口网中捕到鳗。
[搭] freshwater eel 淡水鳗, electric eel 电鳗, eel migration 鳗迁徙, slippery eel 滑溜的鳗（喻）
[联] fish 鱼, snake 蛇, river 河, ocean 海洋, slippery 滑溜的, long 长的
[记] 可数：an eel。作菜归 4-01；作物种归本章

**crayfish**
英/美 /ˈkreɪfɪʃ/
n. 小龙虾；淡水螯虾
[义] A small freshwater animal like a lobster, with claws. 似龙虾、有钳的小型淡水动物（crayfish trap 捕虾笼）。可数。
[例] ① Crayfish scuttled among stones in the clear creek. 小龙虾在清溪石间疾行。 ② Kids caught crayfish with nets in the stream. 孩子在溪里用网抓小龙虾。
[搭] crayfish trap 捕虾笼, freshwater crayfish 淡水小龙虾, crayfish boil 小龙虾宴（食材→4-01）
[联] lobster 龙虾, crab 蟹, claw 钳, freshwater 淡水的, river 河, shell 壳
[记] 可数：a crayfish。美式亦指 crawfish。作菜归 4-01

**trout**
英/美 /traʊt/
n. 鳟鱼
[义] A freshwater fish valued for sport fishing and food, often with speckled skin. 淡水鱼，常具斑点，宜垂钓与食用（rainbow trout 虹鳟，trout stream 鳟鱼溪）。可数/不可数。
[例] ① Trout rose to the fly on the mountain stream. 鳟鱼在山溪里扑向假蝇。 ② The lake is stocked with rainbow trout each year. 该湖每年放养虹鳟。
[搭] rainbow trout 虹鳟, trout stream 鳟鱼溪, fly fishing 飞蝇钓, speckled trout 斑点鳟
[联] fish 鱼, salmon 鲑鱼, river 河, freshwater 淡水的, fishing 钓鱼, stream 溪流
[记] 可数/不可数。作鱼→本章；作菜→4-01

n. 偷猎者；非法取用者
[义] A person who illegally hunts or catches animals on protected land. 在保护地非法猎捕动物的人（rhino poacher 盗猎犀牛者，poacher trap 偷猎陷阱）。可数。
[例] ① Rangers arrested poachers near the reserve boundary. 护林员在保护区边界附近逮捕偷猎者。 ② Poachers target elephants and rhinos for ivory and horns. 偷猎者以象牙和犀角为目标猎杀象与犀牛。
[搭] rhino poacher 盗猎犀牛者, anti-poaching unit 反偷猎队, poacher trap 偷猎陷阱, wildlife poacher 盗猎野生动物者
[联] poach 偷猎, hunt 狩猎, illegal 非法的, rhino 犀牛, elephant 象, ranger 护林员
[记] 可数：a poacher。poacher 亦指「偷拿他人创意/资源者」，比喻义

**leopard seal**
英/美 /ˈlepəd siːl/
n. 豹海豹
[义] A large Antarctic seal with spotted fur and a powerful bite. 南极大型海豹，毛有斑点、咬合力强（leopard seal hunt 豹海豹捕猎）。可数。
[例] ① A leopard seal surfaced near the research ice floe. 豹海豹在研究站浮冰旁浮出水面。 ② Leopard seals prey on penguins and other seals. 豹海豹捕食企鹅及其他海豹。
[搭] leopard seal hunt 豹海豹捕猎, Antarctic leopard seal 南极豹海豹, spotted fur 斑点毛皮
[联] seal 海豹, penguin 企鹅, predator 捕食者, Antarctic 南极的, ocean 海洋, spotted 有斑点的
[记] 可数：a leopard seal。虽名 leopard，是海豹不是豹

**weasel**
英/美 /ˈwiːzl/
n. 鼬；黄鼠狼
[义] A small thin mammal that hunts mice and rabbits. 体细小型哺乳动物，猎鼠兔（stoat/weasel 白鼬〔近缘〕）。可数。
[例] ① A weasel slipped through the hedge gap. 黄鼠狼溜过树篱缝隙。 ② Weasels have long bodies and short legs. 鼬体长腿短。
[搭] stoat 白鼬, weasel family 鼬科, weasel hunt 鼬捕猎
[联] mammal 哺乳动物, predator 捕食者, mouse 老鼠, rabbit 兔, slender 细长的, fur 毛皮
[记] 可数：a weasel。weasel out of 指「耍赖逃脱」，比喻义

**ferret**
英/美 /ˈferɪt/
n. 雪貂；白鼬
v. 搜寻；查出
[义] A small domesticated carnivore used for hunting rabbits or kept as a pet. 小型驯养食肉动物，用于猎兔或作宠物（ferret out 搜出〔喻〕）。可数/动词。
[例] ① Ferrets were once used to drive rabbits from burrows. 雪貂曾用于把兔从洞穴赶出。 ② She keeps two ferrets as unusual pets. 她养两只雪貂当另类宠物。
[搭] ferret out 搜出, pet ferret 宠物雪貂, ferret hunting 雪貂猎兔
[联] weasel 鼬, mammal 哺乳动物, pet 宠物, rabbit 兔, burrow 洞穴, hunt 狩猎
[记] 可数/动词。动词 ferret out 指「搜出、查明」，与动物猎性相关

**lynx**
英/美 /lɪŋks/
n. 猞猁；山猫
[义] A wild cat with tufted ears and spotted fur, living in northern forests. 耳有簇毛、毛有斑点的野猫，栖于北方森林（Eurasian lynx 欧亚猞猁）。可数。
[例] ① Lynx tracks appeared in the snow after the storm. 暴风雪后雪地上出现猞猁足迹。 ② Lynx populations recovered where reforestation expanded. 再造林扩大处猞猁数量有所恢复。
[搭] Eurasian lynx 欧亚猞猁, lynx tracks 猞猁足迹, tufted ears 簇耳
[联] cat 猫, predator 捕食者, forest 森林, wild 野生的, spotted 有斑点的, snow 雪
[记] 可数：a lynx。lynx-eyed 指「目光锐利的」，比喻义

**bobcat**
英/美 /ˈbɒbkæt/
n. 短尾猫；美洲山猫
[义] A wild cat of North America with a short tail and tufted ears. 北美野猫，尾短、耳有簇毛（bobcat territory 短尾猫领地）。可数。
[例] ① A bobcat crossed the trail at dawn. 短尾猫黎明时穿过小径。 ② Bobcats adapt well to suburban woodland edges. 短尾猫很适应郊区林缘。
[搭] bobcat territory 短尾猫领地, North American bobcat 北美短尾猫, short tail 短尾
[联] lynx 猞猁, cat 猫, wild 野生的, predator 捕食者, forest 森林, North America 北美
[记] 可数：a bobcat。因短尾（bob tail）得名

**ox**
英/美 /ɒks/
n. 阉牛；牛
（复数 oxen /ˈɒksn/）
[义] A castrated bull used for pulling carts and ploughs; cattle in general (old use). 用于拉车犁的阉公牛；旧用法泛指牛（pair of oxen 一对牛，ox cart 牛车）。可数。复数 oxen。
[例] ① Oxen pulled the wooden plough through the field. 牛拉着木犁过田。 ② Farmers still use oxen in some remote villages. 一些偏远村庄农民仍用牛耕作。
[搭] pair of oxen 一对牛, ox cart 牛车, ox plough 牛犁, draft ox 役牛
[联] cattle 牛, cow 母牛, bull 公牛, yak 牦牛, farm 农场, plough 犁
[记] 复数 oxen。ox 特指阉役牛；cow/bull 分性别

**manta ray**
英/美 /ˈmæntə reɪ/
n. 蝠鲼；魔鬼鱼
[义] A very large flat sea fish with wing-like fins that swims by gliding. 翼状鳍、滑翔游动的大型扁海鱼（giant manta ray 巨型蝠鲼）。可数。
[例] ① A manta ray glided past the divers like a flying carpet. 蝠鲼像飞毯般从潜水员旁滑过。 ② Manta rays feed on plankton near the surface. 蝠鲼在近水面滤食浮游生物。
[搭] giant manta ray 巨型蝠鲼, manta glide 蝠鲼滑翔, wing-like fins 翼状鳍
[联] ray 鳐鱼, ocean 海洋, fish 鱼, fin 鳍, dive 潜水, giant 巨大的
[记] 可数：a manta ray。亦称 devil ray；与 stingray 不同，通常无尾刺

**barracuda**
英/美 /ˌbærəˈkjuːdə/
n. 梭鱼
[义] A long aggressive tropical sea fish with sharp teeth. 热带长身、攻击性强、利齿的海鱼（barracuda attack 梭鱼袭击，Great Barracuda 大梭鱼）。可数。
[例] ① A barracuda darted from the reef shadow. 梭鱼从礁影中猛冲而出。 ② Divers keep distance from barracudas near wrecks. 潜水员在沉船附近与梭鱼保持距离。
[搭] barracuda attack 梭鱼袭击, Great Barracuda 大梭鱼, sharp teeth 利齿, reef predator 礁区捕食者
[联] fish 鱼, shark 鲨鱼, predator 捕食者, ocean 海洋, teeth 牙齿, reef 礁石
[记] 可数：a barracuda。热带潜水与钓鱼新闻常见

**millipede**
英/美 /ˈmɪlɪpiːd/
n. 马陆
[义] A long creature with many legs, two pairs on most body segments. 体长、多数体节各有两对足的多足动物（garden millipede 园马陆）。可数。
[例] ① A millipede curled up when touched. 马陆被碰时蜷成一圈。 ② Millipedes feed on decaying leaves in the forest. 马陆在林中吃腐叶。
[搭] garden millipede 园马陆, millipede curl 马陆蜷曲, many legs 多足
[联] centipede 蜈蚣, insect 昆虫, legs 足, curl 蜷缩, forest 森林, decay 腐烂
[记] 可数：a millipede。milli-（千）+ ped（足）为词源；与 centipede（每节一对足）不同

**caribou**
英/美 /ˈkærɪbuː/
n. 北美驯鹿
（复数 caribou）
[义] A large deer of Arctic and subarctic North America; the wild form of reindeer. 北极与亚北极北美大型鹿；驯鹿的野生物种（caribou migration 驯鹿迁徙，caribou herd 驯鹿群）。单复数同形。
[例] ① Caribou crossed the frozen tundra in long lines. 驯鹿长队穿越冻原。 ② Indigenous hunters followed the caribou herds. 原住民猎人跟随驯鹿群。
[搭] caribou migration 驯鹿迁徙, caribou herd 驯鹿群, Arctic caribou 北极驯鹿
[联] reindeer 驯鹿, deer 鹿, Arctic 北极的, herd 兽群, migrate 迁徙, antler 鹿角
[记] 单复数同形。北美常用 caribou；欧亚驯养种多称 reindeer

**kingfisher**
英/美 /ˈkɪŋfɪʃə(r)/
n. 翠鸟
[义] A brightly coloured bird that dives into water to catch fish. 羽色鲜艳、潜入水中捕鱼的鸟（common kingfisher 普通翠鸟，kingfisher dive 翠鸟潜水）。可数。
[例] ① A kingfisher flashed blue along the riverbank. 翠鸟沿河岸闪过一道蓝光。 ② Kingfishers nest in burrows dug into riverbanks. 翠鸟在河岸洞穴中筑巢。
[搭] common kingfisher 普通翠鸟, kingfisher dive 翠鸟潜水, riverbank nest 河岸巢穴
[联] bird 鸟, fish 鱼, dive 潜水, river 河, colourful 鲜艳的, beak 喙
[记] 可数：a kingfisher。普通翠鸟橙腹蓝背，极易辨认

**muskrat**
英/美 /ˈmʌskræt/
n. 麝鼠
[义] A medium-sized rodent that lives in wetlands and builds lodges. 栖于湿地、筑巢居的中型啮齿动物（muskrat lodge 麝鼠巢）。可数。
[例] ① Muskrats swam between reeds at dusk. 黄昏时麝鼠在芦苇间游动。 ② A muskrat lodge rose from the marsh pool. 麝鼠巢露出沼泽水塘。
[搭] muskrat lodge 麝鼠巢, wetland rodent 湿地啮齿动物, marsh 沼泽
[联] rodent 啮齿动物, wetland 湿地, beaver 河狸, swim 游泳, fur 毛皮, lodge 巢
[记] 可数：a muskrat。名带 rat 但为独立物种，非家鼠

**mink**
英/美 /mɪŋk/
n. 水貂
[义] A small carnivorous mammal with valuable fur, often farmed. 毛皮珍贵、常养殖的小型食肉哺乳动物（American mink 美洲水貂，mink coat 貂皮大衣〔制品〕）。可数。
[例] ① A wild mink hunted along the stream bank. 野生水貂沿溪岸捕猎。 ② Escaped farm minks became invasive in some regions. 逃逸的养殖水貂在部分地区成入侵种。
[搭] American mink 美洲水貂, mink fur 貂皮, farmed mink 养殖水貂, wild mink 野生水貂
[联] fur 毛皮, mammal 哺乳动物, weasel 鼬, predator 捕食者, river 河, invasive 入侵的
[记] 可数：a mink。mink coat 指制品，涉动物福利议题

**grasshopper**
英/美 /ˈɡrɑːshɒpə(r)/
n. 蚱蜢；蝗虫
[义] An insect with long back legs for jumping, that eats grass and plants. 后足长、善跳、食草植物的昆虫（locust swarm 蝗灾〔近缘种〕）。可数。
[例] ① Grasshoppers leapt from the dry meadow grass. 蚱蜢从干草甸中跃起。 ② Farmers watched for grasshopper damage to crops. 农民留意蚱蜢对作物的损害。
[搭] grasshopper jump 蚱蜢跳跃, locust swarm 蝗灾, crop damage 作物损害
[联] insect 昆虫, locust 蝗虫, jump 跳, field 田野, crop 作物, chirp 唧唧叫
[记] 可数：a grasshopper。与 locust 近缘；locust 常指群飞成灾种类

**locust**
英/美 /ˈləʊkəst/
n. 蝗虫
[义] A large grasshopper that migrates in huge swarms and destroys crops. 大规模迁飞、毁坏庄稼的大型蝗（locust swarm 蝗群，locust plague 蝗灾）。可数。
[例] ① Locust swarms darkened the sky over the fields. 蝗群使田野上空天色变暗。 ② Governments sprayed pesticides to control locusts. 政府喷药控制蝗虫。
[搭] locust swarm 蝗群, locust plague 蝗灾, crop destruction 毁坏庄稼
[联] grasshopper 蚱蜢, insect 昆虫, swarm 群, plague 灾害, crop 作物, migrate 迁徙
[记] 可数：a locust。新闻与粮食安全议题高频词

**goldfish**
英/美 /ˈɡəʊldfɪʃ/
n. 金鱼
（复数 goldfish）
[义] A small orange or red fish often kept in bowls or ponds. 常养于缸或池的橙红小型鱼（pet goldfish 宠物金鱼，goldfish bowl 金鱼缸）。单复数同形。
[例] ① Goldfish swam lazily in the garden pond. 金鱼在花园池塘里悠游。 ② Children won a goldfish at the fair. 孩子在集市上赢了一条金鱼。
[搭] pet goldfish 宠物金鱼, goldfish bowl 金鱼缸, pond goldfish 池养金鱼
[联] fish 鱼, pet 宠物, aquarium 水族馆, orange 橙色的, pond 池塘, carp 鲤鱼
[记] 单复数同形：one goldfish, two goldfish。由鲤科驯化而来

**carp**
英/美 /kɑːp/
n. 鲤鱼
[义] A large freshwater fish common in rivers and lakes, often farmed for food. 河湖中常见、常养殖食用的大型淡水鱼（common carp 鲤鱼）。可数/不可数。
[例] ① Carp stirred mud at the bottom of the lake. 鲤鱼在湖底搅起淤泥。 ② Anglers baited hooks for carp near the reeds. 钓客在芦苇旁用饵钓鲤。
[搭] common carp 鲤鱼, carp fishing 钓鲤, freshwater carp 淡水鲤
[联] fish 鱼, goldfish 金鱼, river 河, lake 湖, freshwater 淡水的, farm 养殖
[记] 可数/不可数。作鱼→本章；作菜→4-01

**catfish**
英/美 /ˈkætfɪʃ/
n. 鲶鱼
[义] A freshwater fish with whisker-like barbels around its mouth. 淡水鱼，口旁有触须状须（channel catfish 沟鲶，catfish whiskers 鲶鱼触须）。可数/不可数。
[例] ① Catfish fed on the river bottom at night. 鲶鱼夜间在河底觅食。 ② Southern cooks fry catfish in cornmeal batter. 南方厨师用玉米面糊炸鲶鱼。
[搭] channel catfish 沟鲶, catfish whiskers 鲶鱼触须, bottom feeder 底栖鱼
[联] fish 鱼, freshwater 淡水的, whisker 触须, river 河, bottom 底部, farm 养殖
[记] 可数/不可数。作鱼→本章；fried catfish 等菜肴→4-01

**plankton**
英/美 /ˈplæŋktən/
n. 浮游生物
[义] Very small plants and animals that drift in sea or freshwater. 在海或淡水中漂流的极小微细生物（marine plankton 海洋浮游生物，plankton bloom 浮游生物水华）。不可数。
[例] ① Whales filter plankton through baleen plates. 鲸用鲸须板滤食浮游生物。 ② A plankton bloom turned the bay green. 浮游生物水华使海湾变绿。
[搭] marine plankton 海洋浮游生物, plankton bloom 浮游生物水华, drift in water 水中漂流
[联] whale 鲸, ocean 海洋, krill 磷虾, food chain 食物链, microscopic 微细的, bloom 水华
[记] 不可数。phytoplankton 指植物性；zooplankton 指动物性

**krill**
英/美 /krɪl/
n. 磷虾
[义] Small shrimp-like creatures eaten in huge numbers by whales and penguins. 鲸与企鹅大量摄食的小型虾状生物（Antarctic krill 南极磷虾，krill swarm 磷虾群）。不可数/复数。
[例] ① Baleen whales gulp swarms of krill near the surface. 须鲸在近水面吞食磷虾群。 ② Krill support much of the Antarctic food web. 磷虾支撑南极大部分食物网。
[搭] Antarctic krill 南极磷虾, krill swarm 磷虾群, whale food 鲸的食物
[联] plankton 浮游生物, whale 鲸, penguin 企鹅, Antarctic 南极的, ocean 海洋, shrimp 虾
[记] 不可数/复数。南极生态与渔业新闻常见

**barnacle**
英/美 /ˈbɑːnəkl/
n. 藤壶
[义] A small sea animal with a hard shell that attaches firmly to rocks, ships, and whales. 硬壳、固着于岩石、船体与鲸体的小型海洋动物（barnacle encrustation 藤壶附着层）。可数。
[例] ① Barnacles covered the hull below the waterline. 水线以下船壳布满藤壶。 ② Workers scraped barnacles off the pier pilings. 工人刮除码头桩上的藤壶。
[搭] barnacle encrustation 藤壶附着层, scrape barnacles 刮藤壶, attach to hull 附着船体
[联] ocean 海洋, shell 壳, whale 鲸, ship 船, crust 硬壳层, marine 海洋的
[记] 可数：a barnacle。barnacle goose 为「雁」非藤壶，勿混

**anemone**
英/美 /əˈneməni/
n. 海葵；银莲花
[义] A sea animal with a soft body and tentacles, related to jellyfish; also a flowering plant. 体软、有触手、与水母同纲的海洋动物；亦指银莲花（sea anemone 海葵，anemone tentacles 海葵触手）。可数。植物义归植物章。
[例] ① A sea anemone waved its tentacles in the current. 海葵在流中摆动触手。 ② Clownfish live among anemone tentacles for protection. 小丑鱼在海葵触手间寻求保护。
[搭] sea anemone 海葵, anemone tentacles 海葵触手, clownfish partner 与小丑鱼共生
[联] jellyfish 水母, ocean 海洋, tentacle 触手, reef 礁石, marine 海洋的, clownfish 小丑鱼
[记] 可数。sea anemone 为动物；garden anemone 为花。勿与 flower 章混淆

**wolverine**
英/美 /ˈwʊlvəriːn/
n. 狼獾；貂熊
[义] A strong stocky carnivore of northern forests, known for ferocity. 北方森林强壮敦实的食肉兽，以凶猛著称（wolverine track 狼獾足迹）。可数。
[例] ① A wolverine crossed the snowy pass alone. 一只狼獾独自穿过雪隘。 ② Wolverines can drive bears off a kill. 狼獾能把熊从猎物边赶走。
[搭] wolverine track 狼獾足迹, northern forest 北方森林, fierce carnivore 凶猛食肉兽
[联] bear 熊, predator 捕食者, forest 森林, mammal 哺乳动物, fierce 凶猛的, snow 雪
[记] 可数：a wolverine。与 wolf 不同种；漫威 Wolverine 为文化借用

**tadpole**
英/美 /ˈtædpəʊl/
n. 蝌蚪
[义] The larva of a frog or toad, living in water with a tail and no legs at first. 蛙或蟾的幼体，初时居水、有尾无足（tadpole stage 蝌蚪期，tadpole tail 蝌蚪尾）。可数。
[例] ① Tadpoles swarmed in the shallow pond. 蝌蚪在浅塘里成群游动。 ② Legs slowly formed as the tadpole matured. 蝌蚪成熟过程中腿慢慢长出。
[搭] tadpole stage 蝌蚪期, tadpole tail 蝌蚪尾, frog larva 蛙幼体
[联] frog 蛙, toad 蟾蜍, larva 幼体, pond 池塘, tail 尾巴, metamorphosis 变态
[记] 可数：a tadpole。亦作 polliwog（美式旧称）

**larva**
英/美 /ˈlɑːvə/
n. 幼虫；幼体
（复数 larvae /ˈlɑːviː/）
[义] The immature form of an insect or other animal that changes shape when adult. 昆虫或其他动物在成体前形态不同的未成熟阶段（caterpillar larva 毛虫幼体，mosquito larva 蚊幼虫）。可数。复数 larvae。
[例] ① Mosquito larvae hatched in standing water. 蚊幼虫在积水中孵化。 ② The beetle larva pupated underground. 甲虫幼虫在地下化蛹。
[搭] mosquito larva 蚊幼虫, insect larva 昆虫幼虫, larval stage 幼虫期
[联] caterpillar 毛虫, insect 昆虫, tadpole 蝌蚪, pupa 蛹, hatch 孵化, metamorphosis 变态
[记] 复数 larvae。larval 形容词。与 lava（熔岩）勿混

**starling**
英/美 /ˈstɑːlɪŋ/
n. 椋鸟
[义] A dark bird with speckled feathers, often seen in large flocks. 羽色深、有斑点的鸟，常大群出现（European starling 欧椋鸟，murmuration 椋鸟群飞〔现象〕）。可数。
