[例] ① Starlings swirled in a huge flock at dusk. 黄昏时椋鸟旋成大群。 ② A starling mimicked car alarms from the rooftop. 椋鸟在屋顶模仿汽车警报声。
[搭] European starling 欧椋鸟, starling flock 椋鸟群, murmuration 群飞现象
[联] bird 鸟, flock 鸟群, mimic 模仿, speckled 有斑点的, urban 城市的, chirp 啁啾
[记] 可数：a starling。murmuration 指椋鸟群飞壮观景象

**swallow**
英/美 /ˈswɒləʊ/
n. 燕子
v. 吞咽
[义] A small bird with long pointed wings that catches insects in flight. 翼尖长、飞行中捕虫的小鸟（barn swallow 家燕，swallow nest 燕巢）。可数/动词。动词 swallow 指「吞咽」——多义。
[例] ① Swallows returned to the same nest under the eaves. 燕子回到檐下旧巢。 ② A swallow darted over the field after flies. 燕子在田野上疾飞追蝇。
[搭] barn swallow 家燕, swallow nest 燕巢, swallow migration 燕子迁徙
[联] bird 鸟, fly 飞, nest 巢, spring 春天, insect 昆虫, migration 迁徙
[记] 可数/动词。动物义与 swallow food（吞咽）同形

**hermit crab**
英/美 /ˈhɜːmɪt kræb/
n. 寄居蟹
[义] A crab that lives in empty shells left by other animals. 栖居于其他动物遗壳中的蟹（hermit crab shell 寄居蟹壳，change shells 换壳）。可数。
[例] ① A hermit crab peeked out of a borrowed shell. 寄居蟹从借来的壳里探出头。 ② As it grows, a hermit crab seeks a larger shell. 寄居蟹长大须找更大壳。
[搭] hermit crab shell 寄居蟹壳, change shells 换壳, tide pool 潮池
[联] crab 蟹, shell 壳, ocean 海洋, beach 海滩, tide pool 潮池, grow 生长
[记] 可数：a hermit crab。hermit 本义「隐士」，因借壳独居得名

**clownfish**
英/美 /ˈklaʊnfɪʃ/
n. 小丑鱼
[义] A small brightly coloured sea fish that often lives among sea anemones. 常栖于海葵中的小型艳丽海鱼（clownfish and anemone 小丑鱼与海葵共生）。可数。
[例] ① Clownfish darted among the waving anemone tentacles. 小丑鱼在摆动的海葵触手间穿梭。 ② A clownfish pair defended their anemone home. 一对小丑鱼守护它们的海葵家园。
[搭] clownfish and anemone 小丑鱼与海葵共生, anemone home 海葵家园, reef fish 礁鱼
[联] anemone 海葵, fish 鱼, reef 礁石, ocean 海洋, colourful 艳丽的, symbiosis 共生
[记] 可数：a clownfish。因橙白条纹似小丑妆得名；《海底总动员》提升知名度

**pupa**
英/美 /ˈpjuːpə/
n. 蛹
（复数 pupae /ˈpjuːpiː/）
[义] An insect in the stage between larva and adult, often in a cocoon. 介于幼虫与成虫之间、常处茧中的昆虫阶段（butterfly pupa 蝶蛹，pupa stage 蛹期）。可数。复数 pupae。
[例] ① The caterpillar formed a pupa on the twig. 毛虫在枝条上化蛹。 ② Inside the pupa, the insect's body reorganizes completely. 蛹内昆虫身体完全重组。
[搭] butterfly pupa 蝶蛹, pupa stage 蛹期, cocoon 茧, emerge as adult 羽化成虫
[联] larva 幼虫, caterpillar 毛虫, butterfly 蝴蝶, cocoon 茧, metamorphosis 变态, insect 昆虫
[记] 复数 pupae。chrysalis 专指蝶蛹，略诗意

**metamorphosis**
英/美 /ˌmetəˈmɔːfəsɪs/
n. 变态；蜕变
（复数 metamorphoses /-siːz/）
[义] The process by which an animal's body changes form as it grows to adult, as in insects and frogs. 动物成长过程中身体变形为成体的过程，如昆虫与蛙（insect metamorphosis 昆虫变态，complete metamorphosis 完全变态）。可数/不可数。
[例] ① Frogs undergo metamorphosis from tadpole to adult. 蛙经历从蝌蚪到成体的变态。 ② Complete metamorphosis includes egg, larva, pupa, and adult. 完全变态包括卵、幼虫、蛹、成虫。
[源] 源自希腊语 *metamorphoun*（变形），*meta-*（改变）+ *morphē*（形态）。
[搭] insect metamorphosis 昆虫变态, complete metamorphosis 完全变态, frog metamorphosis 蛙变态
[联] larva 幼虫, pupa 蛹, tadpole 蝌蚪, butterfly 蝴蝶, transform 转变, growth 生长
[记] 可数/不可数。卡夫卡《变形记》标题即用此词；比喻义「巨变」常见

**ray**
英/美 /reɪ/
n. 鳐鱼；射线
[义] A flat sea fish with a long tail, related to sharks. 扁身、长尾、与鲨同类的海鱼（stingray 刺魟，manta ray 蝠鲼）。可数。亦指光线——多义。
[例] ① A ray glided over the sandy seabed. 一条鳐鱼滑过沙质海床。 ② Divers watched rays feed near the wreck. 潜水员看鳐鱼在沉船旁觅食。
[搭] stingray 刺魟, manta ray 蝠鲼, ray tail 鳐鱼尾, flat fish 扁鱼
[联] shark 鲨鱼, ocean 海洋, fish 鱼, sting 蜇, fin 鳍, seabed 海床
[记] 可数：a ray。manta ray、stingray 已单独立条；ray 作总称

**food chain**
英/美 /ˈfuːd tʃeɪn/
n. 食物链
[义] A series of living things in which each feeds on the one below it. 生物彼此取食形成逐级关联的系列（top of the food chain 食物链顶端，food chain disruption 食物链破坏）。可数。
[例] ① Owls sit high on the local food chain. 猫头鹰位居当地食物链上层。 ② Pollution can disrupt the entire food chain. 污染可扰乱整条食物链。
[搭] top of the food chain 食物链顶端, food chain disruption 食物链破坏, marine food chain 海洋食物链
[联] predator 捕食者, prey 猎物, ecosystem 生态系统, plankton 浮游生物, hunt 狩猎, species 物种
[记] 可数。food web（食物网）更精确，但 food chain 更口语

**venomous**
英/美 /ˈvenəməs/
adj. 有毒的；恶毒的
[义] Producing venom that is injected by a bite or sting. 能产生毒液并经咬蜇注入的（venomous snake 毒蛇，venomous spider 毒蜘蛛）。只作形容词。亦可喻「恶毒」。
[例] ① Only a few snakes in this region are venomous. 该地区只有少数蛇有毒。 ② Venomous spiders hide in dry rocky crevices. 毒蜘蛛躲在干燥岩缝中。
[搭] venomous snake 毒蛇, venomous spider 毒蜘蛛, venomous bite 毒咬
[联] venom 毒液, poison 毒, snake 蛇, sting 蜇, bite 咬, dangerous 危险的
[记] 只作形容词。venomous（注入毒液）与 poisonous（误食中毒）有区别

**gull**
英/美 /ɡʌl/
n. 海鸥
[义] A common grey and white sea bird with long wings, often seen at the coast. 常见灰白海鸟、翼长，多见于海岸（herring gull 银鸥，seagull 海鸥〔口语〕）。可数。
[例] ① Gulls circled above the fishing boats. 海鸥在渔船上方盘旋。 ② A gull snatched chips from the tourist's hand. 海鸥从游客手中抢走薯条。
[搭] herring gull 银鸥, seagull 海鸥, coastal bird 海岸鸟类, flock of gulls 海鸥群
[联] bird 鸟, ocean 海洋, beach 海滩, coast 海岸, flock 鸟群, wing 翅膀
[记] 可数：a gull。seagull 口语常用；严格说 gull 为学名通称

**robin**
英/美 /ˈrɒbɪn/
n. 知更鸟
[义] A small brown bird with a red or orange breast, common in gardens. 胸腹红橙、常见于花园的小型褐鸟（European robin 欧亚知更鸟，American robin 美洲鸫〔不同种〕）。可数。
[例] ① A robin hopped across the frosty lawn. 知更鸟跳过霜冻草坪。 ② Robins sing from fence posts on winter mornings. 冬日清晨知更鸟在栅栏柱上鸣唱。
[搭] European robin 欧亚知更鸟, American robin 美洲鸫, robin redbreast 红胸知更鸟（诗）
[联] bird 鸟, garden 花园, sing 鸣叫, breast 胸部, winter 冬天, nest 巢
[记] 可数：a robin。英式 robin 与美式 American robin 不同种

**marsupial**
英/美 /mɑːˈsuːpiəl/
n. 有袋动物
adj. 有袋的
[义] A mammal whose young are born undeveloped and continue growing in the mother's pouch. 幼崽未充分发育即出生、在母兽育儿袋中继续生长的哺乳动物（Australian marsupials 澳大利亚有袋动物，marsupial pouch 育儿袋）。可数/形容词。
[例] ① Kangaroos and koalas are marsupials. 袋鼠与考拉是有袋动物。 ② Marsupial joeys stay in the pouch for months. 有袋类幼崽在袋中待数月。
[搭] Australian marsupials 澳大利亚有袋动物, marsupial pouch 育儿袋, marsupial mammal 有袋哺乳动物
[联] kangaroo 袋鼠, koala 考拉, pouch 育儿袋, mammal 哺乳动物, Australia 澳大利亚, joey 幼崽
[记] 可数/形容词。源自拉丁语 marsupium（育儿袋）

**blowhole**
英/美 /ˈbləʊhəʊl/
n. 呼吸孔；喷水孔
[义] The hole on top of a whale's head through which it breathes. 鲸头顶用于呼吸的孔（whale blowhole 鲸呼吸孔，blowhole spout 喷水）。可数。
[例] ① The whale surfaced and exhaled through its blowhole. 鲸浮出水面，经呼吸孔呼气。 ② Children watched the blowhole spout on the boat tour. 孩子在船上观光时看鲸喷水。
[搭] whale blowhole 鲸呼吸孔, blowhole spout 喷水, surface to breathe 浮出水面呼吸
[联] whale 鲸, dolphin 海豚, breathe 呼吸, ocean 海洋, spout 喷水, mammal 哺乳动物
[记] 可数：a blowhole。亦指海岸岩石喷潮孔，不同义

**anglerfish**
英/美 /ˈæŋɡləfɪʃ/
n. 鮟鱇鱼；琵琶鱼
[义] A deep-sea fish with a glowing lure on its head to attract prey. 深海鱼，头顶有发光诱饵引猎物（deep-sea anglerfish 深海鮟鱇）。可数。
[例] ① The anglerfish dangled its lure in the dark water. 鮟鱇在暗水中晃动着诱饵。 ② Female anglerfish are far larger than males. 雌性鮟鱇远大于雄性。
[搭] deep-sea anglerfish 深海鮟鱇, glowing lure 发光诱饵, anglerfish light 鮟鱇诱光
[联] fish 鱼, deep sea 深海, predator 捕食者, lure 诱饵, ocean 海洋, bizarre 奇异的
[记] 可数：an anglerfish。雌雄差异极大，深海纪录片常见

**wombat**
英/美 /ˈwɒmbæt/
n. 袋熊
[义] An Australian marsupial that digs burrows and has cube-shaped droppings. 澳大利亚有袋动物，挖洞而居，粪便呈立方形（common wombat 普通袋熊）。可数。
[例] ① A wombat waddled back to its burrow at dusk. 黄昏时袋熊摇摇摆摆回洞。 ② Wombats can run surprisingly fast when threatened. 受威胁时袋熊跑得惊人地快。
[搭] common wombat 普通袋熊, wombat burrow 袋熊洞, cube-shaped droppings 立方粪便
[联] marsupial 有袋动物, Australia 澳大利亚, burrow 洞穴, koala 考拉, mammal 哺乳动物, nocturnal 夜行的
[记] 可数：a wombat。立方粪便是著名冷知识

**osprey**
英/美 /ˈɒspreɪ/
n. 鱼鹰；鹗
[义] A large bird of prey that catches fish by diving into water. 大型猛禽，俯冲入水捕鱼（osprey nest 鱼鹰巢，fish hawk 鱼鹰〔美〕）。可数。
[例] ① An osprey plunged into the lake and rose with a trout. 鱼鹰俯冲入湖，叼起一条鳟鱼。 ② Ospreys build huge nests on dead trees. 鱼鹰在枯树上筑大巢。
[搭] osprey nest 鱼鹰巢, fish hawk 鱼鹰, diving bird 俯冲捕鱼的鸟
[联] bird 鸟, fish 鱼, predator 捕食者, nest 巢, lake 湖, hawk 鹰
[记] 可数：an osprey。专食鱼类，全球湿地常见

**condor**
英/美 /ˈkɒndɔː(r)/
n. 秃鹫；神鹰
[义] A very large vulture with a bald head, found in the Americas. 美洲巨型秃鹫，头秃（Andean condor 安第斯神鹰，California condor 加州秃鹫）。可数。
[例] ① The Andean condor soared on thermal currents. 安第斯神鹰借热气流翱翔。 ② Conservationists released captive-bred condors into the wild. 保育人员将人工繁育的神鹰放归野外。
[搭] Andean condor 安第斯神鹰, California condor 加州秃鹫, endangered condor 濒危神鹰
[联] vulture 秃鹫, bird 鸟, soar 翱翔, endangered 濒危的, mountain 山地, wing 翅膀
[记] 可数：a condor。翼展可达三米以上

**albatross**
英/美 /ˈælbətrɒs/
n. 信天翁
[义] A very large seabird with long narrow wings, able to glide for hours. 翼长窄、可滑翔数小时的大型海鸟（wandering albatross 漂泊信天翁）。可数。
[例] ① Albatrosses follow fishing boats across the Southern Ocean. 信天翁跟随渔船穿越南大洋。 ② An albatross can sleep while gliding over the waves. 信天翁可在浪上滑翔时睡眠。
[搭] wandering albatross 漂泊信天翁, seabird 海鸟, albatross wingspan 信天翁翼展
[联] bird 鸟, ocean 海洋, wing 翅膀, glide 滑翔, seabird 海鸟, migrate 迁徙
[记] 可数：an albatross。习语 albatross around one's neck 沉重负担（源自《古舟子咏》）

**woodcock**
英/美 /ˈwʊdkɒk/
n. 丘鹬；山鹬
[义] A brown woodland bird with a long bill, active at dusk. 林栖褐鸟、喙长，黄昏活动（Eurasian woodcock 欧亚丘鹬）。可数。
[例] ① A woodcock rose from the bracken at twilight. 暮色中丘鹬从蕨丛飞起。 ② Woodcocks probe soft soil for worms with their bills. 丘鹬用长喙在软土中探虫。
[搭] Eurasian woodcock 欧亚丘鹬, woodland bird 林鸟, probing bill 探食长喙
[联] bird 鸟, woodland 林地, worm 蠕虫, dusk 黄昏, game bird 猎鸟, bill 喙
[记] 可数：a woodcock。与 woodpecker 不同；亦作猎鸟

**hippopotamus**
英/美 /ˌhɪpəˈpɒtəməs/
n. 河马
[义] A very large African mammal with thick grey skin that lives in rivers and lakes. 厚灰皮、栖于河湖的超大型非洲哺乳动物（river hippopotamus 河流河马，common hippo 普通河马）。可数。
[例] ① The hippopotamus submerged until only its eyes showed. 河马潜入水中，只露出双眼。 ② Hippopotamuses are among Africa's most dangerous animals. 河马是非洲最危险的动物之一。
[形] hippopotami /ˌhɪpəˈpɒtəmaɪ/ 河马（复数，罕用）；口语多用 hippos
[搭] river hippopotamus 河流河马, common hippo 普通河马, hippo 河马（简称）
[联] hippo 河马, Africa 非洲, river 河, mammal 哺乳动物, dangerous 危险的, herbivore 食草动物
[记] 可数：a hippopotamus。口语常用 hippo；复数口语 hippos

**predation**
英/美 /prɪˈdeɪʃn/
n. 捕食；掠夺
[义] The act of one animal killing and eating another. 一动物猎杀并取食另一动物（predation pressure 捕食压力，reduce predation 减少捕食）。不可数；亦指掠夺行为。
[例] ① Predation keeps rabbit populations in check. 捕食制约兔群数量。 ② Nest predation by crows is a major threat to songbirds. 乌鸦掠巢是鸣禽的主要威胁。
[搭] predation pressure 捕食压力, nest predation 巢捕食, reduce predation 减少捕食
[联] predator 捕食者, prey 猎物, hunt 狩猎, food chain 食物链, population 种群, threat 威胁
[记] 不可数。predate（动词）较书面；predation 生态学常用

**poisonous**
英/美 /ˈpɔɪzənəs/
adj. 有毒的；恶毒的
[义] Containing poison that can harm or kill if eaten or touched. 含毒，误食或接触可致害或致死（poisonous mushroom 毒蘑菇，poisonous plant 有毒植物）。只作形容词。与 venomous（毒液经咬蜇注入）不同。
[例] ① Some brightly coloured frogs are highly poisonous. 一些色彩鲜艳的蛙类剧毒。 ② Never eat wild berries unless you know they are not poisonous. 不知无毒勿食野果。
[搭] poisonous mushroom 毒蘑菇, poisonous plant 有毒植物, poisonous to eat 误食有毒
[联] poison 毒, venomous 有毒液的, mushroom 蘑菇, plant 植物, dangerous 危险的, toxic 有毒的
[记] 只作形容词。poisonous（经口/接触）与 venomous（咬蜇注入）宜区分

**symbiosis**
英/美 /ˌsɪmbiˈəʊsɪs/
n. 共生
[义] A close relationship between two different species that benefits at least one. 不同物种间的紧密关系，至少一方受益（mutual symbiosis 互利共生，symbiosis with bacteria 与细菌共生）。不可数；可数指具体共生关系。
[例] ① Coral reefs depend on symbiosis between coral and algae. 珊瑚礁依赖珊瑚与藻类的共生。 ② Cleaner fish live in symbiosis with larger reef fish. 清洁鱼与大型礁鱼共生。
[搭] mutual symbiosis 互利共生, symbiosis with bacteria 与细菌共生, ecological symbiosis 生态共生
[联] symbiotic 共生的, species 物种, coral 珊瑚, bacteria 细菌, ecosystem 生态系统, mutual 互利的
[记] 不可数为主。形容词 symbiotic；biology 与生态报道常见

**symbiotic**
英/美 /ˌsɪmbɪˈɒtɪk/ 美 /ˌsɪmbaɪˈɑːtɪk/
adj. 共生的；互惠互利的
[义] (Of two different species) living together in a close relationship that benefits at least one. （两不同物种）紧密共生、至少一方受益的（symbiotic relationship 共生关系， symbiotic bacteria 共生细菌， symbiotic with coral 与珊瑚共生）。只作形容词。
[例] ① Algae live in symbiotic partnership with reef-building coral. 藻类与造礁珊瑚共生。 ② Gut bacteria form a symbiotic community inside mammals. 肠道细菌在哺乳动物体内形成共生群落。
[搭] symbiotic relationship 共生关系, symbiotic bacteria 共生细菌, symbiotic with coral 与珊瑚共生, mutually symbiotic 互利共生
[联] symbiosis 共生, species 物种, bacteria 细菌, coral 珊瑚, mutual 互利的, ecosystem 生态系统
[记] 只作形容词。symbiotic 亦可喻「互惠互利的关系」（symbiotic business deal），属比喻

**pollinate**
英/美 /ˈpɒləneɪt/
v. 授粉；传粉
[义] To carry pollen from one flower to another so that plants can produce seeds. 将花粉从一朵花传到另一朵，使植物结实（bees pollinate flowers 蜜蜂为花授粉，pollinate crops 为作物授粉）。及物动词。
[例] ① Bees pollinate apple blossoms in spring. 春天蜜蜂为苹果花授粉。 ② Without insects to pollinate them, many crops would fail. 若无昆虫传粉，许多作物将歉收。
[形] pollination /ˌpɒləˈneɪʃn/ 授粉（不可数）
[搭] pollinate flowers 为花授粉, pollinate crops 为作物授粉, cross-pollinate 异花授粉
[联] pollen 花粉, bee 蜜蜂, flower 花, insect 昆虫, crop 作物, blossom 花朵
[记] 及物：pollinate sth。名词 pollination；pollinator 传粉者

**pollination**
英/美 /ˌpɒləˈneɪʃn/
n. 授粉；传粉
[义] The process of carrying pollen between flowers so that plants can produce seeds and fruit. 花粉在花间传递、使植物结实的过程（cross-pollination 异花授粉， pollination service 授粉服务， decline in pollination 授粉下降）。不可数。
[例] ① Bees are essential for the pollination of many orchard crops. 蜜蜂对许多果园作物授粉不可或缺。 ② Wind pollination spreads pine pollen for miles. 风媒授粉将松树花粉传播数英里。
[搭] cross-pollination 异花授粉, pollination service 授粉服务, decline in pollination 授粉下降, insect pollination 虫媒授粉
[联] pollinate 授粉, pollinator 传粉者, pollen 花粉, bee 蜜蜂, flower 花, crop 作物, ecosystem 生态系统
[记] 不可数。与 pollinate（动词）、pollinator（传粉者）同族；农业与生态高频

**pollinator**
英/美 /ˈpɒləneɪtə(r)/
n. 传粉者；授粉媒介
[义] An animal or insect that carries pollen between flowers. 在花间传粉的动物或昆虫（key pollinators 关键传粉者，pollinator decline 传粉者减少）。可数。
[例] ① Bees are the most important pollinators in agriculture. 蜜蜂是农业最重要的传粉者。 ② Pesticides can harm pollinators such as butterflies. 农药可伤害蝴蝶等传粉者。
[搭] key pollinators 关键传粉者, pollinator decline 传粉者减少, native pollinators 本地传粉者
[联] pollinate 授粉, bee 蜜蜂, butterfly 蝴蝶, pollen 花粉, flower 花, ecosystem 生态系统
[记] 可数：a pollinator。生态与农业报道高频词

**toucan**
英/美 /ˈtuːkæn/
n. 巨嘴鸟
[义] A tropical American bird with a very large colourful bill. 热带美洲鸟类，喙大且色彩鲜艳（toco toucan 巨嘴䴕）。可数。
[例] ① A toucan perched on the branch, its bill gleaming. 巨嘴鸟栖于枝头，巨喙闪亮。 ② Toucans feed mainly on fruit in the rainforest canopy. 巨嘴鸟主要在雨林树冠取食水果。
[搭] toco toucan 巨嘴䴕, colourful bill 彩色巨喙, rainforest bird 雨林鸟类
[联] bird 鸟, rainforest 雨林, bill 喙, tropical 热带的, fruit 水果, colourful 色彩鲜艳的
[记] 可数：a toucan。大喙轻而中空，纪录片与动物园常见

**mongoose**
英/美 /ˈmɒŋɡuːs/
n. 獴
[义] A small carnivorous mammal known for killing snakes. 以捕蛇闻名的小型食肉哺乳动物（Indian grey mongoose 印度灰獴）。可数；复数 mongooses。
[例] ① The mongoose darted at the cobra with lightning speed. 獴闪电般扑向眼镜蛇。 ② Mongooses were introduced to control rats on some islands. 一些岛屿引入獴以控鼠。
[搭] Indian grey mongoose 印度灰獴, mongoose vs snake 獴与蛇, small carnivore 小型食肉动物
[联] snake 蛇, carnivore 食肉动物, mammal 哺乳动物, predator 捕食者, cobra 眼镜蛇, quick 敏捷的
[记] 可数：a mongoose；复数 mongooses（非 mongeese）

**arctic fox**
英/美 /ˌɑːktɪk ˈfɒks/
n. 北极狐
[义] A small fox of the Arctic with thick white fur in winter. 北极小型狐，冬毛厚白（Arctic fox coat 北极狐皮毛）。可数。
[例] ① The arctic fox turned pure white as winter approached. 入冬前北极狐毛色转纯白。 ② Arctic foxes follow polar bears to scavenge leftovers. 北极狐跟随北极熊捡食残羹。
[搭] Arctic fox coat 北极狐皮, white winter fur 白色冬毛, tundra fox 苔原狐
[联] fox 狐, Arctic 北极, tundra 苔原, fur 毛皮, polar bear 北极熊, winter 冬天
[记] 可数：an arctic fox。毛色随季节白褐变化

**prairie dog**
英/美 /ˈpreəri dɒɡ/
n. 草原犬鼠
[义] A small burrowing rodent of North American grasslands that lives in colonies. 北美草原穴居啮齿动物，群居（prairie dog town 犬鼠群落）。可数。
[例] ① Prairie dogs popped up from their burrows to watch for hawks. 草原犬鼠从洞口探身警戒鹰类。 ② A prairie dog town can cover hundreds of hectares. 一片犬鼠群落可占地数百公顷。
[搭] prairie dog town 犬鼠群落, burrowing rodent 穴居啮齿动物, grassland mammal 草原哺乳动物
[联] rodent 啮齿动物, burrow 洞穴, grassland 草原, colony 群落, hawk 鹰, North America 北美
[记] 可数：a prairie dog。与 dog 无关，因吠叫似犬得名

**hare**
英/美 /heə(r)/
n. 野兔
[义] A fast-running animal like a large rabbit with long ears and hind legs; lives in the open rather than burrows. 似大兔、善跑，耳长腿壮，栖于开阔地而非穴居（brown hare 褐野兔，snowshoe hare 雪鞋兔）。可数。
[例] ① A hare bolted across the field at dusk. 黄昏时野兔窜过田野。 ② Hares are generally larger and faster than rabbits. 野兔通常比家兔更大、更快。
[搭] brown hare 褐野兔, snowshoe hare 雪鞋兔, hare and tortoise 龟兔（寓言）
[联] rabbit 兔, run 奔跑, field 田野, fast 快速的, mammal 哺乳动物, burrow 洞穴
[记] 可数：a hare。寓言 the hare and the tortoise 龟兔赛跑；与 rabbit 栖息地、体型有别

**tortoise**
英/美 /ˈtɔːtəs/
n. 陆龟
[义] A slow-moving reptile with a hard shell that lives on land. 行动缓慢、背有硬壳的陆栖爬行动物（giant tortoise 象龟，Galápagos tortoise 加拉帕戈斯象龟）。可数。
[例] ① The tortoise crept across the sun-warmed rocks. 陆龟爬过晒暖的岩石。 ② Giant tortoises can live for more than a century. 象龟可活逾百年。
[搭] giant tortoise 象龟, Galápagos tortoise 加拉帕戈斯象龟, land tortoise 陆龟
[联] turtle 龟（海龟/水龟通称）, shell 壳, reptile 爬行动物, slow 缓慢的, long-lived 长寿的, desert 沙漠
[记] 可数：a tortoise。英式 tortoise 多指陆龟；美式 turtle 常兼指水龟

**echidna**
英/美 /ɪˈkɪdnə/
n. 针鼹
[义] An egg-laying Australian mammal with spines and a long snout. 澳大利亚卵生哺乳动物，身有棘、吻长（short-beaked echidna 短吻针鼹）。可数。
[例] ① The echidna curled into a spiky ball when threatened. 受威胁时针鼹蜷成刺球。 ② Echidnas use their long tongues to feed on ants. 针鼹用长舌食蚁。
[搭] short-beaked echidna 短吻针鼹, spiny mammal 有棘哺乳动物, egg-laying mammal 卵生哺乳动物
[联] platypus 鸭嘴兽, mammal 哺乳动物, Australia 澳大利亚, ant 蚂蚁, spine 刺, monotreme 单孔类

**tapir**
英/美 /ˈteɪpə(r)/
n. 貘
[义] A large mammal with a short trunk-like snout, found in Central and South America and Asia. 吻似短鼻、分布于中美南美及亚洲的大型哺乳动物（Malayan tapir 马来貘）。可数。
[例] ① A tapir waded through the river at night. 貘夜间涉河而过。 ② Tapirs are shy herbivores of tropical forests. 貘是热带森林中怯懦的食草动物。
[搭] Malayan tapir 马来貘, forest tapir 森林貘, herbivorous mammal 食草哺乳动物
[联] mammal 哺乳动物, forest 森林, herbivore 食草动物, snout 鼻吻, tropical 热带的, shy 怯懦的

**okapi**
英/美 /əʊˈkɑːpi/
n. 㺢㹢狓
[义] A forest mammal of central Africa with striped legs and a long neck, related to the giraffe. 中非森林哺乳动物，腿有条纹、颈长，与长颈鹿近缘（forest okapi 森林㺢㹢狓）。可数。
[例] ① The okapi remained hidden in dense rainforest undergrowth. 㺢㹢狓隐于茂密雨林下层。 ② Okapis were unknown to Western science until the early twentieth century. 㺢㹢狓至二十世纪初方被西方科学界确认。

[联] giraffe 长颈鹿, Africa 非洲, rainforest 雨林, mammal 哺乳动物, stripe 条纹, rare 稀有的
[记] 可数：an okapi。俗称「森林长颈鹿」，极难野外目击

**gazelle**
英/美 /ɡəˈzel/
n. 瞪羚
[义] A small graceful antelope that can run very fast. 体态优雅、奔跑极快的小型羚羊（Thomson's gazelle 汤氏瞪羚，gazelle leap 瞪羚跃）。可数。
[例] ① Gazelles sprang away as the jeep approached. 吉普靠近时瞪羚弹跃而去。 ② A cheetah chased a gazelle across the savanna. 猎豹在草原上追逐瞪羚。
[搭] Thomson's gazelle 汤氏瞪羚, gazelle leap 瞪羚跃, savanna antelope 草原羚羊

**jackal**
英/美 /ˈdʒækl/
n. 豺；走狗
[义] A wild dog-like animal of Africa and Asia that often hunts or scavenges in pairs. 非亚野犬形动物，常结对狩猎或食腐（golden jackal 金豺）。可数。喻「走狗、帮凶」。
[例] ① Jackals howled in the distance after sunset. 日落后远处豺声嚎叫。 ② Scavenging jackals followed the lion pride. 食腐豺跟随狮群捡剩食。
[搭] golden jackal 金豺, scavenging jackal 食腐豺, jackal howl 豺嚎
[联] wolf 狼, scavenger 食腐动物, Africa 非洲, howl 嚎叫, wild dog 野犬, predator 捕食者

英/美 /ˈmɪəkɑːt/
n. 猫鼬；狐獴
[义] A small African mongoose that stands upright to watch for danger. 非洲小型獴，直立瞭望危险（meerkat sentry 猫鼬哨兵，meerkat clan 猫鼬家族）。可数。
[例] ① A meerkat stood on its hind legs scanning the horizon. 猫鼬后腿直立扫视地平线。 ② Meerkats take turns acting as sentries for the group. 猫鼬轮流为群体站岗放哨。
[搭] meerkat sentry 猫鼬哨兵, meerkat clan 猫鼬家族, upright watch 直立瞭望

n. 狐猴
[义] A primate with a long tail, found only in Madagascar. 尾长、仅产于马达加斯加的灵长类（ring-tailed lemur 环尾狐猴，lemur troop 狐猴群）。可数。
[例] ① Ring-tailed lemurs sunbathe with arms spread wide. 环尾狐猴张臂晒日光浴。 ② Lemurs leap between trees in the rainforest canopy. 狐猴在雨林树冠间跳跃。
[搭] ring-tailed lemur 环尾狐猴, lemur troop 狐猴群, Madagascar primate 马达加斯加灵长类
[联] primate 灵长类, Madagascar 马达加斯加, tail 尾巴, monkey 猴子, rainforest 雨林, endangered 濒危的

英/美 /ˈɡɪbən/
n. 长臂猿
[义] A small ape with very long arms that swings through trees. 臂极长、在林间摆荡的小型类人猿（white-handed gibbon 白手长臂猿，gibbon song 长臂猿鸣）。可数。
[例] ① Gibbons brachiate effortlessly from branch to branch. 长臂猿轻松摆荡于枝间。 ② A gibbon's morning call echoed through the valley. 长臂猿晨鸣回荡山谷。
[搭] white-handed gibbon 白手长臂猿, gibbon song 长臂猿鸣, swing through trees 林间摆荡

英/美 /bəˈbuːn/
n. 狒狒
[义] A large African monkey with a dog-like face and long canine teeth. 非洲大型猴，面似犬、犬齿长（olive baboon 橄榄狒狒，baboon troop 狒狒群）。可数。
[例] ① Baboons groomed each other on the rocky hillside. 狒狒在岩坡上相互理毛。 ② A baboon troop marched across the road in single file. 狒狒群单列穿过公路。

[记] 可数：a baboon。群居性强，与狮豹冲突纪录片常见

**cougar**
英/美 /ˈkuːɡə(r)/
n. 美洲狮；彪马
[义] A large wild cat of the Americas, also called puma or mountain lion. 美洲大型野猫，亦称 puma、mountain lion（mountain cougar 山地美洲狮）。可数。
[例] ① A cougar padded silently through the pine forest. 美洲狮悄然穿过松林。 ② Cougars prey on deer and smaller mammals. 美洲狮捕食鹿及较小哺乳动物。
[搭] mountain cougar 山地美洲狮, puma 美洲狮, mountain lion 美洲狮（美）

[记] 可数：a cougar。与 puma、mountain lion 同物异名；俚语 cougar 指「熟女追小伙」，不同义

**beluga**
英/美 /bɪˈluːɡə/
n. 白鲸
[义] A small white whale of Arctic and sub-Arctic waters. 北极及亚北极水域的小型白鲸（beluga whale 白鲸，beluga pod 白鲸群）。可数。
[例] ① Belugas surfaced in the icy channel, chirping to one another. 白鲸在冰封水道浮出水面，彼此吱鸣交流。 ② Beluga whales are known for their expressive faces. 白鲸以表情丰富著称。

[联] whale 鲸, dolphin 海豚, Arctic 北极, mammal 哺乳动物, pod 鲸群, white 白色的

**dugong**
英/美 /ˈduːɡɒŋ/

[义] A large marine mammal of warm coastal waters, related to manatees. 暖海岸水域大型海洋哺乳动物，与海牛近缘（dugong population 儒艮种群）。可数。
[例] ① Dugongs graze on seagrass in shallow bays. 儒艮在浅湾食海草。 ② Dugong numbers have fallen sharply in some regions. 部分地区儒艮数量锐减。
[搭] dugong population 儒艮种群, seagrass grazer 食海草动物, coastal mammal 海岸哺乳动物

**wasp**

[例] ① A wasp buzzed around the picnic table. 黄蜂在野餐桌旁嗡嗡盘旋。 ② She was stung by a wasp while gardening. 她在园艺时被黄蜂蜇了。
[搭] paper wasp 胡蜂, wasp sting 蜂蜇, wasp nest 蜂巢

**pheasant**
英/美 /ˈfeznt/
n. 雉；野鸡
[义] A large bird with a long tail, often hunted for sport or food. 尾长的大型鸟，常作狩猎或食用（ring-necked pheasant 环颈雉，game bird 猎鸟）。可数。
[例] ① A male pheasant strutted across the field with its tail fanned. 雄雉展尾大步走过田野。 ② Pheasants were introduced to Britain for hunting. 雉鸡为狩猎引入英国。
[搭] ring-necked pheasant 环颈雉, game bird 猎鸟, pheasant hunting 猎雉
[联] bird 鸟, hunt 狩猎, tail 尾巴, field 田野, game 猎物, colourful 色彩鲜艳的

**finch**

[义] A small seed-eating songbird with a short thick beak. 喙短厚、以种子为食的小型鸣禽（house finch 家朱雀，goldfinch 金翅雀）。可数。
[例] ① Finches crowded the bird feeder in winter. 冬天雀鸟挤满喂鸟器。 ② Darwin studied finches on the Galápagos Islands. 达尔文在加拉帕戈斯研究雀鸟。
[搭] house finch 家朱雀, goldfinch 金翅雀, Darwin's finches 达尔文雀
[联] bird 鸟, songbird 鸣禽, seed 种子, beak 喙, feeder 喂鸟器, evolution 进化

**chipmunk**
英/美 /ˈtʃɪpmʌŋk/

[义] A small striped ground squirrel that stores food in cheek pouches. 体有条纹、颊囊储粮的地面松鼠（eastern chipmunk 东部花栗鼠）。可数。
[例] ① A chipmunk stuffed seeds into its cheek pouches. 花栗鼠把种子塞进颊囊。 ② Chipmunks dart between rocks in the garden. 花栗鼠在花园岩石间窜动。
[搭] eastern chipmunk 东部花栗鼠, cheek pouches 颊囊, striped squirrel 条纹松鼠
[联] squirrel 松鼠, rodent 啮齿动物, stripe 条纹, cheek 脸颊, seed 种子, garden 花园

**emu**
英/美 /ˈiːmjuː/
n. 鸸鹋
[义] A large flightless bird of Australia, smaller than the ostrich. 澳大利亚大型不能飞鸟类，体型小于鸵鸟（emu farm 鸸鹋养殖场）。可数。
[例] ① An emu raced across the outback road ahead of us. 鸸鹋在我们前方的内陆公路飞奔。 ② Emus cannot fly but run at high speed. 鸸鹋不能飞但奔跑极快。
[搭] emu farm 鸸鹋养殖场, Australian flightless bird 澳大利亚不能飞鸟, outback bird 内陆鸟
[联] ostrich 鸵鸟, Australia 澳大利亚, flightless 不能飞的, bird 鸟, run 奔跑, outback 内陆
[记] 可数：an emu。澳大利亚国徽动物之一，与袋鼠并列

**polar bear**
英/美 /ˌpəʊlə ˈbeə(r)/
n. 北极熊
[义] A large white bear that lives on Arctic sea ice and hunts seals. 栖于北极海冰、捕食海豹的大型白熊（polar bear cub 北极熊幼崽，polar bear habitat 北极熊栖息地）。可数。
[例] ① A polar bear waited by a seal breathing hole in the ice. 北极熊在冰上海豹呼吸孔旁守候。 ② Polar bears are threatened by melting sea ice. 北极熊因海冰融化而受威胁。
[搭] polar bear cub 北极熊幼崽, polar bear habitat 北极熊栖息地, Arctic predator 北极捕食者
[联] bear 熊, Arctic 北极, seal 海豹, ice 冰, endangered 濒危的, predator 捕食者

**cockatoo**
英/美 /ˌkɒkəˈtuː/
n. 凤头鹦鹉
[义] A parrot with a crest of feathers on its head, often white or pink. 头顶有羽冠的鹦鹉，常白色或粉色（sulphur-crested cockatoo 葵花凤头鹦鹉）。可数。
[例] ① The cockatoo raised its yellow crest in alarm. 凤头鹦鹉惊起黄色冠羽。 ② Cockatoos are loud and long-lived companion birds. 凤头鹦鹉叫声大、寿命长，常作伴侣鸟。
[搭] sulphur-crested cockatoo 葵花凤头鹦鹉, crested parrot 有冠鹦鹉, companion bird 伴侣鸟
[联] parrot 鹦鹉, bird 鸟, crest 羽冠, Australia 澳大利亚, loud 吵闹的, pet 宠物

**jay**
英/美 /dʒeɪ/
n. 松鸦
[义] A noisy woodland bird with bright feathers, often blue. 林栖鸣禽，羽色鲜艳，常蓝色（Eurasian jay 欧亚松鸦，blue jay 冠蓝鸦〔美〕）。可数。
[例] ① A jay screeched from the oak tree. 松鸦在橡树上尖声鸣叫。 ② Jays hide acorns in the forest floor for winter. 松鸦将橡实藏于林下过冬。
[搭] Eurasian jay 欧亚松鸦, blue jay 冠蓝鸦, woodland bird 林鸟
[联] bird 鸟, woodland 林地, oak 橡树, acorn 橡实, noisy 吵闹的, feather 羽毛
[记] 可数：a jay。欧亚 jay 偏棕色斑纹；北美 blue jay 蓝色醒目

**crane**
英/美 /kreɪn/
n. 鹤
[义] A tall wading bird with long legs and a long neck. 腿颈修长的高大涉禽（common crane 灰鹤，whooping crane 美洲鹤）。可数。
[例] ① Cranes danced and trumpeted at the wetland. 鹤在湿地起舞鸣叫。 ② A pair of cranes built a nest in the marsh. 一对鹤在沼泽筑巢。
[搭] common crane 灰鹤, whooping crane 美洲鹤, crane migration 鹤迁徙
[联] bird 鸟, wetland 湿地, nest 巢, migrate 迁徙, tall 高大的, wading bird 涉禽
[记] 可数：a crane。crane 亦指起重机（机械），同形异义

**shrimp**
英/美 /ʃrɪmp/
n. 虾
[义] A small shellfish with a curved body and many legs. 体弯、多足的小型甲壳类（freshwater shrimp 淡水虾，shrimp farm 养虾场）。可数/不可数。
[例] ① Tiny shrimp swarmed in the tidal pool. 小虾群聚于潮池。 ② Fishermen hauled in nets full of shrimp. 渔民拉起满网虾。
[搭] freshwater shrimp 淡水虾, shrimp farm 养虾场, fried shrimp 炸虾（食材→4-01）
[联] shellfish 贝类, ocean 海洋, crab 蟹, seafood 海鲜, farm 养殖, small 小的
[记] 可数/不可数。作海鲜菜肴归 4-01；作物种归本章

**oyster**
英/美 /ˈɔɪstə(r)/
n. 牡蛎；蚝
[义] A shellfish with a rough shell that lives in the sea, some kinds producing pearls. 海生壳粗糙的贝类，部分种类产珠（pearl oyster 珠母贝，oyster bed 牡蛎床）。可数。
[例] ① Oysters cling to rocks in the estuary. 牡蛎附着在河口岩石上。 ② Pearl oysters can grow gems over many years. 珠母贝可经年养成珍珠。
[搭] pearl oyster 珠母贝, oyster bed 牡蛎床, raw oyster 生蚝（食材→4-01）
[联] shellfish 贝类, pearl 珍珠, ocean 海洋, shell 壳, reef 礁, seafood 海鲜
[记] 可数：an oyster。the world is your oyster 机会无限（习语）；生蚝归 4-01

**macaque**
英/美 /məˈkɑːk/
n. 猕猴
[义] A monkey of Asia and Africa with cheek pouches and no tail. 亚非猴类，有颊囊、无尾（rhesus macaque 恒河猴，macaque troop 猕猴群）。可数。
[例] ① Macaques groomed one another on the temple steps. 猕猴在庙阶上相互理毛。 ② Rhesus macaques are widely used in medical research. 恒河猴广泛用于医学研究。
[搭] rhesus macaque 恒河猴, macaque troop 猕猴群, temple monkey 庙猴
[联] monkey 猴子, primate 灵长类, baboon 狒狒, cheek pouch 颊囊, Asia 亚洲, troop 兽群
[记] 可数：a macaque。与 baboon 不同，macaque 通常无长尾

**marmot**
英/美 /ˈmɑːmət/
n. 旱獭；土拨鼠
[义] A large burrowing rodent of mountains and grasslands that hibernates in winter. 山地与草原大型穴居啮齿动物，冬蛰（alpine marmot 阿尔卑斯旱獭）。可数。
[例] ① Marmots whistled warnings from rocky slopes. 旱獭在岩坡鸣笛示警。 ② The marmot retreated into its burrow before the storm. 暴风雨前旱獭退回洞中。
[搭] alpine marmot 阿尔卑斯旱獭, marmot burrow 旱獭洞, whistling rodent 鸣笛啮齿动物
[联] rodent 啮齿动物, burrow 洞穴, hibernate 冬眠, mountain 山地, prairie dog 草原犬鼠, whistle 鸣笛
[记] 可数：a marmot。与 prairie dog 不同属；高山草甸常见

**gerbil**
英/美 /ˈdʒɜːbl/
n. 沙鼠
[义] A small desert rodent often kept as a pet. 沙漠小型啮齿动物，常作宠物（Mongolian gerbil 蒙古沙鼠）。可数。
[例] ① The children watched the gerbil run on its wheel. 孩子看沙鼠在滚轮上跑。 ② Gerbils dig tunnels in sandy soil. 沙鼠在沙土中挖隧道。
[搭] Mongolian gerbil 蒙古沙鼠, pet gerbil 宠物沙鼠, desert rodent 沙漠啮齿动物
[联] rodent 啮齿动物, hamster 仓鼠, desert 沙漠, pet 宠物, burrow 洞穴, small 小的
[记] 可数：a gerbil。与 hamster 近缘，尾较长，宠物店常见

**kiwi**
英/美 /ˈkiːwi/
n. 几维鸟
[义] A flightless bird of New Zealand with a long bill and no visible wings. 新西兰不能飞鸟，喙长、翼不显眼（North Island brown kiwi 北岛褐几维）。可数。
[例] ① The kiwi foraged for worms in the forest litter at night. 几维鸟夜间在落叶层中觅食蠕虫。 ② Kiwis are national symbols of New Zealand. 几维鸟是新西兰国鸟象征。
[搭] North Island brown kiwi 北岛褐几维, flightless bird 不能飞鸟, national bird 国鸟
[联] bird 鸟, New Zealand 新西兰, flightless 不能飞的, nocturnal 夜行的, worm 蠕虫, endangered 濒危的
[记] 可数：a kiwi。Kiwi 亦指新西兰人昵称；kiwi fruit 猕猴桃，不同义

**ibis**
英/美 /ˈaɪbɪs/
n. 朱鹭；鹮
[义] A wading bird with a long curved bill, often white or glossy. 喙长弯的涉禽，常白色或有光泽（sacred ibis 圣鹮，scarlet ibis 猩红鹮）。可数。
[例] ① Ibises probed the mudflats at low tide. 退潮时朱鹭在泥滩探食。 ② A flock of scarlet ibises roosted in the mangroves. 一群猩红鹮栖于红树林。
[搭] sacred ibis 圣鹮, scarlet ibis 猩红鹮, wading bird 涉禽
[联] bird 鸟, wetland 湿地, bill 喙, flock 鸟群, wading 涉水的, mangrove 红树林
[记] 可数：an ibis。古埃及圣鹮意象常见；湿地观鸟高频词

**egret**
英/美 /ˈiːɡrət/
n. 白鹭
[义] A white or partly white heron, especially one with fine plumes in breeding season. 白色或部分白色的鹭，繁殖期羽饰华美（great egret 大白鹭，little egret 小白鹭）。可数。
[例] ① Egrets stood motionless in the shallow rice paddies. 白鹭静立于浅稻田。 ② Great egrets spread their plumes during courtship. 大白鹭求偶时展开饰羽。
[搭] great egret 大白鹭, little egret 小白鹭, breeding plumes 繁殖羽
[联] heron 鹭, bird 鸟, white 白色的, wetland 湿地, plume 羽饰, wading bird 涉禽
[记] 可数：an egret。与 heron 近缘；历史上因羽饰遭过度捕猎

**clam**
英/美 /klæm/
n. 蛤蜊；蚌
[义] A shellfish with a shell in two hinged parts, living buried in sand or mud. 壳分两瓣铰合、埋栖沙泥的贝类（hard clam 硬蛤，clam shell 蛤壳）。可数。
[例] ① Clams buried themselves as the tide went out. 退潮时蛤蜊埋入沙中。 ② Children dug for clams on the beach. 孩子在海滩挖蛤。
[搭] hard clam 硬蛤, clam shell 蛤壳, clam digging 挖蛤
[联] shellfish 贝类, shell 壳, beach 海滩, tide 潮汐, oyster 牡蛎, seafood 海鲜
[记] 可数：a clam。作海鲜归 4-01；clam up 指「闭口不言」，不同义

**mussel**
英/美 /ˈmʌsl/
n. 贻贝；青口
[义] A shellfish with a dark elongated shell that attaches to rocks with threads. 壳长暗色、以足丝附石的贝类（blue mussel 蓝贻贝，mussel bed 贻贝床）。可数。
[例] ① Mussels clung to the pier in dense clusters. 贻贝密密依附码头。 ② Fresh mussels must be cooked while still alive. 鲜贻贝须活烹。
[搭] blue mussel 蓝贻贝, mussel bed 贻贝床, steamed mussels 蒸青口（食材→4-01）
[联] shellfish 贝类, shell 壳, reef 礁, ocean 海洋, clam 蛤蜊, seafood 海鲜
[记] 可数：a mussel。作菜肴归 4-01；与 muscle（肌肉）同音异义

**pufferfish**
英/美 /ˈpʌfəfɪʃ/
n. 河豚
[义] A fish that inflates its body and often contains deadly poison. 遇险充气膨胀、体内常含剧毒的鱼（pufferfish toxin 河豚毒素，fugu 河豚〔日〕）。可数。
[例] ① The pufferfish swelled into a spiky ball when threatened. 受威胁时河豚胀成刺球。 ② Pufferfish poison can be lethal if prepared wrongly. 河豚处理不当可致命。
[搭] pufferfish toxin 河豚毒素, fugu 河豚, inflate when threatened 受威胁充气
[联] fish 鱼, poison 毒, ocean 海洋, reef 礁, toxic 有毒的, Japan 日本
[记] 可数：a pufferfish。日本 fugu 需持证厨师处理；毒素归本章生态义

**blue whale**
英/美 /ˌbluː ˈweɪl/
n. 蓝鲸
[义] The largest animal that has ever lived, a blue-grey whale of the open ocean. 已知史上最大动物，开洋蓝灰色鲸（blue whale calf 蓝鲸幼崽）。可数。
[例] ① A blue whale surfaced, spouting a tall plume of mist. 蓝鲸浮出水面，喷出高柱水雾。 ② Blue whales feed almost entirely on krill. 蓝鲸几乎完全以磷虾为食。
[搭] blue whale calf 蓝鲸幼崽, largest mammal 最大哺乳动物, krill feeder 食磷虾者
[联] whale 鲸, krill 磷虾, ocean 海洋, mammal 哺乳动物, endangered 濒危的, huge 巨大的
[记] 可数：a blue whale。与 whale 词条呼应，强调物种与体型纪录

**brown bear**
英/美 /ˌbraʊn ˈbeə(r)/
n. 棕熊
[义] A large bear with brown fur, found in North America, Europe, and Asia. 毛褐的大型熊，分布于北美欧亚（grizzly bear 灰熊〔北美亚种〕，brown bear habitat 棕熊栖息地）。可数。
[例] ① A brown bear fished for salmon in the river. 棕熊在河中捕鱼。 ② Brown bears hibernate through the winter months. 棕熊整个冬季冬眠。
[搭] grizzly bear 灰熊, brown bear habitat 棕熊栖息地, salmon fishing 捕鱼
[联] bear 熊, grizzly 灰熊, hibernate 冬眠, salmon 鲑, forest 森林, mammal 哺乳动物
[记] 可数：a brown bear。北美 grizzly 为棕熊亚种；与 polar bear 对照

**musk ox**
英/美 /ˈmʌsk ɒks/
n. 麝牛
[义] A shaggy Arctic mammal related to goats and sheep, with curved horns. 北极毛茸茸哺乳动物，与羊近缘、角弯（musk ox herd 麝牛群）。可数；复数 musk oxen。
[例] ① Musk oxen formed a defensive circle around their calves. 麝牛围成防御圈护幼崽。 ② A musk ox's thick coat insulates it against Arctic cold. 麝牛厚毛御寒于北极严寒。
[搭] musk ox herd 麝牛群, Arctic mammal 北极哺乳动物, defensive circle 防御圈
[联] ox 牛, Arctic 北极, herd 兽群, horn 角, mammal 哺乳动物, tundra 苔原
[记] 可数：a musk ox；复数 musk oxen。遇险群体围圈护幼，纪录片常见

**canary**
英/美 /kəˈneəri/
n. 金丝雀
[义] A small yellow songbird often kept in cages. 常笼养的小型黄色鸣禽（domestic canary 家养金丝雀，canary song 金丝雀鸣）。可数。
[例] ① The canary trilled from its cage by the window. 金丝雀在窗边笼中啭鸣。 ② Canaries were once used in coal mines to detect gas. 金丝雀曾用于煤矿测毒气。
[搭] domestic canary 家养金丝雀, canary song 金丝雀鸣, cage bird 笼鸟
[联] bird 鸟, songbird 鸣禽, yellow 黄色的, cage 笼子, sing 鸣叫, pet 宠物
[记] 可数：a canary。习语 canary in the coal mine 早期预警信号

**macaw**
英/美 /məˈkɔː/
n. 金刚鹦鹉
[义] A large colourful parrot of Central and South America with a long tail. 中美南美大型彩色鹦鹉，尾长（scarlet macaw 绯红金刚鹦鹉）。可数。
[例] ① Macaws flew in pairs above the rainforest canopy. 金刚鹦鹉成对飞越雨林树冠。 ② A scarlet macaw's call echoed for miles. 绯红金刚鹦鹉鸣声数里可闻。
[搭] scarlet macaw 绯红金刚鹦鹉, rainforest parrot 雨林鹦鹉, colourful plumage 彩色羽衣
[联] parrot 鹦鹉, bird 鸟, rainforest 雨林, colourful 色彩鲜艳的, tail 尾巴, fly 飞
[记] 可数：a macaw。寿命长，宠物与走私贸易议题常见

**parakeet**
英/美 /ˈpærəkiːt/
n. 长尾鹦鹉；虎皮鹦鹉
[义] A small slender parrot with a long tail, often kept as a pet. 体纤、尾长的小型鹦鹉，常作宠物（budgerigar 虎皮鹦鹉〔俗称 budgie〕，ring-necked parakeet 红领绿鹦鹉）。可数。
[例] ① Parakeets chattered on the bird feeder in the garden. 长尾鹦鹉在花园喂鸟器上叽喳。 ② Budgies are the most popular pet parakeets worldwide. 虎皮鹦鹉是全球最受欢迎的宠物鹦鹉。
[搭] budgerigar 虎皮鹦鹉, ring-necked parakeet 红领绿鹦鹉, pet parakeet 宠物鹦鹉
[联] parrot 鹦鹉, budgie 虎皮鹦鹉, bird 鸟, pet 宠物, cage 笼子, chatter 叽喳
[记] 可数：a parakeet。budgie 为 budgerigar 口语简称

**tern**
英/美 /tɜːn/
n. 燕鸥
[义] A seabird with long pointed wings and a forked tail, often diving for fish. 翼尖长、尾叉状的海鸟，常俯冲捕鱼（Arctic tern 北极燕鸥，common tern 普通燕鸥）。可数。
[例] ① Terns plunged into the sea after shoals of fish. 燕鸥俯冲入海追鱼群。 ② The Arctic tern migrates farther than any other bird. 北极燕鸥迁徙距离冠绝鸟类。
[搭] Arctic tern 北极燕鸥, common tern 普通燕鸥, diving seabird 俯冲海鸟
[联] seabird 海鸟, gull 海鸥, migrate 迁徙, fish 鱼, ocean 海洋, dive 俯冲
[记] 可数：a tern。北极燕鸥年迁徙可达数万公里

**cormorant**
英/美 /ˈkɔːmərənt/
n. 鸬鹚
[义] A large black seabird that dives underwater to catch fish. 大型黑色海鸟，潜水捕鱼（great cormorant 普通鸬鹚，cormorant fishing 鸬鹚捕鱼〔传统〕）。可数。
[例] ① Cormorants dried their wings on the rocks after diving. 鸬鹚潜水后在岩石上晾翼。 ② Fishermen once trained cormorants to catch fish in China. 中国曾训鸬鹚捕鱼。
[搭] great cormorant 普通鸬鹚, cormorant fishing 鸬鹚捕鱼, diving bird 潜水鸟
[联] seabird 海鸟, fish 鱼, dive 潜水, wing 翅膀, ocean 海洋, fisherman 渔民
[记] 可数：a cormorant。翼脂少，常展翅晾干；东亚传统渔法有名

**quail**
英/美 /kweɪl/
n. 鹌鹑
[义] A small game bird related to partridges, with a plump body. 体型圆润的小型猎鸟，与鹧鸪近缘（Japanese quail 日本鹌鹑，quail egg 鹌鹑蛋）。可数。
[例] ① A covey of quail scurried through the underbrush. 一群鹌鹑窜过灌木丛。 ② Quail eggs are smaller than chicken eggs. 鹌鹑蛋比鸡蛋小。
[搭] Japanese quail 日本鹌鹑, quail egg 鹌鹑蛋（食材→4-01）, game bird 猎鸟
[联] bird 鸟, partridge 鹧鸪, egg 蛋, game 猎物, small 小的, covey 鸟群
[记] 可数：a quail；一群 a covey of quail。鹌鹑蛋作食材归 4-01

**cuckoo**
英/美 /ˈkʊkuː/
n. 杜鹃；布谷鸟
[义] A bird known for its two-note call and for laying eggs in other birds' nests. 以双音节鸣叫、寄生于他鸟巢产卵闻名（common cuckoo 大杜鹃，cuckoo call 布谷声）。可数。
[例] ① We heard the first cuckoo of spring in the woods. 林中听见春天第一声布谷。 ② Cuckoos are brood parasites that trick other species. 杜鹃为巢寄生，欺骗他种育雏。
[搭] common cuckoo 大杜鹃, cuckoo call 布谷声, brood parasite 巢寄生
[联] bird 鸟, nest 巢, spring 春天, call 鸣叫, parasite 寄生, egg 蛋
[记] 可数：a cuckoo。cuckoo 亦指「傻」〔英俚〕；as mad as a cuckoo 疯疯癫癫

**thrush**
英/美 /θrʌʃ/
n. 鸫
[义] A medium-sized songbird, often brown above and spotted below, that feeds on worms and berries. 中型鸣禽，上褐下常具斑，食虫果（song thrush 欧歌鸫，mistle thrush 槲鸫）。可数。
[例] ① A thrush hammered a snail on a stone to break the shell. 鸫在石上敲碎螺壳。 ② Thrushes sing from treetops at dawn. 鸫在黎明树梢鸣唱。
[搭] song thrush 欧歌鸫, mistle thrush 槲鸫, spotted breast 斑点胸
[联] bird 鸟, songbird 鸣禽, worm 蠕虫, berry 浆果, sing 鸣唱, spotted 有斑点的
[记] 可数：a thrush。thrush 亦指鹅口疮（病），同形异义

**blackbird**
英/美 /ˈblækbɜːd/
n. 乌鸫
[义] A common European songbird; the male is black with a yellow bill. 欧洲常见鸣禽，雄鸟黑色、喙黄（common blackbird 欧乌鸫）。可数。
[例] ① A blackbird sang from the garden hedge at dusk. 黄昏乌鸫在花园树篱鸣唱。 ② Blackbirds probe the lawn for worms after rain. 雨后乌鸫在草坪探虫。
[搭] common blackbird 欧乌鸫, garden bird 庭园鸟, yellow bill 黄喙
[联] thrush 鸫, bird 鸟, garden 花园, sing 鸣唱, worm 蠕虫, songbird 鸣禽
[记] 可数：a blackbird。北美 blackbird 常指黑鹂类，与欧乌鸫不同

**wren**
英/美 /ren/
n. 鹪鹩
[义] A very small brown songbird with a loud voice and an upright tail. 体小褐羽、声洪、尾常竖起的鸣禽（Eurasian wren 欧亚鹪鹩）。可数。
[例] ① A wren darted through the ivy on the wall. 鹪鹩窜过墙上常春藤。 ② Despite its size, the wren has a remarkably loud song. 鹪鹩虽小，鸣声却极响亮。
[搭] Eurasian wren 欧亚鹪鹩, tiny songbird 微型鸣禽, loud song 响亮鸣声
[联] bird 鸟, songbird 鸣禽, small 小的, tail 尾巴, sing 鸣唱, garden 花园
[记] 可数：a wren。英国「鹪鹩之王」民间传说有名

**pangolin**
英/美 /ˈpæŋɡəlɪn/
n. 穿山甲
[义] A mammal covered in scales that rolls into a ball when threatened; eats ants and termites. 全身覆鳞、受威胁蜷球的哺乳动物，食蚁白蚁（endangered pangolin 濒危穿山甲）。可数。
[例] ① The pangolin curled into an armoured ball as the predator approached. 捕食者靠近时穿山甲蜷成鳞甲球。 ② Pangolins are the most trafficked mammals in the world. 穿山甲是全球走私最严重的哺乳动物。
[搭] endangered pangolin 濒危穿山甲, scaled mammal 有鳞哺乳动物, ant eater 食蚁动物
[联] scale 鳞片, mammal 哺乳动物, ant 蚂蚁, termite 白蚁, endangered 濒危的, traffick 走私
[记] 可数：a pangolin。鳞片似爬行动物，实为哺乳动物；保育热点物种

**impala**
英/美 /ɪmˈpɑːlə/
n. 黑斑羚
[义] A medium-sized African antelope known for its leaps. 非洲中型羚羊，以弹跳闻名（impala herd 黑斑羚群，impala leap 黑斑羚跃）。可数。
[例] ① Impalas leapt over the stream in graceful arcs. 黑斑羚优美弧线跃过溪流。 ② An impala herd grazed nervously on the savanna. 黑斑羚群在草原紧张食草。
[搭] impala herd 黑斑羚群, impala leap 黑斑羚跃, savanna antelope 草原羚羊
[联] antelope 羚羊, gazelle 瞪羚, savanna 草原, leap 跳跃, herd 兽群, Africa 非洲
[记] 可数：an impala。非洲草原纪录片与动物园常见

**red panda**
英/美 /ˌred ˈpændə/
n. 小熊猫
[义] A raccoon-sized mammal of the Himalayas with reddish fur and a bushy ringed tail. 喜马拉雅浣熊大小哺乳动物，毛红褐、尾蓬松有环纹（lesser panda 小熊猫）。可数。
[例] ① The red panda climbed slowly through the bamboo. 小熊猫缓攀竹丛。 ② Red pandas are more closely related to raccoons than to giant pandas. 小熊猫与浣熊亲缘更近，而非大熊猫。
[搭] lesser panda 小熊猫, bamboo forest 竹林, ringed tail 环纹尾
[联] panda 熊猫, raccoon 浣熊, bamboo 竹, tail 尾巴, Himalaya 喜马拉雅, endangered 濒危的
[记] 可数：a red panda。亦称 lesser panda；与 giant panda 不同科

**aardvark**
英/美 /ˈɑːdvɑːk/
n. 土豚
[义] An African mammal with a long snout that eats ants and termites. 非洲哺乳动物，吻长，食蚁白蚁（aardvark burrow 土豚洞）。可数。
[例] ① The aardvark emerged at night to hunt termites. 土豚夜间出洞猎食白蚁。 ② An aardvark can dig through hard soil with powerful claws. 土豚以有力利爪掘穿硬土。
[搭] aardvark burrow 土豚洞, ant eater 食蚁动物, nocturnal mammal 夜行哺乳动物
[联] mammal 哺乳动物, ant 蚂蚁, termite 白蚁, Africa 非洲, burrow 洞穴, snout 鼻吻
[记] 可数：an aardvark。Afrikaans 语源，意为「土猪」；与 anteater 不同科

**springbok**
英/美 /ˈsprɪŋbɒk/
n. 跳羚
[义] A small antelope of southern Africa known for leaping when startled. 南非小型羚羊，受惊时弹跳（springbok herd 跳羚群，pronking 弹跳跃）。可数。
[例] ① Springboks pronked across the dry veld in stiff-legged leaps. 跳羚在干燥草原上僵腿弹跃。 ② The springbok is a national symbol of South Africa. 跳羚是南非国家象征之一。
[搭] springbok herd 跳羚群, pronking leap 弹跳跃, national antelope 国羚
[联] antelope 羚羊, gazelle 瞪羚, Africa 非洲, leap 跳跃, herd 兽群, savanna 草原
[记] 可数：a springbok。南非橄榄球队亦称 Springboks

**ocelot**
英/美 /ˈɒsəlɒt/
n. 豹猫；虎猫
[义] A wild cat of Central and South America with a spotted coat. 中美南美野猫，毛皮有斑（ocelot coat 豹猫皮，spotted wild cat 斑点野猫）。可数。
[例] ① An ocelot slipped through the moonlit jungle undergrowth. 豹猫掠过月光下丛林下层。 ② Ocelots hunt small mammals and birds at night. 豹猫夜间捕食小型哺乳动物与鸟类。
[搭] ocelot coat 豹猫皮, spotted wild cat 斑点野猫, jungle predator 丛林捕食者
[联] cat 猫, jaguar 美洲豹, leopard 豹, predator 捕食者, spotted 有斑点的, jungle 丛林
[记] 可数：an ocelot。体型小于 jaguar；历史上因毛皮遭滥捕

**serval**
英/美 /ˈsɜːvl/
n. 薮猫
[义] A long-legged African wild cat with large ears and a spotted coat. 非洲野猫，腿长腿大、有斑（serval leap 薮猫跃，grassland cat 草原猫）。可数。
[例] ① The serval pounced on a mouse in the tall grass. 薮猫从长草中扑向老鼠。 ② Servals use their huge ears to locate prey underground. 薮猫以巨耳定位地下猎物。
[搭] serval leap 薮猫跃, grassland cat 草原猫, large-eared cat 大耳猫
[联] cat 猫, Africa 非洲, ear 耳朵, leap 跳跃, predator 捕食者, spotted 有斑点的
[记] 可数：a serval。耳大、腿极长，跳跃捕食能力强

**black bear**
英/美 /ˌblæk ˈbeə(r)/
n. 黑熊
[义] A medium-sized bear, usually black, found in North America and Asia. 中型熊，通常黑色，分布于北美与亚洲（American black bear 美洲黑熊，Asiatic black bear 亚洲黑熊）。可数。
[例] ① A black bear raided the campsite for food. 黑熊为觅食洗劫营地。 ② Black bears climb trees more readily than brown bears. 黑熊比棕熊更易爬树。
[搭] American black bear 美洲黑熊, Asiatic black bear 亚洲黑熊, bear cub 熊崽
[联] bear 熊, brown bear 棕熊, forest 森林, cub 幼崽, omnivore 杂食动物, campsite 营地
[记] 可数：a black bear。与 brown bear、polar bear 对照；美洲种体色亦有肉桂色型

**grizzly**
英/美 /ˈɡrɪzli/
n. 灰熊
adj. 灰白的
[义] A large brown bear of North America with grizzled fur. 北美大型棕熊，毛色灰白相间（grizzly bear 灰熊，grizzly habitat 灰熊栖息地）。可数。
[例] ① Grizzlies fish for salmon in Alaskan rivers each autumn. 灰熊每年秋天在阿拉斯加河流捕鱼。 ② Hikers carry bear spray in grizzly country. 在灰熊地区徒步者携带防熊喷雾。
[搭] grizzly bear 灰熊, grizzly habitat 灰熊栖息地, bear spray 防熊喷雾
[联] brown bear 棕熊, bear 熊, salmon 鲑, Alaska 阿拉斯加, predator 捕食者, hibernate 冬眠
[记] 可数：a grizzly。grizzly 为 brown bear 北美亚种；形容词 grizzled 指「花白的」

**dingo**
英/美 /ˈdɪŋɡəʊ/
n. 澳洲野犬
[义] A wild dog of Australia, usually sandy-coloured. 澳大利亚野犬，通常沙色（dingo pack 澳洲野犬群）。可数。
[例] ① A dingo howled on the edge of the outback settlement. 澳洲野犬在内陆定居点边缘嚎叫。 ② Dingoes have lived in Australia for thousands of years. 澳洲野犬在澳已生存数千年。
[搭] dingo pack 澳洲野犬群, wild dog 野犬, Australian dingo 澳洲野犬
[联] dog 狗, Australia 澳大利亚, wolf 狼, pack 兽群, wild 野生的, howl 嚎叫
[记] 可数：a dingo。与家犬可杂交；「a dingo ate my baby」为澳著名案件梗

**ibex**
英/美 /ˈaɪbeks/
n. 岩羊
[义] A wild goat of mountains with long curved horns. 山地野山羊，角长而弯（Alpine ibex 阿尔卑斯岩羊，Nubian ibex 努比亚岩羊）。可数；复数 ibex 或 ibexes。
[例] ① Ibexes scaled near-vertical cliffs with ease. 岩羊轻松攀上近乎垂直的峭壁。 ② Male ibexes clash horns during the rut. 繁殖期雄岩羊角斗。
[搭] Alpine ibex 阿尔卑斯岩羊, Nubian ibex 努比亚岩羊, mountain goat 山羊
[联] goat 山羊, mountain 山地, horn 角, cliff 峭壁, wild 野生的, herd 兽群
[记] 可数：an ibex；复数 ibex / ibexes。高山攀岩能力极强

**cuttlefish**
英/美 /ˈkʌtlfɪʃ/
n. 乌贼；墨鱼
[义] A sea animal related to squid with eight arms and two tentacles, able to change colour. 与鱿鱼近缘的海生动物，八腕两触，能变色（cuttlefish ink 墨鱼汁，cuttlebone 海螵蛸）。可数。
[例] ① The cuttlefish flashed patterns as it glided over the reef. 乌贼掠过礁石时闪现斑纹。 ② Cuttlefish are among the most intelligent invertebrates. 乌贼是最聪慧的无脊椎动物之一。
[搭] cuttlefish ink 墨鱼汁, cuttlebone 海螵蛸, colour-changing 变色的
[联] squid 鱿鱼, octopus 章鱼, ocean 海洋, ink 墨汁, reef 礁, invertebrate 无脊椎动物
[记] 可数：a cuttlefish。cuttlebone 为宠物鸟补钙常见物

**sea urchin**
英/美 /ˌsiː ˈɜːtʃɪn/
n. 海胆
[义] A small round sea animal covered in spines that grazes on algae. 球形海生动物，满身棘刺，食藻类（purple sea urchin 紫海胆，sea urchin spine 海胆刺）。可数。
[例] ① Sea urchins grazed the rocky seabed bare in places. 海胆将部分岩床啃得光秃。 ② Divers watched for sea urchins among the kelp. 潜水者在海带丛中留意海胆。
[搭] purple sea urchin 紫海胆, sea urchin spine 海胆刺, graze on algae 食藻
[联] spine 刺, ocean 海洋, reef 礁, algae 藻类, invertebrate 无脊椎动物, diver 潜水者
[记] 可数：a sea urchin。部分种类可食，作料理归 4-01

**swordfish**
英/美 /ˈsɔːdfɪʃ/
n. 剑鱼
[义] A large fast fish with a long pointed upper jaw like a sword. 大型速游鱼，上颌延长如剑（swordfish bill 剑鱼吻，swordfish steak 剑鱼排）。可数。
[例] ① A swordfish leapt clear of the water, slashing its bill. 剑鱼跃出水面，挥动剑吻。 ② Swordfish are prized by sport fishermen for their fight. 剑鱼因搏斗激烈受运动渔民青睐。
[搭] swordfish bill 剑鱼吻, swordfish steak 剑鱼排（食材→4-01）, sport fishing 运动钓鱼
[联] fish 鱼, ocean 海洋, fast 快速的, bill 吻, predator 捕食者, tuna 金枪鱼
[记] 可数：a swordfish。剑吻用于击晕猎物；鱼肉作食材归 4-01

**cod**
英/美 /kɒd/
n. 鳕鱼
[义] A large edible sea fish living in cold waters. 冷水大型食用海鱼（Atlantic cod 大西洋鳕，cod fishery 鳕鱼渔业）。可数/不可数。
[例] ① Cod stocks collapsed in the North Atlantic in the 1990s. 九十年代北大西洋鳕鱼资源崩溃。 ② Fishermen hauled crates of cod onto the deck. 渔民把一箱箱鳕鱼拉上甲板。
[搭] Atlantic cod 大西洋鳕, cod fishery 鳕鱼渔业, fish and chips 炸鱼薯条（食材→4-01）
[联] fish 鱼, ocean 海洋, fishery 渔业, cold water 冷水, seafood 海鲜, overfishing 过度捕捞
[记] 可数/不可数。作鱼→本章；炸鱼薯条等菜肴→4-01

**sardine**
英/美 /ˌsɑːˈdiːn/
n. 沙丁鱼
[义] A small oily fish, often eaten canned. 小型多油海鱼，常罐头食用（sardine tin 沙丁鱼罐头，school of sardines 沙丁鱼群）。可数。
[例] ① Sardines shimmered in a tight ball to confuse predators. 沙丁鱼紧结成球迷惑捕食者。 ② She opened a tin of sardines for lunch. 她开一罐沙丁鱼当午餐。
[搭] sardine tin 沙丁鱼罐头, school of sardines 沙丁鱼群, canned sardines 罐装沙丁鱼（食材→4-01）
[联] fish 鱼, ocean 海洋, tin 罐头, school 鱼群, oily 多油的, whale 鲸
[记] 可数：a sardine。packed in like sardines 挤得像沙丁鱼（习语）

**angelfish**
英/美 /ˈeɪndʒlfɪʃ/
n. 神仙鱼；天使鱼
[义] A brightly coloured tropical fish, often kept in aquariums. 色彩鲜艳的热带鱼，常养于水族箱（marine angelfish 海水神仙鱼，freshwater angelfish 淡水神仙鱼）。可数。
[例] ① Angelfish drifted gracefully through the coral aquarium. 神仙鱼优雅游过珊瑚水族箱。 ② Reef angelfish feed on sponges and algae. 礁神仙鱼以海绵和藻类为食。
[搭] marine angelfish 海水神仙鱼, freshwater angelfish 淡水神仙鱼, tropical aquarium 热带水族箱
[联] fish 鱼, reef 礁, tropical 热带的, aquarium 水族箱, colourful 色彩鲜艳的, coral 珊瑚
[记] 可数：an angelfish。海水种与淡水种不同科，观赏鱼店常见

**partridge**
英/美 /ˈpɑːtrɪdʒ/
n. 鹧鸪
[义] A plump game bird related to quails and pheasants. 体态圆润的猎鸟，与鹌鹑、雉近缘（grey partridge 灰鹧鸪，red-legged partridge 红腿鹧鸪）。可数。
[例] ① A covey of partridges burst from the stubble field. 一群鹧鸪从茬田惊飞。 ② Partridges prefer open farmland with hedgerows. 鹧鸪喜开阔农田与树篱。
[搭] grey partridge 灰鹧鸪, red-legged partridge 红腿鹧鸪, game bird 猎鸟
[联] quail 鹌鹑, pheasant 雉, bird 鸟, game 猎物, farmland 农田, covey 鸟群
[记] 可数：a partridge。圣诞颂歌 The Twelve Days of Christmas 有名

**hoopoe**
英/美 /ˈhuːpuː/
n. 戴胜
[义] A bird with a crest of feathers and a long down-curved bill. 头顶羽冠、喙长下弯的鸟（Eurasian hoopoe 欧亚戴胜，hoopoe crest 戴胜冠羽）。可数。
[例] ① A hoopoe fanned its orange crest on the lawn. 戴胜在草坪上展开橙色冠羽。 ② Hoopoes probe the soil for insects with their bills. 戴胜以长喙在土中探虫。
[搭] Eurasian hoopoe 欧亚戴胜, hoopoe crest 戴胜冠羽, crested bird 有冠鸟
[联] bird 鸟, crest 羽冠, bill 喙, insect 昆虫, lawn 草坪, colourful 色彩鲜艳的
[记] 可数：a hoopoe。冠羽可开合；欧亚非广泛分布

**buzzard**
英/美 /ˈbʌzəd/
n. 鵟；秃鹫
[义] A large bird of prey that soars on thermals; in Britain, the common buzzard. 借热气流翱翔的大型猛禽；英式常指普通鵟（common buzzard 普通鵟，soaring buzzard 翱翔的鵟）。可数。
[例] ① A buzzard circled high above the moorland. 鵟在高沼地上空盘旋。 ② Buzzards feed on small mammals and carrion. 鵟食小型哺乳动物与腐肉。
[搭] common buzzard 普通鵟, soaring buzzard 翱翔的鵟, bird of prey 猛禽
[联] hawk 鹰, eagle 雕, vulture 秃鹫, prey 猎物, soar 翱翔, carrion 腐肉
[记] 可数：a buzzard。美式 buzzard 有时指 vulture；英式多指鵟

**gannet**
英/美 /ˈɡænɪt/
n. 鲣鸟
[义] A large seabird that dives steeply into the sea to catch fish. 大型海鸟，陡直俯冲入海捕鱼（northern gannet 北方鲣鸟，gannet colony 鲣鸟繁殖群）。可数。
[例] ① Gannets plunged into the sea like white arrows. 鲣鸟如白箭俯冲入海。 ② A gannet colony covered the cliff in thousands of nests. 鲣鸟繁殖群在崖上布满数千巢穴。
[搭] northern gannet 北方鲣鸟, gannet colony 鲣鸟繁殖群, diving seabird 俯冲海鸟
[联] seabird 海鸟, dive 俯冲, fish 鱼, cliff 峭壁, colony 群落, ocean 海洋
[记] 可数：a gannet。英式俚语 gannet 指「贪吃的人」，不同义

**bald eagle**
英/美 /ˌbɔːld ˈiːɡl/
n. 白头海雕
[义] A large eagle of North America with a white head and tail. 北美大型雕，头尾白色（bald eagle nest 白头海雕巢，national bird 国鸟）。可数。
[例] ① A bald eagle perched above the river, scanning for fish. 白头海雕栖于河上瞭望鱼群。 ② Bald eagles have recovered since the ban on DDT. 禁用 DDT 后白头海雕数量已恢复。
[搭] bald eagle nest 白头海雕巢, national bird 国鸟, fish eagle 鱼雕
[联] eagle 雕, America 美洲, nest 巢, fish 鱼, symbol 象征, predator 捕食者
[记] 可数：a bald eagle。美国国鸟；bald 指「白头」非「秃顶」

**kestrel**
英/美 /ˈkestrəl/
n. 红隼
[义] A small falcon that often hovers while hunting. 小型隼，捕食时常悬停（common kestrel 普通红隼，hovering kestrel 悬停红隼）。可数。
[例] ① A kestrel hung in the wind above the motorway verge. 红隼在高速公路旁迎风悬停。 ② Kestrels spot mice from their hovering perch. 红隼悬停时发现老鼠。
[搭] common kestrel 普通红隼, hovering kestrel 悬停红隼, small falcon 小型隼
[联] falcon 隼, hawk 鹰, hover 悬停, bird 鸟, prey 猎物, motorway 公路
[记] 可数：a kestrel。悬停狩猎是辨识要点；欧亚郊野极常见

**swift**
英/美 /swɪft/
n. 雨燕
adj. 迅速的
[义] A fast-flying bird with long narrow wings that spends most of its life in the air. 翼狭长、飞行极快、一生多在空中度过的鸟（common swift 普通雨燕，swift migration 雨燕迁徙）。可数。
[例] ① Swifts screamed as they raced above the rooftops. 雨燕在屋顶上空疾飞尖叫。 ② Young swifts leave the nest and fly to Africa without landing. 幼雨燕离巢直飞非洲，途中不落地。
[搭] common swift 普通雨燕, swift migration 雨燕迁徙, fast-flying bird 疾飞鸟
[联] swallow 燕子, bird 鸟, fly 飞, wing 翅膀, migrate 迁徙, fast 快速的
[记] 可数：a swift。与 swallow 外形近但翼更长、脚极短；形容词 swift 义不同

**nightjar**
英/美 /ˈnaɪtdʒɑː(r)/
n. 夜鹰
[义] A nocturnal bird with a wide mouth that catches insects in flight. 夜行鸟，口阔，飞行中捕虫（European nightjar 欧夜鹰，churring call 夜鹰颤鸣）。可数。
[例] ① A nightjar churred from the heath at dusk. 黄昏欧石南丛传来夜鹰颤鸣。 ② Nightjars are hard to spot by day as they rest on the ground. 夜鹰白昼伏地，极难发现。
[搭] European nightjar 欧夜鹰, churring call 夜鹰颤鸣, nocturnal bird 夜行鸟
[联] bird 鸟, nocturnal 夜行的, insect 昆虫, dusk 黄昏, moth 蛾, camouflage 伪装
[记] 可数：a nightjar。与 owl 同为夜行鸟，但口阔、善伪装

**sandpiper**
英/美 /ˈsændpaɪpə(r)/
n. 鹬
[义] A small wading bird that runs along shores probing mud for food. 小型涉禽，沿水滨奔跑在泥中探食（common sandpiper 矶鹬，migratory sandpiper 迁徙鹬）。可数。
[例] ① Sandpipers scurried ahead of the incoming tide. 鹬在涨潮前沿岸疾走。 ② A flock of sandpipers wheeled over the mudflat. 鹬群在滩涂上空盘旋。
[搭] common sandpiper 矶鹬, migratory sandpiper 迁徙鹬, wading bird 涉禽
[联] bird 鸟, shore 海岸, wetland 湿地, migrate 迁徙, mudflat 滩涂, wading 涉水的
[记] 可数：a sandpiper。滨鸟观鸟入门常见类群

**grouse**
英/美 /ɡraʊs/
n. 松鸡
v. 抱怨
[义] A plump game bird of heaths and mountains. 石南地与山地圆润猎鸟（red grouse 红松鸡，grouse moor 松鸡猎场）。可数。
[例] ① Red grouse burst from the heather with a cackle. 红松鸡咯咯叫着从石楠丛中飞起。 ② Grouse populations depend on healthy moorland habitat. 松鸡种群依赖健康的沼地栖息地。
[搭] red grouse 红松鸡, grouse moor 松鸡猎场, game bird 猎鸟
[联] bird 鸟, heath 石南地, game 猎物, moorland 沼地, hunt 狩猎, pheasant 雉
[记] 可数：a grouse。动词 grouse 指「抱怨」〔口语〕，同形异义

**caracal**
英/美 /ˈkærəkæl/
n. 狞猫；非洲猞猁
[义] A wild cat of Africa and Asia with long black ear tufts. 非亚野猫，耳尖有黑色长簇毛（caracal leap 狞猫跃，ear tufts 耳簇）。可数。
[例] ① The caracal sprang two metres into the air to snatch a bird. 狞猫跃起两米擒鸟。 ② Caracals were once trained to hunt in Persia. 波斯曾训狞猫狩猎。
[搭] caracal leap 狞猫跃, ear tufts 耳簇, wild cat 野猫
[联] cat 猫, lynx 猞猁, serval 薮猫, predator 捕食者, Africa 非洲, leap 跳跃
[记] 可数：a caracal。耳簇显著；跳跃捕鸟能力极强

**golden eagle**
英/美 /ˌɡəʊldən ˈiːɡl/
n. 金雕
[义] A large eagle of the Northern Hemisphere with golden-brown feathers on the head and neck. 北半球大型雕，头颈羽金褐（golden eagle nest 金雕巢，mountain eagle 山地雕）。可数。
[例] ① A golden eagle soared over the Scottish highlands. 金雕翱翔苏格兰高地。 ② Golden eagles prey on hares and young deer. 金雕捕食野兔与幼鹿。
[搭] golden eagle nest 金雕巢, mountain eagle 山地雕, bird of prey 猛禽
[联] eagle 雕, hawk 鹰, predator 捕食者, mountain 山地, soar 翱翔, nest 巢
[记] 可数：a golden eagle。欧亚北美高山常见；与 bald eagle 分布与羽色不同

**harrier**
英/美 /ˈhæriə(r)/
n. 鹞；骚扰者
[义] A hawk that flies low over open ground hunting small animals. 低空掠过开阔地猎食的小型猛禽（hen harrier 白尾鹞，marsh harrier 白头鹞）。可数。
[例] ① A hen harrier quartered the moor in slow, low flight. 白尾鹞低速低空搜索沼地。 ② Marsh harriers nest in reed beds beside lakes. 白头鹞在湖边芦苇丛筑巢。
[搭] hen harrier 白尾鹞, marsh harrier 白头鹞, low-flying hawk 低空鹰
[联] hawk 鹰, buzzard 鵟, moorland 沼地, wetland 湿地, prey 猎物, soar 翱翔
[记] 可数：a harrier。harrier 亦指「骚扰者」或鹞式战机，同形异义

**skylark**
英/美 /ˈskaɪlɑːk/
n. 云雀
v. 嬉闹
[义] A small brown bird that sings while flying high in the sky. 小型褐鸟，高飞空中鸣唱（Eurasian skylark 欧亚云雀，skylark song 云雀之歌）。可数。
[例] ① A skylark poured out its song hundreds of metres above the field. 云雀在田野上空数百米处放声歌唱。 ② Skylark numbers have fallen on modern farmland. 现代农田云雀数量已下降。
[搭] Eurasian skylark 欧亚云雀, skylark song 云雀之歌, singing flight 鸣唱飞行
[联] bird 鸟, songbird 鸣禽, field 田野, sing 鸣唱, fly 飞, farmland 农田
[记] 可数：a skylark。动词 skylark 指「嬉闹」〔旧〕；华兹华斯诗《致云雀》有名

**lapwing**
英/美 /ˈlæpwɪŋ/
n. 麦鸡；凤头麦鸡
[义] A black-and-white wading bird with a crest and a wavering flight. 黑白相间、有冠、飞行摇摆的涉禽（northern lapwing 凤头麦鸡，lapwing flock 麦鸡群）。可数。
[例] ① Lapwings tumbled through the air above the wet meadow. 麦鸡在湿草地上空翻飞。 ② The lapwing's crest stood up as it called from the field. 麦鸡在田间鸣叫时冠羽竖起。
[搭] northern lapwing 凤头麦鸡, lapwing flock 麦鸡群, crested plover 有冠鸻
[联] bird 鸟, plover 鸻, wetland 湿地, flock 鸟群, crest 羽冠, farmland 农田
[记] 可数：a lapwing。亦作 peewit〔英〕；保护农田湿地鸟类指标种

**herring**
英/美 /ˈherɪŋ/
n. 鲱鱼
[义] A small oily sea fish that swims in large schools. 多油小型海鱼，大群洄游（Atlantic herring 大西洋鲱，herring gull 银鸥〔食鲱〕）。可数/不可数。
[例] ① Herring shoals moved north as the water warmed. 水温升高时鲱鱼群北移。 ② Pickled herring is a traditional Scandinavian dish. 腌鲱鱼是斯堪的纳维亚传统菜。
[搭] Atlantic herring 大西洋鲱, herring shoal 鲱鱼群, pickled herring 腌鲱鱼（食材→4-01）
[联] fish 鱼, sardine 沙丁鱼, ocean 海洋, school 鱼群, oily 多油的, gull 海鸥
[记] 可数/不可数。作鱼→本章；腌鲱等菜肴→4-01

**mackerel**
英/美 /ˈmækərəl/
n. 鲭鱼
[义] A striped sea fish with oily flesh, often caught for food. 体有条纹、肉质多油的海鱼，常作食用（Atlantic mackerel 大西洋鲭，mackerel sky 鱼鳞云〔天象〕）。可数/不可数。
[例] ① Mackerel shimmered in the net as the boat hauled it aboard. 起网时鲭鱼在网中闪光。 ② Fresh mackerel should be cooked on the day it is caught. 鲜鲭宜当日烹食。
[搭] Atlantic mackerel 大西洋鲭, mackerel sky 鱼鳞云, grilled mackerel 烤鲭鱼（食材→4-01）
[联] fish 鱼, ocean 海洋, oily 多油的, stripe 条纹, tuna 金枪鱼, seafood 海鲜
[记] 可数/不可数。作鱼→本章；烤鱼等→4-01。mackerel sky 指高积云似鱼鳞

**prawn**
英/美 /prɔːn/
n. 对虾；明虾
[义] A large edible shrimp-like shellfish. 似虾的大型食用甲壳类（king prawn 大虾，prawn cocktail 鲜虾杯）。可数。
[例] ① Prawns scuttled across the tropical rock pool. 对虾在热带潮池横爬。 ② She ordered grilled prawns at the seaside restaurant. 她在海边餐厅点了烤虾。
[搭] king prawn 大虾, prawn cocktail 鲜虾杯（食材→4-01）, freshwater prawn 淡水对虾
[联] shrimp 虾, shellfish 贝类, ocean 海洋, seafood 海鲜, grill 烤, cocktail 杯装冷盘
[记] 可数：a prawn。英式 prawn 常较大；美式 shrimp 与 prawn 用法有地域差异

**sea cucumber**
英/美 /ˌsiː ˈkjuːkʌmbə(r)/
n. 海参
[义] A sausage-shaped sea animal that lives on the seabed and feeds on sediment. 肠状海生动物，栖于海底食沉积物（dried sea cucumber 干海参，sea cucumber fishery 海参渔业）。可数。
[例] ① Sea cucumbers slowly crawled over the sandy seabed. 海参在沙质海床上缓慢爬行。 ② Overharvesting has depleted sea cucumber stocks in some reefs. 部分礁区过度采捕使海参资源枯竭。
[搭] dried sea cucumber 干海参, sea cucumber fishery 海参渔业, seabed animal 海底动物
[联] ocean 海洋, reef 礁, invertebrate 无脊椎动物, seabed 海床, fishery 渔业, Asia 亚洲
[记] 可数：a sea cucumber。干海参为亚州名贵食材→4-01；受时收缩

**scallop**
英/美 /ˈskɒləp/
n. 扇贝
[义] A shellfish with a fan-shaped shell that swims by clapping its valves. 扇形贝壳的贝类，开合壳瓣游泳（king scallop 国王扇贝，scallop shell 扇贝壳）。可数。
[例] ① Scallops darted across the seabed by snapping their shells shut. 扇贝猛合双壳在海底窜行。 ② Divers collected scallops from the cold Scottish waters. 潜水者在苏格兰冷水域采集扇贝。
[搭] king scallop 国王扇贝, scallop shell 扇贝壳, pan-seared scallops 香煎扇贝（食材→4-01）
[联] shellfish 贝类, shell 壳, ocean 海洋, diver 潜水者, seafood 海鲜, swim 游泳
[记] 可数：a scallop。扇贝肉作料理归 4-01；扇贝壳常作装饰

**moray**
英/美 /məˈreɪ/
n. 海鳝；海鳗
[义] A long snake-like fish with sharp teeth, often hiding in reef crevices. 形似蛇、齿尖利的长鱼，常隐于礁缝（moray eel 海鳝，giant moray 巨型海鳝）。可数。
[例] ① A moray eel gaped from a coral crevice. 海鳝在珊瑚缝中张口露齿。 ② Morays hunt at night along tropical reefs. 海鳝夜间沿热带礁石狩猎。
[搭] moray eel 海鳝, giant moray 巨型海鳝, reef crevice 礁缝
[联] eel 鳗, fish 鱼, reef 礁, teeth 牙齿, predator 捕食者, tropical 热带的
[记] 可数：a moray。常与 cleaner wrasse 共生；潜水常见

**anchovy**
英/美 /ˈæntʃəvi/
n. 凤尾鱼；鳀鱼
[义] A small oily fish of warm seas, often preserved in oil or used as paste. 暖海小型多油鱼，常油浸或制酱（anchovy paste 鳀鱼酱，anchovy pizza 鳀鱼披萨）。可数。
[例] ① Anchovies swim in dense schools near the surface. 鳀鱼在近水面密集成群。 ② She added anchovy fillets to the pasta sauce. 她在意面酱里加入鳀鱼片。
[搭] anchovy paste 鳀鱼酱, anchovy fillet 鳀鱼片（食材→4-01）, oily fish 多油鱼
[联] fish 鱼, sardine 沙丁鱼, ocean 海洋, oily 多油的, pizza 披萨, school 鱼群
[记] 可数：an anchovy。食材义归 4-01；地中海料理常见

**haddock**
英/美 /ˈhædək/
n. 黑线鳕
[义] A North Atlantic food fish related to cod, with a dark line along the side. 北大西洋食用鱼，与鳕近缘，体侧有暗线（smoked haddock 熏黑线鳕，fish and chips 炸鱼薯条）。可数/不可数。
[例] ① Haddock are caught in the same waters as cod. 黑线鳕与鳕鱼在同水域捕捞。 ② Smoked haddock gives the soup a rich flavour. 熏黑线鳕使汤味浓郁。
[搭] smoked haddock 熏黑线鳕, North Atlantic fish 北大西洋鱼, fish and chips 炸鱼薯条（食材→4-01）
[联] cod 鳕鱼, fish 鱼, ocean 海洋, smoke 熏, seafood 海鲜, Atlantic 大西洋
[记] 可数/不可数。作鱼→本章；熏鱼、炸鱼薯条→4-01

**humpback whale**
英/美 /ˌhʌmpbæk ˈweɪl/
n. 座头鲸
[义] A large whale with long flippers and a habit of leaping from the water. 大型鲸，鳍肢长，常跃出水面（humpback song 座头鲸歌，breaching humpback 跃水座头鲸）。可数。
[例] ① A humpback whale breached, crashing back into the sea. 座头鲸跃出水面，轰然落回海中。 ② Humpbacks sing complex songs during the breeding season. 繁殖季座头鲸唱复杂歌声。
[搭] humpback song 座头鲸歌, breaching humpback 跃水座头鲸, whale migration 鲸迁徙
[联] whale 鲸, ocean 海洋, migrate 迁徙, sing 鸣唱, breach 跃水, mammal 哺乳动物
[记] 可数：a humpback whale。观鲸旅游明星物种；歌谱年年变化

**oystercatcher**
英/美 /ˈɔɪstəkætʃə(r)/
n. 蛎鹬
[义] A black-and-white shore bird with a long orange bill for opening shellfish. 黑白滨鸟，喙长橙色，用于撬开贝类（Eurasian oystercatcher 欧亚蛎鹬，oystercatcher bill 蛎鹬喙）。可数。
[例] ① Oystercatchers hammered mussels open on the rocks. 蛎鹬在岩石上敲开贻贝。 ② An oystercatcher piped loudly as we crossed the beach. 我们穿过海滩时蛎鹬高声鸣叫。
[搭] Eurasian oystercatcher 欧亚蛎鹬, oystercatcher bill 蛎鹬喙, shore bird 滨鸟
[联] bird 鸟, shore 海岸, oyster 牡蛎, mussel 贻贝, bill 喙, beach 海滩
[记] 可数：an oystercatcher。喙强韧，专食贝类；海岸常见

**curlew**
英/美 /ˈkɜːljuː/
n. 杓鹬
[义] A large wading bird with a long down-curved bill. 大型涉禽，喙长下弯（Eurasian curlew 白腰杓鹬，curlew call 杓鹬鸣）。可数。
[例] ① A curlew's bubbling call drifted over the estuary at dusk. 黄昏河口飘来杓鹬汩汩鸣声。 ② Curlews probe deep mud for worms with their long bills. 杓鹬以长喙探深泥觅食蠕虫。
[搭] Eurasian curlew 白腰杓鹬, curlew call 杓鹬鸣, long-billed wader 长喙涉禽
[联] bird 鸟, wetland 湿地, bill 喙, wading bird 涉禽, estuary 河口, worm 蠕虫
[记] 可数：a curlew。鸣声独特；潮间带与河口标志性鸟类

**plover**
英/美 /ˈplʌvə(r)/
n. 鸻
[义] A small wading bird of shores and grasslands, often with a banded breast. 滨鸟与草地小型涉禽，胸常具环纹（ringed plover 环颈鸻，golden plover 金斑鸻）。可数。
[例] ① Ringed plovers pretended to have a broken wing to lure us away. 环颈鸻佯装断翼引开我们。 ② Plovers ran in short stops and starts along the tideline. 鸻在潮水线边走走停停。
[搭] ringed plover 环颈鸻, golden plover 金斑鸻, shore plover 滨鸻
[联] lapwing 麦鸡, bird 鸟, shore 海岸, wetland 湿地, nest 巢, wading 涉水的
[记] 可数：a plover。佯伤护雏是著名行为；与 lapwing 近缘

**snipe**
英/美 /snaɪp/
n. 沙锥；鹬
v. 狙击；抨击
[义] A medium-sized wading bird with a very long bill, found in marshes. 中型涉禽，喙极长，栖于沼泽（common snipe 扇尾沙锥，snipe marsh 沙锥沼泽）。可数。
[例] ① A snipe rose from the reed bed with a zigzag flight. 沙锥从芦苇丛 zigzag 飞起。 ② Snipes probe soft mud for insects with their sensitive bills. 沙锥以敏感长喙探软泥食虫。
[搭] common snipe 扇尾沙锥, snipe marsh 沙锥沼泽, zigzag flight 之字飞行
[联] bird 鸟, wetland 湿地, marsh 沼泽, bill 喙, wading bird 涉禽, sandpiper 鹬
[记] 可数：a snipe。动词 snipe 指「狙击、抨击」；snipe hunt 为恶作剧

**guinea fowl**
英/美 /ˈɡɪni faʊl/
n. 珍珠鸡
[义] A grey African bird with white spots, often kept for its meat. 非洲灰鸟，白点斑，常饲养取肉（helmeted guinea fowl 头盔珍珠鸡，guinea fowl flock 珍珠鸡群）。可数。
[例] ① Guinea fowl scratched noisily through the farmyard. 珍珠鸡在农家院大声刨地。 ② The farmer kept guinea fowl to control ticks in the fields. 农夫养珍珠鸡以控田间蜱虫。
[搭] helmeted guinea fowl 头盔珍珠鸡, guinea fowl flock 珍珠鸡群, farmyard bird 农家鸟
[联] bird 鸟, chicken 鸡, Africa 非洲, farm 农场, flock 鸟群, poultry 家禽
[记] 可数：a guinea fowl。肉用家禽→亦涉 4-01；叫声刺耳

**martin**
英/美 /ˈmɑːtɪn/
n. 崖沙燕；毛脚燕
[义] A swallow-like bird that nests in colonies on cliffs or buildings. 似燕鸟类，群栖崖壁或建筑物（house martin 毛脚燕，sand martin 崖沙燕）。可数。
[例] ① House martins built mud nests under the eaves. 毛脚燕在檐下筑泥巢。 ② Martins wheeled over the river catching insects. 燕群在河上盘旋捕虫。
[搭] house martin 毛脚燕, sand martin 崖沙燕, colonial nester 群栖筑巢鸟
[联] swallow 燕子, bird 鸟, nest 巢, fly 飞, insect 昆虫, colony 群落
[记] 可数：a martin。与 swallow 近缘；martin 亦作人名

**peregrine**
英/美 /ˈperɪɡriːn/
n. 游隼
adj. 外来的；漫游的
[义] A powerful falcon that hunts other birds in high-speed dives. 强健隼类，高速俯冲猎禽（peregrine falcon 游隼，fastest animal 最快动物〔俯冲〕）。可数。
[例] ① A peregrine stooped on a pigeon at breathtaking speed. 游隼以惊人速度俯冲扑鸽。 ② Peregrines have adapted to nest on city skyscrapers. 游隼已适应在城市摩天楼筑巢。
[搭] peregrine falcon 游隼, high-speed dive 高速俯冲, urban nest 城市巢
[联] falcon 隼, hawk 鹰, predator 捕食者, dive 俯冲, fast 快速的, bird 鸟
[记] 可数：a peregrine。俯冲时速可达三百多公里；城市适应成功

**ptarmigan**
英/美 /ˈtɑːmɪɡən/
n. 雷鸟
[义] A grouse of Arctic and mountain regions that turns white in winter. 北极与山地松鸡，冬羽转白（rock ptarmigan 岩雷鸟，white winter plumage 白色冬羽）。可数。
[例] ① Ptarmigans were almost invisible against the snow. 雷鸟在雪中几乎隐形。 ② Rock ptarmigans feed on buds and berries above the treeline. 岩雷鸟在林线以上食芽果。
[搭] rock ptarmigan 岩雷鸟, white winter plumage 白色冬羽, mountain grouse 山地松鸡
[联] grouse 松鸡, bird 鸟, Arctic 北极, snow 雪, mountain 山地, camouflage 伪装
[记] 可数：a ptarmigan。冬夏羽色剧变；高山与极地常见

**wagtail**
英/美 /ˈwæɡteɪl/
n. 鹡鸰
[义] A small bird that constantly wags its long tail, often near water. 体小、不断摆动长尾的鸟，常近水（pied wagtail 白鹡鸰，yellow wagtail 黄鹡鸰）。可数。
[例] ① A wagtail bobbed along the riverbank chasing insects. 鹡鸰沿河岸点头追逐昆虫。 ② Pied wagtails often nest in old buildings in towns. 白鹡鸰常在城镇旧建筑筑巢。
[搭] pied wagtail 白鹡鸰, yellow wagtail 黄鹡鸰, tail-wagging bird 摆尾鸟
[联] bird 鸟, tail 尾巴, river 河, insect 昆虫, urban 城市的, bob 点头
[记] 可数：a wagtail。摆尾为辨识特征；欧亚水域常见

**wallaby**
英/美 /ˈwɒləbi/
n. 小袋鼠；沙袋鼠
[义] A small or medium-sized marsupial related to the kangaroo. 与袋鼠近缘的小型或中型有袋动物（red-necked wallaby 红颈沙袋鼠，wallaby joey 幼沙袋鼠）。可数。
[例] ① A wallaby grazed quietly at the edge of the campground. 沙袋鼠在营地边缘安静食草。 ② Wallabies are more compact than full-sized kangaroos. 沙袋鼠比全尺寸袋鼠更紧凑。
[搭] red-necked wallaby 红颈沙袋鼠, wallaby joey 幼沙袋鼠, marsupial 有袋动物
[联] kangaroo 袋鼠, marsupial 有袋动物, Australia 澳大利亚, joey 幼崽, graze 食草, pouch 育儿袋
[记] 可数：a wallaby。与 kangaroo 无严格单线分界，口语常混用

**cassowary**
英/美 /ˈkæsəweri/
n. 鹤鸵
[义] A large flightless bird of New Guinea and Australia with a helmet-like casque and dangerous claws. 新几内亚与澳大型不能飞鸟，头有盔状冠、爪危险（southern cassowary 南方鹤鸵，cassowary casque 鹤鸵盔冠）。可数。
[例] ① The cassowary vanished into the rainforest undergrowth. 鹤鸵没入雨林下层。 ② Cassowaries can injure people with their powerful kicks. 鹤鸵猛踢可伤人。
[搭] southern cassowary 南方鹤鸵, cassowary casque 鹤鸵盔冠, flightless bird 不能飞鸟
[联] emu 鸸鹋, ostrich 鸵鸟, bird 鸟, rainforest 雨林, flightless 不能飞的, dangerous 危险的
[记] 可数：a cassowary。世界最危险鸟类之一；雨林种子传播者
